**Перевод:**

**28. O mighty-armed! The wise person, distinguishing material** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma*** **from the** ***ātmā*****, does not associate with them. He thinks, “I am different, bound in** ***prakṛti*** **by some event, I am performing actions by** ***karma*** **and** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****.”**

**28. O mighty-armed! The wise person, knowing the nature of** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma,*** **he, in all actions, thinks “I am** ***ātmā*****, a** ***svarūpa*** **of consciousness and bliss. I have accepted material actions, being bound by material** ***ahaṅkāra*** **due to mistaking my** ***svarūpa*****. I am actually a pure** ***ātmā*** **and do not perform these actions. But by the** ***upādhis*** **of the senses and material** ***ahaṅkāra*** **I perform material actions. I am not the sole doer.” Thus, he is not attached to those actions. The three doers in all material actions are — the** ***jīva*** **who thinks he is the body, the** ***prakṛti*** **and the Paramātmā, who is the regulator of all beings.**

### Text 29

*prakṛter guṇa-saṁmūḍhāḥ sajjante guṇa-karmasu \|*  
*tān akṛtsna-vido mandān kṛtsna-vin na vicālayet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. Bewildered persons, not understanding this, think themselves to be** ***prakṛti*** **and become related to** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma*** **of** ***prakṛti*****. The knower of truth does not uselessly disturb these foolish persons having little knowledge.**

**After making them qualified \[gradually by Vedic** ***karma-yoga*****\], the knowers of truth will then give them knowledge of the truth, situated with higher qualification.**

### Text 30

*mayi sarvāṇi karmāṇi sannyasyādhyātma-cetasā \|*  
*nirāśīr nirmamo bhūtvā yudhyasva vigata-jvaraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. O Arjuna! Endowed with knowledge of the truth, being spiritually minded, offer to me all your activities (*****karmas*****) while giving up desire for results and material** ***ahaṅkāra*****. While giving up {worry and doubt}/\[distress\], take support of fighting which is your** ***sva-dharma*****.**

### Text 31

*ye me matam idaṁ nityam anutiṣṭhanti mānavāḥ \|*  
*śraddhāvanto ’nasūyanto mucyante te ’pi karmabhiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. Those who always perform** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to the Lord and have faith in me without envy, attain liberation from the bondage of karma.**

**31. Those who always perform** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to the Lord attain liberation from the bondage of karma. And those who are incapable of performing this, and, who are without envy and have faith, they also attain the result.**

### Text 32

*ye tv etad abhyasūyanto nānutiṣṭhanti me matam \|*  
*sarva-jñāna-vimūḍhāṁs tān viddhi naṣṭān acetasaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. But know that those who do not follow this teaching {of mine}, showing envy, are cheated of all knowledge, are destroyed and are ignorant.**

### Text 33

*sadṛśaṁ ceṣṭate svasyāḥ prakṛter jñānavān api \|*  
*prakṛtiṁ yānti bhūtāni nigrahaḥ kiṁ kariṣyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. One should not think that a learned man, while distinguishing** ***ātmā*** **form non-*****ātmā*****, by suddenly giving up material** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma,*** **by taking shelter of** ***sannyāsa-dharma,*** **will be auspicious.**

**Though he has knowledge, the bound** ***jīva*** **will act according to his nature accrued over a long period of time. It is not that suddenly controlling it, one can give up one’s nature. All the bound** ***jīvas*** **will easily take support of their nature, in the form of habits practiced for a long time.**

**The method of giving up this nature is this: while remaining in that nature, perform all** ***karmas*** **{carefully} according to one’s nature. Until detachment combined with** ***bhakti-yoga*** **is fixed in the heart, the best path is** ***karma-yoga*** **offered to the Lord without desires since simultaneously both results—maintaining one’s** ***sva-dharma*** **and purifying one’s** ***sva-dharma*** **take place. If one gives up** ***sva-dharma*****, the final result is disturbance on the path.**

**When** ***bhakti-yoga*** **becomes fixed by mercy of devotees and by my mercy, having attained a method superior to** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to me, there is no advantage in the rules of maintaining** ***sva-dharma*****. But other than this,** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to me is the best.**

### Text 34

*indriyasyendriyasyārthe rāga-dveṣau vyavasthitau \|*  
*tayor na vaśam āgacchet tau hy asya paripanthinau \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. If you say that, by accepting sense objects, it is possible that one will become more bound to sense objects, and cannot be liberated from karma, then please listen.**

**It is not that all sense objects are obstacles to the** ***jīva*****. Attachment and repulsion to sense objects is the greatest enemy of the** ***jīva*****. When one accepts sense objects, one must control attachment and repulsion. Then, even though one accepts all sense objects, one will not be bound by them.**

**As long as the material body exists, one must accept sense objects. But in actions, the attraction and repulsion arising from thinking the body to be the self will gradually decrease and you will attain detachment from the sense objects.**

**I do not give instructions to tame the attachment and repulsion related to the Lord – attraction to objects or actions that stimulate** ***bhakti*** **and repulsion to things or actions which obstruct** ***bhakti*****. But understand that I only give instructions to control attachment and repulsion related to comfort of oneself.**

### Text 35

*śreyān sva-dharmo viguṇaḥ para-dharmāt sv-anuṣṭhitāt \|*  
*sva-dharme nidhanaṁ śreyaḥ para-dharmo bhayāvahaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. Thus, considering** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to me, it is better for the bound** ***jīva*** **to perform** ***sva-dharma*** **even if faulty. Performing other’s** ***dharma*** **(*****para-dharma*****), though done excellently, is not proper.**

**While observing** ***sva-dharma*****, if one dies before attaining a higher** ***dharma*****, this also produces auspiciousness since performing others’** ***dharma*** **is not without fear in all cases. But if one develops** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **there is objection to giving up** ***sva-dharma*** **since then the** ***jīva’s*** **eternal** ***dharma*** **manifests as one’s** ***sva-dharma*** **and conditional** ***sva-dharma*** **becomes others’** ***dharma*****.**

### Text 36

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*atha kena prayukto ’yaṁ pāpaṁ carati pūruṣaḥ \|*  
*anicchann api vārṣṇeya balād iva niyojitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36. Hearing this, Arjuna said: O descendent of the Vṛṣṇis, by what does man engage in sin, though unwilling?**

**You said that the** ***jīva*** **is an eternal, pure particle of consciousness, different from all material** ***guṇas*** **and relationships. It is thus not the** ***jīva’s*** **nature to commit sin in the material world. But it is seen that the** ***jīvas*** **always commit sins. Thus please tell me clearly, what engages the** ***jīva*** **in sin?**

### Text 37

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*kāma eṣa krodha eṣa rajo-guṇa-samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*mahāśano mahā-pāpmā viddhy enam iha vairiṇam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**37. Hearing the question, the Lord said: O Arjuna! It is lust arising from** ***rajoguṇa*****, which engages man in sin. {The** ***svarūpa*** **of} lust or** ***kāma*** **is desire for sense objects\[, caused by ancient impressions\].** ***Kāma,*** **taking another form, becomes anger.** ***Kāma,*** **taking shelter of** ***rajoguṇa,*** **arises and when there is an obstacle to attaining a desire, it takes shelter of** ***tamoguṇa*** **and becomes anger.** ***Kāma*** **is most ferocious and devours everyone. In** ***jñāna-yoga,*** **know that** ***kāma*** **is the chief enemy of the** ***jīva*****.**

### Text 38

*dhūmenāvriyate vahnir yathā-darśo malena ca \|*  
*yatholbenāvṛto garbhas tathā tenedam āvṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. This** ***kāma*** **sometimes covers the world in a restricted form and sometimes in profound form and sometimes in a most profound form. I will give examples. Please listen.**

**Like fire covered by smoke, the conscious** ***jīva*****, covered in a limited way by** ***kāma,*** **can perform actions like remembrance of the Lord. In this position, the** ***jīva*** **takes shelter of** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****, which has the form of budding consciousness.**

**Like a mirror covered by dirt, the conscious** ***jīva,*** **covered densely by** ***kāma,*** **cannot remember the supreme** ***īśvara*****, even when situated as a human being. The** ***jīva,*** **in this position with restricted consciousness, is very atheistic and moral. Such persons are like birds and animals.**

**Like the embryo covered by the womb, the conscious** ***jīva*** **covered most thickly by** ***kāma*** **becomes a tree or other lower forms of life.**

### Text 39

*āvṛtaṁ jñānam etena jñānino nitya-vairiṇā \|*  
*kāma-rūpeṇa kaunteya duṣpūreṇānalena ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. This** ***kāma*** **is the** ***jīva’s*** **ignorance. It is his eternal enemy \[like an irrepressible fire\]. It covers the conscious** ***jīva*** **{like unsatisfied fire}.**

**Just as I, the Lord, am a spiritual entity; the** ***jīva*** **is likewise a spiritual entity. The essential difference between us is that I am complete with all** ***śaktis*****. The** ***jīva*** **is a particle of consciousness and is capable {of action} by** ***śakti*** **given by me. The** ***jīva’s*** **eternal** ***dharma*** **is to be my eternal servant. That is called** ***prema*** **or** ***niṣkāma jaiva-dharma*****. A conscious entity alone is by its nature independent. The pure** ***jīva,*** **by nature, is independent. Thus by its will, it is my eternal servant.**

***Kāma*** **and ignorance are reversals of this pure independence. All** ***jīvas*** **who do not accept to be my servant by their will, accept this** ***kāma,*** **which is far removed from the pure** ***tattva*** **of the** ***jīva*****. By that, gradually the** ***jīva*** **becomes covered and his covered consciousness becomes material-like. This is called the** ***jīva’s*** **“bondage of karma” or “pains of** ***saṁsāra*****”.**

### Text 40

*indriyāṇi mano buddhir asyādhiṣṭhānam ucyate \|*  
*etair vimohayaty eṣa jñānam āvṛtya dehinam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. The pure** ***jīva*****, with a** ***svarūpa*** **of knowledge, is known as the** ***dehī*****, possessor of the body when he accepts a body. This** ***kāma*** **covers the** ***jīva’s*** **knowledge by bases known as the senses, mind and intelligence and bewilders him.**

**When ignorance, the subtle** ***tattva*** **of** ***kāma,*** **first covers the** ***jīva,*** **who is a particle of pure consciousness, having a sense of “I”, with material** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **material intelligence acts as its location. Then when material** ***ahaṅkāra*** **matures, it gives a second location in the form of the mind. The mind, turning to material objects, makes the senses as the third location. Taking shelter of these three locations,** ***kāma*** **throws the** ***jīva*** **towards material sense objects.**

**The** ***jīva’s*** **turning towards me by free will, is called knowledge (*****vidyā*****) and turning away from me by free will is called ignorance (*****avidyā*****).**

### Text 41

*tasmāt tvam indriyāṇy ādau niyamya bharatarṣabha \|*  
*pāpmānaṁ prajahi hy enaṁ jñāna-vijñāna-nāśanam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41. Therefore O best of the Bharata lineage, first control the senses \[by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****\] and conquer this** ***kāma,*** **the form of great sin, which destroys** ***jñāna*** **and** ***vijñāna*****. Destroying this diseased condition, leading the** ***jīva*** **to its true nature, take support of** ***jīva’s svarūpa*** **composed of** ***prema*****.**

**{The** ***jīva*** **bound by matter should first observe** ***sva-dharma*** **and** ***yukta-vairāgya*****. Then, after attaining** ***sādhana-bhakti,*** **you will attain** ***prema*****. Pure** ***bhakti*** **attained by my mercy and devotees’ mercy is most rare and also sometimes arises suddenly.}**

**\[The** ***jīva*** **bound by matter should first observe** ***sva-dharma*** **through** ***karma-yoga*** **and then, after attaining** ***sādhana-bhakti,*** **you will attain** ***prema*****.\]**

### Text 42

*indriyāṇi parāṇy āhur indriyebhyaḥ paraṁ manaḥ \|*  
*manasas tu parā buddhir yo buddheḥ paratas tu saḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**42. To summarize, you are a** ***jīva*****. That is your** ***tattva*****. Unexpectedly, being bound by matter, you think** ***ātmā*** **is senses, mind and intelligence. This is an error arising from ignorance.**

**The senses are superior to and finer than matter. But superior to and finer than the senses is the mind. Superior to and finer than the mind is the intelligence. Superior to the intelligence is the {*****ātmā*** **or** ***jīva*****}/\[*****jīvātmā*****\].**

### Text 43

*evaṁ buddheḥ paraṁ buddhvā saṁstabhyātmānam ātmanā \|*  
*jahi śatruṁ mahā-bāho kāma-rūpaṁ durāsadam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**43. Thus, understanding your spiritual tattva , and knowing yourself to be servant of the Lord, superior to material variety and non-variety, steadying yourself by {cit-śakti} / \[ātmā-śakti\], destroy invincible kāma, \[which is a form of ignorance contrary to the spiritual tattva,\] taking support of the gradual method.**

**Комментарий:**

Hearing the conclusions of the previous chapter, a doubt arose in Arjuna’s mind. If the method of karma alone produces understanding of the ātmā’s true nature, it is better to take support of this understanding at once. With the intention of removing this doubt, showing that the jīva who has attained a material body cannot avoid karma , that it is necessary that he engage in karma, that one must have an attraction for ātmā , that one must engage in sva-dharma , that one must control the strong senses which produce akarma and vikarma, and that karma-yoga is the only means of conquering material kāma , the Lord ascertains that one must practice karma-yoga, in the form of offering it to the Lord. There is also deliberation on the uselessness of attempting to renounce karma when in an immature state and to control the senses and mind separately.

## Chapter Four - Jñāna-prāpty-upāya-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*imaṁ vivasvate yogaṁ proktavān aham avyayam \|*  
*vivasvān manave prāha manur ikṣvākave ’bravīt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: I previously spoke this indestructible knowledge of** ***niṣkāma-karma*** **leading to** ***jñāna-yoga*** **to the sun god, Vivasvān. He spoke it to Manu, and Manu spoke it to Ikṣvāku.**

### Text 2

*evaṁ paramparā-prāptam imaṁ rājarṣayo viduḥ \|*  
*sa kāleneha mahatā yogo naṣṭaḥ parantapa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. All the saintly kings understood this** ***yoga*** **received in this disciplic succession. Over a long time, this** ***yoga*** **was unexpectedly lost in this world, O afflicter of enemies.**

### Text 3

*sa evāyaṁ mayā te ’dya yogaḥ proktaḥ purātanaḥ \|*  
*bhakto ’si me sakhā ceti rahasyaṁ hy etad uttamam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. I spoke this eternal** ***yoga*** **to you today because you are a devotee and friend. Thus, though this highest** ***yoga*** **is most secret, I have taught it to you.**

**Taking my teaching as all the Vedic scriptures, fight, taking support of this** ***yoga*****.**

### Text 4

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*aparaṁ bhavato janma paraṁ janma vivasvataḥ \|*  
*katham etad vijānīyāṁ tvam ādau proktavān iti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. Arjuna said: Vivasvān was born previous to you. You have appeared recently. How can I have faith that you spoke this** ***yoga*** **to Vivasvān previously?**

### Text 5

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*bahūni me vyatītāni janmāni tava cārjuna \|*  
*tāny ahaṁ veda sarvāṇi na tvaṁ vettha parantapa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. The Lord said: O Arjuna, conqueror of enemies! You and I have gone through many births. Since I am the supreme** ***īśvara*****, I remember all of these. You, being a** ***jīva,*** **a particle of consciousness, do not remember all this.**

**When I appear in this world, you, a perfected devotee, take birth with me for nourishing the pastimes. But I alone understand everything since I alone am the omniscient person.**

### Text 6

*ajo ’pi sann avyayātmā bhūtānām īśvaro ’pi san \|*  
*prakṛtiṁ svām adhiṣṭhāya saṁbhavāmy ātma-māyayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. Though I and all of you come to the world repeatedly, there is a difference between my and your coming. I am the** ***īśvara*** **(lord) of all beings, without birth (*****aja*****) and imperishable in nature. I appear in my** ***svarūpa,*** **by taking shelter of my own** ***cit-śakti,*** **\[giving mercy to the** ***jīvas*****\].**

**But all the** ***jīvas,*** **controlled by** ***māyā-śakti,*** **take birth in this world and do not remember their previous births. Taking shelter of the subtle body by karma, the** ***jīva*** **again takes birth. When I appear as a** ***devatā*** **or animal form, it is by my independent desire alone. My pure, spiritual body is not covered by subtle or gross bodies like the** ***jīva*** **is. I easily manifest in the material world with my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **situated in Vaikuṇṭha.**

**If you say, “How can a spiritual entity manifest in the material world?” then please listen. My** ***śakti*** **is inconceivable and beyond all thought. Thus, whatever is done by that** ***śakti*** **cannot be grasped by your reasoning. By natural knowledge, this alone you should understand: the Lord, endowed with** ***acintya-śakti,*** **is not obstructed by any material law. If he desires, he can manifest in the material world easily and purely the whole Vaikuṇṭha** ***tattva*****. Or reversing the whole of matter, he can bestow a spiritual nature. Is there any doubt that, with** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form, beyond material rules, I manifest purely in full form when I appear in this material world?**

**By whose** ***māyā*** **the** ***jīva*** **moves, that is called my** ***prakṛti*****. But one should understand that my personal** ***prakṛti*** **is the** ***cit-śakti*****. My** ***śakti*** **is one, but near me it is the** ***cit-śakti*** **and near the** ***jīva*** **bound by karma it is** ***māyā-śakti*****. In this way I am endowed with various types of power.**

### Text 7

*yadā yadā hi dharmasya glānir bhavati bhārata \|*  
*abhyutthānam adharmasya tadātmānaṁ sṛjāmy aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. The principle concerning my appearance is this: I am independent. If I desire, I appear. Whenever there is destruction of** ***dharma*** **and rise of** ***adharma*** **then I by my desire appear.**

**All my rules for carrying out the universal affairs {cannot be conquered}/\[are without beginning\] but due to time, when all these rules for some undefined reason are disturbed, then** ***adharma*** **becomes strong, due to faults in time. To remove the fault, except me no one is capable. Therefore, I manifest in the world with assistance of my** ***cit-śakti*** **and extinguish the dissipation of** ***dharma*****. It is not only in Bhārata that one can see me appear. I manifest by my will as necessary among all kingdoms—*****devatā*****, animal, etc.**

**Thus do not think that I do not manifest among the kingdoms of** ***mlecchas*** **and outcastes. If there is destruction of** ***dharma*** **among those lamentable people who accept some** ***dharma*** **as** ***sva-dharma,*** **then I protect their** ***dharma*** **becoming a** ***śakty-āveśa avatāra*** **among them.**

**But in Bhārata, since** ***sva-dharma*** **is performed nicely being related to** ***varṇāśrama-dharma,*** **I take more care to establish** ***dharma*** **for all my citizens of those places. Thus, one will see attractive** ***yugāvatāras*** **and** ***aṁśāvatāras*** **in Bhārata. Where there is no** ***varṇāśrama-dharma,*** **there** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga,*** **its result** ***jñāna-yoga*** **and the final result** ***bhakti-yoga*** **are not practiced nicely. Thus, the** ***bhakti*** **which is seen to arise to some degree among the outcastes should be known as a sudden proliferation due to devotees’ mercy.**

### Text 8

*paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṁ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām \|*  
*dharma-saṁsthāpanārthāya saṁbhavāmi yuge yuge \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Investing my** ***śakti*** **in all my devotees such as the saintly kings (*****rājarṣis*****) and** ***brahmarṣis*****, I establish** ***varṇāśrama-dharma*****. But my own personal** ***avatāra*** **is necessary to protect the greatest devotees from {the non-devotees}/\[the grief of longing to see me\]. Thus, becoming a** ***yugāvatāra,*** **I {protect the devotees}/\[deliver the devotees from grief\].**

**{Separating the non-devotees, I arrange for them destructible** ***dharmas.*****}/\[Killing Rāvaṇa and Kaṁsa, I deliver them.\]**

**Preaching** ***bhakti*** **with hearing and chanting, I establish the** ***jīva’s*** **eternal** ***sva-dharma*****.** ***Yuga*** **after** ***yuga,*** **I appear. By this, even in Kali-yuga, I make my appearance. This you should accept.**

**The Kali-yuga** ***avatāra*** **will establish the highest, rarest** ***prema*** **simply by** ***kīrtana*****, etc. There is no other purport to this. Though this** ***avatāra*** **without doubt is the best of** ***avatāras,*** **it is to be hidden from general people. My greatest devotees naturally will be attracted by this** ***avatāra*** **particularly. You also will be seen to manifest in order to help. This** ***avatāra*** **to deliver the people of Kali does not destroy demons but destroys the sins of the wicked. That is the great secret of this hidden** ***avatāra*****.**

### Text 9

*janma karma ca me divyam evaṁ yo vetti tattvataḥ \|*  
*tyaktvā dehaṁ punar janma naiti mām eti so ’rjuna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. He who understands, with consideration of the** ***tattva*** **mentioned, that I take birth and perform actions by my** ***acintya-cit-śakti,*** **does not take birth again after giving up the \[material\] body.**

**Under control of my** ***hlādinī-śakti*** **manifested through my** ***cit-śakti,*** **he attains my eternal service. Those who conclude that my birth, actions and body manifested in the world are temporary and material attain** ***saṁsāra*** **due to ignorance. People paralysed by** ***karmas*** **generally are bound by such conclusions. Except for mercy of the devotee, they will not develop pure bhakti.**

### Text 10

*vīta-rāga-bhaya-krodhā man-mayā mām upāśritāḥ \|*  
*bahavo jñāna-tapasā pūtā mad-bhāvam āgatāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. People bewildered about the spiritual nature and the purity of my birth, actions and body are motivated by three tendencies: material attachment, fear and anger. Those whose intelligence is extremely bound by matter and they manifest attachment to matter to the extent that they do not accept that there is any eternal entity called spiritual** ***tattva*****. They say that nature (*****svabhāva*****) is the supreme** ***tattva*****. Among them, some point out matter alone as the eternal cause, which manifests spiritual** ***tattva*****. All materialists,** ***svabhāva-vādīs*** **and** ***vidhi-vādīs*** **devoid of consciousness, pushed by attachment, are consequently cheated from spiritual attachment to the supreme** ***tattva*** **in every work.**

**Some deliberators accept the spiritual** ***tattva*** **as an eternal entity, but having given up natural knowledge, always take shelter of logic. Carefully rejecting all types of** ***guṇas*** **and actions in matter as being temporary, they manufacture some indistinct, indescribable Brahman imagined as the opposite of matter. It is nothing other than this: it is only an opposite manifestation of my** ***māyā*****. It is not my eternal** ***svarūpa.***

**With fear that they will take shelter of material** ***dharma*** **in meditating on me, they develop distaste for meditating on my** ***svarūpa*** **and the worship of my** ***svarūpa*****. Because of this fear, they are cheated of the** ***svarūpa*** **of the supreme** ***tattva*****.**

**Some people, unable to be determine anything beyond matter, determine void and** ***nirvāṇa*** **as the supreme** ***tattva*** **in a heart overwhelmed by anger. Buddhism and Jainism philosophies come from this.**

**{Becoming free of attachment, fear and anger of this type, seeing me everywhere and taking complete shelter of me, accepting the previously mentioned knowledge, purifying themselves by the austerity of tolerating the poison of bad logic previously described, many people attained pure** ***prema*** **for me.}**

**\[Rejecting attachment, fear and anger of this type, seeing me everywhere and taking complete shelter of me, purifying themselves by the austerity in the form of knowledge related to me and practice of that, many people attained pure** ***prema*** **for me.\]**

### Text 11

*ye yathā māṁ prapadyante tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham \|*  
*mama vartmānuvartante manuṣyāḥ pārtha sarvaśaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11*****.*** **I respond to persons in the way that they surrender to me. Being the final goal of all philosophies, I am to be attained by all.**

**Those who are pure devotees, always serving my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form in the supreme abode, attain supreme bliss. I give** ***nirvāṇa*** **liberation in the form of** ***nirviśeṣa*** **Brahman to those who are impersonalists, by destroying their** ***ātmā*****. Not accepting the eternal nature of my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form, their** ***cid-ānanda svarūpa*** **disappears. Among them, according to the fault of their faith, I give some temporary birth. Becoming void in nature, I make the existence of those who are voidists full of void.**

**I am attained as a material form by those who are dull, involved in material** ***karmas,*** **or those who are proponents of material rules, giving them forms of consciousness almost like matter, covering their** ***ātmās*****.**

**Those who are** ***karmīs*** **attain me, in the form of {*****īśvara*****}/\[the Lord of** ***yajña*****\], the giver of results of** ***karma*****. To those who are** ***yogīs,*** **I, in the form of** ***īśvara*****, give** ***vibhūtis*** **or impersonal liberation.**

**{In this way, being all** ***svarūpa*****s, I am attained by proponents of all philosophies. Among all attainments, you should know that attaining my service is the chief. All humans follow my various paths.}**

**\[Thus, all men follow various paths of attaining me. Actually, I, the form of Kṛṣṇa, am the final attainment of them all. Worship of the Lord, meditating on the Paramātmā of the size of a thumb,** ***brahma-jñāna,*** **worship of the lord of** ***yajña,*** **are various paths to attain me. The fortunate, intelligent person attains my** ***svarūpa,*** **the attainment, making the various types of worship the means. Those who become attached to those respective** ***tattvas*** **and do not advance have incomplete attainment. That is the deep meaning of the Lord’s words.\]**

### Text 12

*kāṅkṣantaḥ karmaṇāṁ siddhiṁ yajanta iha devatāḥ \|*  
*kṣipraṁ hi mānuṣe loke siddhir bhavati karma-jā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Answering Arjuna’s question, the Lord, clearly explaining his** ***svarūpa*** **and related matters, again begins to teach deliberation on** ***karma-tattva*** **according to what was previously stated.**

**O Arjuna! Previously I said that if you clearly understand** ***karma-tattva,*** **you become free of the bondage of karma. Previously I said that one should give up** ***vikarma*** **and** ***akarma*****.** ***Karmas*** **should be accepted according to one’s condition only.** ***Karma*** **is three types:** ***nitya, naimittika*** **and** ***kāmya*****.** ***Kāmya karma*** **is better than** ***vikarma*** **and** ***akarma*****.**

**In order to fulfill** ***karma,*** **people, {having lost their discrimination by desires for enjoyment,} desiring results, worship many** ***devatās.*** **By that they quickly attain results born of** ***karmas*****. Desiring progress in temporary** ***saṁsāra,*** **people perform all** ***karmas*****. The insignificant** ***devatās,*** **givers of results of** ***karmas,*** **being satisfied by those** ***karmas,*** **quickly give results. I will tell you gradually who these** ***devatās*** **are.**

### Text 13

*cātur-varṇyaṁ mayā sṛṣṭaṁ guṇa-karma-vibhāgaśaḥ \|*  
*tasya kartāram api māṁ viddhy akartāram avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. Thus the four** ***varṇas*** **were created by me according to distinctions of quality and work. In this world there is no other doer than I. Thus I and no one else is the maker of the** ***varṇa-dharma*** **and all the** ***varṇas*****. But you should know me as the non-doer and indestructible, though I am known as the maker of the** ***varṇa-dharma*****.**

**I made this** ***varṇa-dharma*** **by my** ***māyā-śakti*** **because of the** ***jīva’s*** **karma. Actually I, the Lord of the** ***cit-śakti,*** **have no prejudice by creating the path of** ***karmas*****. The cause is the** ***jīva’s*** **karma or misuse of his quality of independence.**

### Text 14

*na māṁ karmāṇi limpanti na me karma-phale spṛhā \|*  
*iti māṁ yo ’bhijānāti karmabhir na sa badhyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. Because of the** ***jīva’s*** **karma, I have made** ***karma-tattva*****. But it cannot contaminate me. I have no desire for results of** ***karma*** **since these results of** ***karma*** **are most insignificant for me, the Lord full of six opulences. The person who can understand my imperishable** ***tattva*** **while deliberating on my independence and the** ***jīva’s*** **path of** ***karma,*** **is never bound by karma. Performing pure** ***bhakti,*** **he attains me.**

### Text 15

*evaṁ jñātvā kṛtaṁ karma pūrvair api mumukṣubhiḥ \|*  
*kuru karmaiva tasmāt tvaṁ pūrvaiḥ pūrvataraṁ kṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Those desiring liberation in ancient times, understanding this** ***tattva,*** **while giving up** ***sakāma-karma,*** **performed** ***niṣkāma-karmas*** **offered to me. You also should take shelter of \[the eternal\]** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **as performed by the pervious great devotees like \[Vivasvān,\] Janaka.**

### Text 16

*kiṁ karma kim akarmeti kavayo ’py atra mohitāḥ \|*  
*tat te karma pravakṣyāmi yaj jñātvā mokṣyase ’śubhāt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. Even the wise are bewildered in determining what is** ***karma*** **and what is** ***akarma*****. I will teach you this subject. Understanding it, you will attain liberation from everything inauspicious.**

### Text 17

*karmaṇo hy api boddhavyaṁ boddhavyaṁ ca vikarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*akarmaṇaś ca boddhavyaṁ gahanā karmaṇo gatiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. One should understand the workings of** ***karma, vikarma*** **and** ***akarma*****, analyzing them each separately. The deep truth about** ***karma*** **is difficult to understand.**

**Obligatory conduct is called** ***karma*****. \[This is** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****.\] Forbidden conduct is called** ***vikarma*****, giving an unfortunate destination. \[*****Kāmya-karma*** **is included in it.\] Not performing** ***karmas*** **is** ***akarma*****. {By not performing karmas, how can the** ***sannyāsī*** **attain auspiciousness? You should know this truth.}**

**\[By not performing** ***karmas,*** **how can the** ***sannyāsī*** **attain auspiciousness? How is one at fault in being qualified for** ***karmas*****? One should know these things.\]**

### Text 18

*karmaṇy akarma yaḥ paśyed akarmaṇi ca karma yaḥ \|*  
*sa buddhimān manuṣyeṣu sa yuktaḥ kṛtsna-karma-kṛt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. He who sees inaction in action and action in inaction is intelligent among men. He is clever and performs all actions.**

**{The purport is that all the** ***karmas*** **performed by the** ***niṣkāma-karma-yogī*** **are** ***akarma,*** **a form of renouncing** ***karma*** **and renouncing** ***karmas*** **(*****akarma*****) is his performance of** ***niṣkāma-karmas*****. Thus, though he performs all** ***karmas*** **he is not a** ***karmī*****.}**

**\[The purport is that all the** ***karmas*** **performed by the** ***niṣkāma-karma-yogī*** **give attainment of the real** ***ātmā*****. He sees all** ***karmas*** **to be a form of** ***akarma*****.\]**

***Akarma*** **and** ***karma*** **take one form in him.**

### Text 19

*yasya sarve samārambhāḥ kāma-saṅkalpa-varjitāḥ \|*  
*jñānāgni-dagdha-karmāṇaṁ tam āhuḥ paṇḍitaṁ budhāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. He is said to be learned who engages in all** ***karmas,*** **while devoid of desire for enjoyment, and has burned up all** ***karmas*** **by the fire of knowledge.**

**{All prescribed** ***karmas*** **and forbidden actions that he does are burned up by the fire of knowledge attained by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga.*****}**

**\[Then his** ***karmas*** **attain the form of knowledge.\]**

### Text 20

*tyaktvā karma-phalāsaṅgaṁ nitya-tṛpto nirāśrayaḥ \|*  
*karmaṇy abhipravṛtto ’pi naiva kiñcit karoti saḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Free from the desire to acquire and maintain objects, satisfied with his own bliss, the person who, giving up attachment to results of his** ***karmas,*** **engages in all** ***karmas*** **does nothing at all, though doing all** ***karmas*****. {He is not bound by the results of** ***karmas*****.}/\[His** ***karmas*** **are without reactions.\]**

### Text 21

*nirāśīr yata-cittātmā tyakta-sarva-parigrahaḥ \|*  
*śārīraṁ kevalaṁ karma kurvan nāpnoti kilbiṣam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. {Keeping one’s body and mind subservient to intelligence, rejecting desire for results and all acquisition, not endeavoring for possessing, he does** ***karmas*** **only for maintaining his body.}**

**\[Making body and mind dependent on intelligence, becoming free of the desire for results and all acquisition, rejecting possessiveness towards acquired objects, he does** ***karmas*** **only for maintaining his body.\]**

**By that, he does not generate sin or piety from his** ***karmas*****.**

### Text 22

*yadṛcchā-lābha-santuṣṭo dvandvātīto vimatsaraḥ \|*  
*samaḥ siddhāv asiddhau ca kṛtvāpi na nibadhyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Satisfied with what comes of its own accord, not controlled by dualities of happiness and suffering, attachment and repulsion, free of envy, calm in the face of success or failure, that person is not bound by whatever** ***karmas*** **he performs.**

### Text 23

*gata-saṅgasya muktasya jñānāvasthita-cetasaḥ \|*  
*yajñāyācarataḥ karma samagraṁ pravilīyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. Devoid of desire for results, free from attachment, mind situated in knowledge, and engaged in actions of yajña for the Lord, he destroys karmic reactions effectively.**

**Комментарий:**

All the karmas of the niṣkāma-karma-yogī do not produce what the karma-mīmāṁsakas call apūrva . Jaimini’s theory of karma-mīmāṁsaka is that the karmas done by a person, attaining the form of apūrva, give results birth after birth. This is not possible for the niṣkāma-karma-yogī.

### Text 24

*brahmārpaṇaṁ brahma havir brahmāgnau brahmaṇā hutam \|*  
*brahmaiva tena gantavyaṁ brahma-karma-samādhinā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. Hear how** ***jñāna*** **is produced by** ***karmas*** **in the form of** ***yajña*****. Later I will describe the various types of** ***yajña*****. Now I will discuss the main principle of** ***yajña*****. Please listen.**

**The spiritual** ***tattva*** **is completely different from the material world. \[The effort to carry out\] material action is unavoidable for the** ***jīva*** **bound in matter. In that material action, whatever can be perceived as spiritual, when performed properly is called** ***yajña*****. When a spiritual condition manifests in matter it is called Brahman. That Brahman is my light or \[mass of\] rays.**

**When the five \[*****aṅgas*** **of** ***yajña*****\]: offering, oblation, fire, priest and result become situated in Brahman, then it becomes proper** ***yajña*****. When a person concentrates in** ***samādhi*** **by making actions full of Brahman, he performs all his actions as a** ***yajña*****. The instrument of offering, the oblation of ghee, the fire and sacrificial priest, that is one’s whole existence, all become Brahman in nature. Thus, his goal is also Brahman.**

### Text 25

*daivam evāpare yajñaṁ yoginaḥ paryupāsate \|*  
*brahmāgnāv apare yajñaṁ yajñenaivopajuhvati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. A person dedicated to such** ***yajña*** **is a** ***yogī*****. There are different types of** ***yogīs*** **according to different types of** ***yajñas*****. There as many types of** ***yogīs*** **as there are types of** ***yajñas*****. When seeing the different forms, there are thus many types of** ***yajñas*** **and** ***yogīs*****. If one divides them with discernment, all** ***yajñas*** **are either** ***karma-yajñas*** **(*****dravya-yajñas*****) or** ***jñāna-yajñas*** **(*****yajñas*** **in the form of spiritual contemplation). These are the two divisions, which will be shown later. Now I will speak about the many varieties of** ***yajña*****. Please listen.**

**The** ***karma-yogīs*** **worship** ***daiva-yajña*** **(worship of** ***devatās*****). In that, there is worship of Indra, Varuṇa and others, persons given responsibility of ruling and endowed with material abilities by me. By this they gradually attain** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****.**

**All the** ***jñāna-yogīs,*** **taking support of** ***mahāvakya tat tvam asi,*** **offer the** ***jīva*** **(*****tvam*****) into** ***tat*** **(Brahman) using the** ***praṇava*****-mantra (*****oṁ*****). Its superiority will be described later.**

### Text 26

*śrotrādīnīndriyāṇy anye saṁyamāgniṣu juhvati \|*  
*śabdādīn viṣayān anya indriyāgniṣu juhvati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. The** ***naiṣṭhikī brahmacārīs*** **offer all the senses like the ear into the fire of mind-control. All householders, \[dedicated to** ***sva-dharma,*****\] offer all the sense objects like sound into the fire of the sense organs.**

### Text 27

*sarvāṇīndriya-karmāṇi prāṇa-karmāṇi cāpare \|*  
*ātma-saṁyama-yogāgnau juhvati jñāna-dīpite \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Proponents of oneness (*****kaivalya*****) such as** ***yogīs*** **following Patañjali, searching for** ***pratyag ātmā*****, offer actions of the ten types of** ***prāṇa*** **and the actions of all the senses into the fire of the pure** ***jīvātmā*****, the object called** ***tvam*****.**

**The** ***ātmā*** **inclined to material objects is called** ***parāg ātmā*****. The** ***ātmā*** **which rejects material objects is called** ***pratyag-ātmā*****. These people conclude that only** ***pratyag-ātmā*** **exists and nothing else such as mind.**

### Text 28

*dravya-yajñās tapo-yajñā yoga-yajñās tathāpare \|*  
*svādhyāya-jñāna-yajñāś ca yatayaḥ saṁśita-vratāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. All these may be divided into four parts:** ***dravya-yajña, tapo-yajña, yoga-yajña*** **and** ***svādhyāya-jñāna-yajña.***

***Yajñas*** **involving materials are called** ***dravya-yajña*****. Those involving austerities such as** ***kṛcchra, cāndrāyaṇa*** **and** ***cāturmāsya*** **are called** ***tapo-yajña*****.** ***Aṣṭāṅga-yoga*** **is** ***yoga-yajña*****. Deliberating on spiritual and material while deliberating on the meaning of the** ***Vedas*** **is called** ***jñāna-yajña*****. Those persons concentrating on these four types of** ***yajña*** **are called renunciates with intense vows (*****yatayaḥ saṁśita-vratāḥ)*****.**

### Text 29

*apāne juhvati prāṇaṁ prāṇe ’pānaṁ tathāpare \|*  
*prāṇāpāna-gatī ruddhvā prāṇāyāma-parāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*apare niyatāhārāḥ prāṇān prāṇeṣu juhvati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29*****.*** **These four types are seen in the Vedas and** ***smṛtis*** **following them. Apart from these, in** ***tantra*** **scriptures which expand the meaning of the Vedas, according to particular time,** ***yajñas*** **in the form of** ***haṭha-yoga*** **and various types of vows of control are taught.**

**Persons following these scriptures become steady in** ***prāṇāyāma*** **and stop the** ***prāṇa-vāyu*** **in the** ***apāna-vāyu*** **and vice versa and gradually practice** ***kumbaka*** **by stopping movements of** ***prāṇa*** **and** ***apāna*****. Some, restricting eating, offer all the** ***prāṇas*** **\[into** ***prāṇas*****\].**

### Text 30

*sarve ’py ete yajña-vido yajña-kṣapita-kalmaṣāḥ \|*  
*yajña-śiṣṭāmṛta-bhujo yānti brahma sanātanam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. All of these are knowers of the** ***tattva*** **of sacrifice and destroy sin through** ***yajña*****. After enjoying the sweet remnants of the** ***yajña*****, they finally attain the previously described eternal Brahman.**

### Text 31

*nāyaṁ loko ’sty ayajñasya kuto ’nyaḥ kuru-sattama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. Therefore O Arjuna, best of the Kurus! The person who does not perform sacrifice attains nothing in this life, what to speak of the next. Therefore,** ***yajña*** **is a necessary** ***karma*****. In this one should understand that** ***smārta-varṇāśrama-dharma, aṣṭāṅga-yoga,*** **and** ***vaidika-yajña*****, etc., are all** ***yajña*****.** ***Brahma-jñāna*** **is a special** ***yajña*****. There is no** ***karma*** **except** ***yajña*** **in this world. Everything else is** ***vikarma*****.**

### Text 32

*evaṁ bahu-vidhā yajñā vitatā brahmaṇo mukhe \|*  
*karma-jān viddhi tān sarvān evaṁ jñātvā vimokṣyase \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. Thus, many types of sacrifice have been described in the** ***Vedas*** **and scriptures following the Vedas. They arise from** ***karmas*** **(actions) of body, mind and words. Thus, they arise from** ***karmas*****. If one can deliberate on this** ***karma-tattva,*** **you can be liberated from the bondage of karma.**

### Text 33

*śreyān dravya-mayād yajñāj jñāna-yajñaḥ parantapa \|*  
*sarvaṁ karmākhilaṁ pārtha jñāne parisamāpyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. Even though one gradually attains** ***jñāna*** **by all these** ***yajñas,*** **attains peace next, and finally develops auspiciousness of the** ***jīva*** **in the form of** ***bhakti*** **to me, one should understand one deep conclusion related to all** ***yajñas*****.**

**According to different types of steadiness, the** ***yajñas*** **are sometimes** ***dravya-maya yajñas*** **(*****yajñas*** **concerned with material objects and goals). Sometimes they are** ***jñāna-maya yajñas*****. The** ***jñāna-maya yajñas*** **are superior to the** ***dravya-maya yajñas*****. O Pārtha! All** ***karmas*** **culminate in** ***jñāna.*** **When all the** ***yajñas*** **are conducted but devoid of spiritual vision, then all the performance becomes merely** ***dravya-maya*****. When they are executed with spiritual vision, then actually though being** ***dravya-maya,*** **they become spiritual (*****cin-maya*****) or** ***jñāna-maya*****. The situation where the** ***yajña*** **is only** ***dravya-maya*** **is called** ***karma-kāṇḍa*** **and when it is** ***jñāna-maya*** **it is called** ***jñāna-kāṇḍa*****. In performing actions of** ***yajña*** **one must be careful of the sacrificer (his mentality).**

### Text 34

*tad viddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā \|*  
*upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. If you say that it is difficult to distinguish between the** ***dravya-maya*** **and** ***jñāna-maya yajña,*** **I teach this: take shelter of a guru who sees** ***tattva,*** **in order to attain knowledge, after considering these distinctions. After offering respects to the guru who sees** ***tattva,*** **satisfying him by serving him sincerely, ask questions concerning** ***tattva*****. He will teach you knowledge.**

### Text 35

*yaj jñātvā na punar moham evaṁ yāsyasi pāṇḍava \|*  
*yena bhūtāny aśeṣāṇi drakṣyasy ātmany atho mayi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. Today, you were eager to give up you** ***sva-dharma*** **of fighting because of bewilderment. When you obtain knowledge of** ***tattva*** **taught by guru, this bewilderment will not take shelter of you. By this knowledge of** ***tattva,*** **you will know that all beings from human to animals {are situated as one spiritual** ***tattva, jīva*****}/\[ are all one spiritual** ***tattva*** **in the form of** ***jīvātmā*****\]. Their material difference occurs because of** ***upādhis*****. All this is situated in me, Bhagavān, the supreme cause, in the form of the effects of my** ***śakti*****.**

### Text 36

*api ced asi pāpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ pāpa-kṛt-tamaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṁ jñāna-plavenaiva vṛjinaṁ santariṣyasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36. Even if you have commited the worst sins, you can cross the ocean of suffering by climbing on the boat of knowledge.**

### Text 37

*yathaidhāṁsi samiddho ’gnir bhasmasāt kurute ’rjuna \|*  
*jñānāgniḥ sarva-karmāṇi bhasma-sāt kurute tathā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**37. O Arjuna! Just as a blazing fire burns to ashes all fuel wood, the fire of knowledge turns to ashes all karmas.**

**\[It destroys the** ***aprārabdha*** **and** ***kriyamāṇa*** **karmas and makes the** ***prārabdha*** **karmas weak.\]**

### Text 38

*na hi jñānena sadṛśaṁ pavitram iha vidyate \|*  
*tat svayaṁ yoga-saṁsiddhaḥ kālenātmani vindati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. In this world, there is nothing as pure as** ***jñāna*** **or spiritual** ***tattva*****. After some time, you will attain this knowledge as the true result of** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **of your** ***ātmā.***

**By this statement, it should be understood that peace, the result of knowledge, is better than knowledge (*****jñāna*****).**

**{One cannot say that there is nothing as pure as** ***jñāna,*** **there is no more excellent** ***tattva*** **than** ***jñāna*****.}**

**\[Another name for peace is – taking shelter of the Lord’s feet. That will be described at the end.\]**

### Text 39

*śraddhāvān labhate jñānaṁ tat-paraḥ saṁyatendriyaḥ \|*  
*jñānaṁ labdhvā parāṁ śāntim acireṇādhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. Attentive and controlling the senses, having faith, a person attains knowledge. The person who has no faith in** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **is not qualified for** ***jñāna*****. Undertaking** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **with faith, the person very quickly attains the highest peace. {I will explain this highest peace.}**

### Text 40

*ajñaś cāśraddadhānaś ca saṁśayātmā vinaśyati \|*  
*nāyaṁ loko ’sti na paro na sukhaṁ saṁśayātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. {That person who is ignorant of the nature of karma and has no faith in it, is always doubtful. This person has no auspiciousness.}**

**\[That person who is ignorant of scripture, has no faith in it, and is always doubtful has no auspiciousness.\]**

**\[Among such people\] he who is filled with doubt, does not attain happiness in this life or the next because suffering in the form of** ***saṁsāra*** **destroys his peace.**

### Text 41

*yoga-sannyasta-karmāṇaṁ jñāna-sañchinna-saṁśayam \|*  
*ātmavantaṁ na karmāṇi nibadhnanti dhanañjaya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41. Thus, O Dhanañjana! He who renounces** ***karma*** **by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****, destroys doubt by** ***jñāna*** **and understands the** ***ātmā’s*** **spiritual** ***svarūpa*****, he is not bound by any karma.**

### Text 42

*tasmād ajñāna-saṁbhūtaṁ hṛt-sthaṁ jñānāsinātmanaḥ \|*  
*chittvainaṁ saṁśayaṁ yogam ātiṣṭhottiṣṭha bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**42. Therefore, O Bhārata, all your doubts concerning niṣkāma-karma-yoga arose from ignorance. Destroy the doubts by the sword of knowledge and fight, having taken shelter of niṣkāma-karma-yoga.**

**Комментарий:**

In this *Sanātana yoga* there are two divisions: the part consisting of material objects and the spiritual portion in the form of the real nature of *ātma*. Examining the material portion, it is seen to be merely *karma*. Those who are bound to this portion are called *karma-jaḍa:* stunned by *karma*. Those who perform material karmas while aiming for the spiritual portion, are called *yukta*. On analyzing the spiritual portion in detail, one sees one portion as the *jīvātmā* and the other portion as *bhagavat-tattva*. Those who realize the *bhagavat-tattva* attain excellent portion of real ātmā. This realization is perfected by realizing the spiritual birth and activities of the Lord and his eternal association with the *jīva*.

This subject is discussed at the beginning of the chapter. The Lord is personally the first teacher of *nitya-dharma*. When the jīva is materially bound by a fault in his eternal intelligence, the Lord manifests by this *cit-śakti*, teaches the *jīva* the *tattva* about himself, and makes him suitable for his pastimes. Those who say that the Lord’s body, birth, and activities are material are very foolish. As much as one worships me purely, to that extent one attains me. All types of *karmas* of the *karma-yogī* are called *yajña*. All types of *yajña* in this world such as *daiva-yajña, brahma-yajña, brahmacarya-yajña, gṛha-medha-yajña, saṁyama-yajña, aṣṭāṅga-yoga-yajña, tapo-yajña, dravya- yajña, svādhyāya-yajña, varṇāśrama-yajña* are all filled with karma. In all of them, whatever spiritual portion of real nature of *ātma* is present that should be sought. Doubt is the greatest enemy of knowledge of *tattva*. A faithful person, taking instruction on this *tattva* from a suitable knower of *tattva* becomes a knower of *ātmā* and removing all doubt, for attaining the real nature of *ātmā*, takes support of *karma-yoga* to the extent that there is relationship with matter.

## Chapter Five - Karma sannyāsa yoga

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*sannyāsaṁ karmaṇāṁ kṛṣṇa punar yogaṁ ca śaṁsasi \|*  
*yac chreya etayor ekaṁ tan me brūhi su-niścitam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**{1. Arjuna said: O Kṛṣṇa! You said that I should give up** ***karma*** **by** ***yoga*** **and you also said that by** ***jñāna*** **cut all doubts and do** ***karma*** **in the form of fighting.}**

**\[1. Arjuna said: O Kṛṣṇa! You have praised giving up** ***karmas*** **and you also praised** ***karma-yoga*****.\]**

**Therefore, definitely tell me what I should do: give up** ***karmas*** **or do** ***karma-yoga*****.**

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*sannyāsaḥ karma-yogaś ca niḥśreyasa-karāv ubhau \|*  
*tayos tu karma-sannyāsāt karma-yogo viśiṣyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. The Lord said: Both renunciation of** ***karmas*** **and** ***karma-yoga*** **produce auspiciousness. But of the two,** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **is better than renunciation of** ***karmas*****. Giving up attachment to** ***karmas*** **is called** ***sannyāsa*** **or renunciation. In the present context, giving up** ***karmas*** **is not taught.**

### Text 3

*jñeyaḥ sa nitya-sannyāsī yo na dveṣṭi na kāṅkṣati \|*  
*nirdvandvo hi mahā-bāho sukhaṁ bandhāt pramucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. {O mighty-armed one!} the person, {who is without duality,} who neither hates nor desires results of** ***karmas,*** **is known as the constant** ***sannyāsī.*** **Easily this person \[without dualities\] becomes freed from bondage of karma.**

### Text 4

*sāṅkhya-yogau pṛthag bālāḥ pravadanti na paṇḍitāḥ \|*  
*ekam apy āsthitaḥ samyag ubhayor vindate phalam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. I will explain to you the basic** ***tattva*** **of** ***sannyāsa*** **and** ***karma-yoga*****. The foolish, ignorant** ***mīmāṁsakas*** **say that** ***sāṅkhya-yoga*** **and** ***karma-yoga*** **are different methods. \[But\] the wise do not say this. One attains the result of both by properly performing either** ***sāṅkhya-yoga*** **or** ***karma-yoga*****.**

### Text 5

*yat sāṅkhyaiḥ prāpyate sthānaṁ tad yogair api gamyate \|*  
*ekaṁ sāṅkhyaṁ ca yogaṁ ca yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. \[This is because though they have different faith, in the form of engagement in action and renunciation of action,\] the two methods are one. {Only there are two different names.}**

**He who knows that** ***sāṅkhya*** **and** ***yoga*** **are one \[till giving up the body\] knows the truth about them.**

### Text 6

*sannyāsas tu mahā-bāho duḥkham āptum ayogataḥ \|*  
*yoga-yukto munir brahma na cireṇādhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. \[O mighty-armed!\] Renunciation alone in the form of renouncing** ***karmas*** **without performing** ***karma-yoga*** **produces suffering. The sage engaged in** ***yoga*** **attains Brahman without difficulties.**

### Text 7

*yoga-yukto viśuddhātmā vijitātmā jitendriyaḥ \|*  
*sarva-bhūtātma-bhūtātmā kurvann api na lipyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. {There are three types of** ***jñānīs*** **engaged in** ***yoga*** **in order of increasing excellence: those with pure intelligence, those with pure heart and those with controlled senses. They are objects of attraction for all** ***jīvas*****. Though doing all** ***karmas,*** **they are not contaminated.}**

**\[*****Jñānīs*** **engaged in** ***yoga*****, becoming pure in intelligence, pure in heart, controlled in senses and object of attraction for all** ***jīvas,*** **though doing all** ***karmas,*** **do not become contaminated.\]**

### Text 8-9

*naiva kiñcit karomīti yukto manyeta tattva-vit \|*  
*paśyañ śṛṇvan spṛśañ jighrann aśnan gacchan svapañ śvasan \|\|*  
*pralapan visṛjan gṛhṇann unmiṣan nimiṣann api \|*  
*indriyāṇīndriyārtheṣu vartanta iti dhārayan \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8-9. A** ***karma-yogī*****, while he is seeing, hearing, touching, smelling, eating, going, sleeping, breathing, because of knowledge of** ***tattva,*** **thinks, “I am doing nothing at all.” While speaking, excreting (through genital or anus), receiving, opening and closing the eyes, he thinks, “I am in this material body. It is doing everything. I am only thinking I am doing everything. \[When I have realization of** ***ātmā*****, my relationship with material objects will cease.\]”**

### Text 10

*brahmaṇy ādhāya karmāṇi saṅgaṁ tyaktvā karoti yaḥ \|*  
*lipyate na sa pāpena padma-patram ivāmbhasā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10*****.*** **He who, giving up attachment to results while offering** ***karmas*** **to Brahman, performs** ***karmas*** **is not contaminated by sin in those actions, just as a lotus leaf though situated in water is not touched by water.**

### Text 11

*kāyena manasā buddhyā kevalair indriyair api \|*  
*yoginaḥ karma kurvanti saṅgaṁ tyaktvātma-śuddhaye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. For purification of the {mind}/\[self\], the** ***karma-yogīs*** **perform action, giving up attachment to results, using voice, mind, intelligence or {even the senses alone}/\[purified senses\].**

### Text 12

*yuktaḥ karma-phalaṁ tyaktvā śāntim āpnoti naiṣṭhikīm \|*  
*ayuktaḥ kāma-kāreṇa phale sakto nibadhyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. The** ***yogīs,*** **giving up results of** ***karmas,*** **attain steady peace, or freedom from karma. On the other hand, those who are not so engaged, i.e. those practicing** ***sakāma-karma,*** **are bound by karma through their impulses of desire, with attachment to results.**

### Text 13

*sarva-karmāṇi manasā sannyasyāste sukhaṁ vaśī \|*  
*nava-dvāre pure dehī naiva kurvan na kārayan \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. Renouncing all** ***karmas*** **by the mind, even while performing all external actions, the** ***jīva*** **continues to live very happily in the house of the body with nine gates. He does nothing in reality and makes others do nothing at all.**

### Text 14

*na kartṛtvaṁ na karmāṇi lokasya sṛjati prabhuḥ \|*  
*na karma-phala-saṁyogaṁ svabhāvas tu pravartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. \[The jīva, master of his body and senses, does not make himself the doer or cause others to do. He does not enjoy the results of karma. His nature produced by ignorance is the cause for all this.\]**

**Комментарий:**

In saying that the jīva is not the doer, do not think that all actions are impelled by the Supreme Lord. By saying that the Supreme Lord makes the jīva the doer and performs his actions would make the Lord unequal and cruel. The Lord is even not connected with results of karma. This all arises by the nature of the jīva, his beginningless ignorance.

### Text 15

*nādatte kasyacit pāpaṁ na caiva sukṛtaṁ vibhuḥ \|*  
*ajñānenāvṛtaṁ jñānaṁ tena muhyanti jantavaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Thus,** ***īśvara*** **does not receive the sinful or pious reactions of the** ***jīva*****. The** ***jīva*** **is naturally conscious by nature. By the** ***avidyā-śakti,*** **his** ***svarūpa*** **is covered and becomes bound up. Bewildered by bodily identification the** ***jīva*** **thinks that he is the doer.**

### Text 16

*jñānena tu tad ajñānaṁ yeṣāṁ nāśitam ātmanaḥ \|*  
*teṣām āditya-vaj jñānaṁ prakāśayati tat param \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. There are two types of knowledge: material and spiritual. Knowledge related to material** ***prakṛti*** **is called the** ***jīva’s*** **ignorance or** ***avidyā*****. Spiritual knowledge is called** ***vidyā*****.** ***Jīvas*** **who develop spiritual knowledge and destroy material knowledge develop spiritual knowledge in the form of the highest knowledge and manifest the spiritual supreme** ***tattva*****.**

### Text 17

*tad-buddhayas tad-ātmānas tan-niṣṭhās tat-parāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*gacchanty apunar-āvṛttiṁ jñāna-nirdhūta-kalmaṣāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. Those who attain fixity of intellect and mind on the Supreme Lord endowed with a spiritual** ***svarūpa,*** **cleansed of the contamination of ignorance by knowledge, they attain liberation in the form of not taking birth again. Those who have attraction to the spiritual no longer have material attraction. They are attracted only to hearing about and glorifying me.**

### Text 18

*vidyā-vinaya-saṁpanne brāhmaṇe gavi hastini \|*  
*śuni caiva śvapāke ca paṇḍitāḥ sama-darśinaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18.** ***Jñānīs*** **who attain spiritual qualities, after giving up unequal vision—seeing in terms of good, mediocre and inferior by material qualities—are termed learned, being endowed with seeing equally all beings—the learned** ***brāhmaṇa,*** **cow, elephant, dog and dog-eater.**

### Text 19

*ihaiva tair jitaḥ sargo yeṣāṁ sāmye sthitaṁ manaḥ \|*  
*nirdoṣaṁ hi samaṁ brahma tasmād brahmaṇi te sthitāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. Those whose minds are situated in equality conquer over** ***saṁsāra,*** **this world. They are faultless, having equal vision of Brahman. Thus, they are situated in Brahman.**

### Text 20

*na prahṛṣyet priyaṁ prāpya nodvijet prāpya cāpriyam \|*  
*sthira-buddhir asaṁmūḍho brahma-vid brahmaṇi sthitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. The knower of Brahman, becoming situated in Brahman, being unattached to externals, has fixed intelligence. He does not experience joy on attaining dear objects or agitation on attaining repulsive objects.**

### Text 21

*bāhya-sparśeṣv asaktātmā vindaty ātmani yat sukham \|*  
*sa brahma-yoga-yuktātmā sukham akṣayam aśnute \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. This knower of Brahman attains spiritual happiness. Absorbed in Brahman, he experiences indestructible happiness.**

### Text 22

*ye hi saṁsparśajā bhogā duḥkha-yonaya eva te \|*  
*ādy-antavantaḥ kaunteya na teṣu ramate budhaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. This discriminating person is not {attached}/\[bound\] to material happiness in the form of sense objects. All happiness arising from sense objects produces suffering only. That happiness arises from contact alone. Since it has a beginning and end, it is not eternal. O Kaunteya! The previously described learned persons do not have attraction for temporary happiness at all. They accept only actions related to bodily maintenance, without desire.**

### Text 23

*śaknotīhaiva yaḥ soḍhuṁ prāk śarīra-vimokṣaṇāt \|*  
*kāma-krodhodbhavaṁ vegaṁ sa yuktaḥ sa sukhī naraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. Knowing that one should \[necessarily\] accept material objects only till giving up the material body, being able to tolerate the impulses of desire and anger by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga,*** **the person is \[absorbed in** ***ātmā-samādhi*** **and\] experiences real happiness.**

### Text 24

*yo ’ntaḥ-sukho ’ntar-ārāmas tathāntar-jyotir eva yaḥ \|*  
*sa yogī brahma-nirvāṇaṁ brahma-bhūto ’dhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. Knowing that happiness, pleasure and knowledge of the external world is temporary, accepting spiritual happiness, pleasure and knowledge of the inner world, becoming Brahman—attaining his nature as pure** ***jīva*****, he is a** ***yogī*** **and attains** ***brahma-nirvāṇa*****.**

### Text 25

*labhante brahma-nirvāṇam ṛṣayaḥ kṣīṇa-kalmaṣāḥ \|*  
*chinna-dvaidhā yatātmānaḥ sarva-bhūta-hite ratāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. The sages, with controlled mind, engaged in the welfare of all beings, devoid of doubt and having destroyed all sin, attain** ***brahma-nirvāṇa*****.**

### Text 26

*kāma-krodha-vimuktānāṁ yatīnāṁ yata-cetasām \|*  
*abhito brahma-nirvāṇaṁ vartate viditātmanām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. Those renunciates who have become free of lust and anger, who have controlled the mind, who know** ***tattva*** **of** ***ātmā*****, quickly attain complete** ***brahma-nirvāṇa*****.**

**The** ***niṣkāma-karma-yogī*****, situated in** ***saṁsāra,*** **deliberating on** ***sat*** **and** ***asat,*** **becomes situated in the** ***sat*** **entity, Brahman, which is beyond matter. The state where all material suffering is destroyed is called** ***brahma-nirvāṇa*****.**

### Texts 27-28

*sparśān kṛtvā bahir bāhyāṁś cakṣuś caivāntare bhruvoḥ \|*  
*prāṇāpānau samau kṛtvā nāsābhyantara-cāriṇau \|\|*  
*yatendriya-mano-buddhir munir mokṣa-parāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*vigatecchā-bhaya-krodho yaḥ sadā mukta eva saḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27-28. O Arjuna! By** ***karma-yoga*** **offered to the Lord purification of** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **arises. From purification of** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **arises** ***jñāna*** **concerning the entity “you”** ***tvam*****.** ***Bhakti,*** **the** ***svarūpa*** **of knowledge of** ***tat,*** **the Lord, arises from that** ***jñāna*****. One realizes Brahman, the Lord, from knowledge beyond the** ***guṇa*** **which arises from** ***bhakti*****. I have described these steps to you.**

**Now I will speak of** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga,*** **a** ***sādhana*** **for realizing Brahman, for a person with pure** ***antaḥkaraṇa*****. Listen as I speak of some of its shadow forms. Removing from the mind all external contact such as sound, touch, form, taste and smell, i.e. practicing** ***pratyāhāra*****, keeping the eyes fixed between the brows, one should concentrate on the tip of the nose. Since there is fear of sleep by keeping the eyes completely closed, and fear of seeing externally if the eyes are completely open, it is prescribed to keep the two eyes half-closed. In this way one looks between brows or at the nose.**

**Making** ***prāṇa*** **and** ***apāna vāyu*** **move in the nostrils while inhaling and exhaling, one should practice equalizing them while restricting the upward and downward flow. Sitting and employing** ***mudrās,*** **controlling the senses, mind and intelligence, the sage intent on liberation, giving up desire, fear and anger, practices realization of Brahman. When he does so, he can obtain liberation from matter in the form of qualities beyond the** ***guṇas*****. Thus, at the time of practicing** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga,*** **one can practice** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*** **as its** ***aṅga*****.**

### Text 29

*bhoktāraṁ yajña-tapasāṁ sarva-loka-maheśvaram \|*  
*suhṛdaṁ sarva-bhūtānāṁ jñātvā māṁ śāntim ṛcchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. The** ***karma-yogīs*** **just described attain liberation by knowledge of Paramātmā arising from** ***bhakti*****. Know me to be the** ***yajña*** **performed by the** ***karmīs*** **and the protector (*****bhoktā*****) of all austerities performed by the** ***jñānīs*****. I am the worshippable** ***antaryāmī puruṣa,*** **the friend of all beings, for the** ***yogīs*****.**

**Teaching** ***bhakti*** **through my devotees, I mercifully give benefit to the** ***jīvas.*** **When the** ***yogīs*** **attain absence of** ***guṇas*** **by meditation on worshippable Paramātmā, they can know me, in the form of Bhagavān. Knowing me alone as the lord of all worlds, as the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān, the** ***yogīs*** **attain liberation.**

**29. The** ***karma-yogīs*** **just described, knowing me to be the protector of all** ***yajñas*** **and austerities, the lord of all beings, and the friend of all beings and in the end gaining** ***bhakti-yoga*** **through association of devotees, attain liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Having heard the previous four chapters, the following doubt arose. If one attains liberation at the completion of *karma-yoga,* what is the position of *jñāna-yoga* and what is the form of *jñāna-yoga?* To remove this doubt all the instructions of this chapter are spoken. *Jñāna-yoga* or *sāṅkhya-yoga* and *karma-yoga* are not different. The final goal of both is only one, *bhakti*. *Karma-yoga’s* first state is *jñāna* predominated by *karma* and its final state is *karma* predominated by *jñāna*.

*Jīva* by its nature is purely spiritual. Bound up to matter by desire for material enjoyment, it gradually degrades into oneness with matter. As long as the material body remains material *karma* is unavoidable. Spiritual activities are the only method of liberation. Therefore, as pure spiritual activities become strong while in the material body, the domination of karma decreases. In this, there is no partiality of the Lord. *Karma-yoga* is an assistant in the spiritual activity. Practicing seeing everything equally, practicing detachment, repeating spiritual activities, conquering material lust and anger, and destroying doubts, one attains *brahma-nirvāṇa* or contact with the happiness of Brahman while negating matter. Practicing *aṣṭāṅga-yoga* in the form of *yama, niyama, āsana, prathyāhāra, prāṇāyama, dhyāna, dhāraṇa* and *samādhi* while passing one’s life and maintaining the body with *karma-yoga,* one gradually manifests the happiness of *bhakti* to the Lord through attaining devotee association. This is called “peace after liberation”. Then, the tendency to perform pure worship manifests the glory of the *jīva*.

## Chapter Six - Dhyāna-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*anāśritaḥ karma-phalaṁ kāryaṁ karma karoti yaḥ \|*  
*sa sannyāsī ca yogī ca na niragnir na cākriyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. One should not think that a person is a** ***sannyāsī*** **if he gives up** ***karmas*** **like** ***agni-hotra*** **(*****niragniḥ*****), or that he is an** ***aṣṭāṅga-yogī*** **if he has no bodily activities and closes his eyes. But he who performs all necessary karmas while giving up results of** ***karmas*** **can be called a** ***sannyāsī*** **and** ***yogī*****.**

### Text 2

*yaṁ sannyāsam iti prāhur yogaṁ taṁ viddhi pāṇḍava \|*  
*na hy asannyasta-saṅkalpo yogī bhavati kaścana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. O son of Pāṇḍu! What is called** ***sannyāsa*** **is also called** ***yoga*****. If the** ***jīva*** **does not give up determination to enjoy, he can never be called a** ***yogī*****.**

**Previously the way I showed you that** ***sāṅkhya*** **and** ***karma-yoga*** **were one, now in that way I will show that** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*** **and** ***karma-yoga*** **are one. Actually,** ***sāṅkhya*****\[*****-yoga*****\]*****, karma-yoga*** **and** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*** **are not different. Fools alone think they are different processes.**

### Text 3

*ārurukṣor muner yogaṁ karma kāraṇam ucyate \|*  
*yogārūḍhasya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇam ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3.** ***Yoga*** **is a special ladder. From the lowest stage in a** ***jīva’s*** **life, i.e. from a state of being absorbed in material objects and being like matter, till the state of pure consciousness there is one ladder. Each portion of the ladder has a particular name. But the all-inclusive name of the ladder is** ***yoga*****. The** ***yoga*** **ladder has two gross divisions.**

**{All the** ***yogārurukṣu*** **sages i.e. those who just begin the activities to ascend, they have** ***karma*** **as the cause or characteristic. The** ***yogārūḍha*** **persons have** ***śama*** **or peace as their cause or characteristic. These two gross divisions are called** ***karma*** **and peace.}**

**\[For all the** ***yogārurukṣu*** **sages i.e. those who just begin the activities to ascend, peforming** ***karmas*** **is the means. For the** ***yogārūḍha*** **persons,** ***śama*** **or giving up agitating** ***karmas*** **is the means.\]**

### Text 4

*yadā hi nendriyārtheṣu na karmaṣv anuṣajjate \|*  
*sarva-saṅkalpa-sannyāsī yogārūḍhas tadocyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**{4. When a** ***yogī*** **has no attachment to all the sense objects and to** ***karmas*****, and when he practices complete renunciation of all desires, then he is called** ***yogārūḍha.*****}**

**\[4. When a** ***yogī*** **has no attachment to the sense objects or to all the** ***karmas,*** **and when he completely practices renounciation of all desires, he is called** ***yogārūḍha.*****\]**

### Text 5

*uddhared ātmanātmānaṁ nātmānam avasādayet \|*  
*ātmaiva hy ātmano bandhur ātmaiva ripur ātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. One should deliver the** ***ātmā*****, i.e. the** ***jīva*** **who has fallen in the well of** ***saṁsāra*****, by the mind devoid of material attachment. One should not degrade the** ***ātmā*** **by desires for** ***saṁsāra*****. The mind is in different situations the friend of the** ***jīva*** **and its enemy as well.**

### Text 6

*bandhur ātmātmanas tasya yenātmaivātmanā jitaḥ \|*  
*anātmanas tu śatrutve vartetātmaiva śatru-vat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. The mind is a friend of that** ***jīva*** **which has conquered the mind. The mind is an enemy of that person who has not controlled the mind.**

### Text 7

*jitātmanaḥ praśāntasya paramātmā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*śītoṣṇa-sukha-duḥkheṣu tathā mānāpamānayoḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**{7. The** ***yogārūḍha*** **person has these characteristics: he has conquered the mind, is devoid of all attachments, is situated in** ***samādhi*** **and is undisturbed in heat and cold, happiness and distress, and honor and dishonor.}**

**\[7. The** ***yogārūḍha*** **person has these characteristics: his mind being unaffected by heat and cold, happiness and distress, and honor and dishonor, his** ***ātmā*** **is extremely steadfast.\]**

### Text 8

*jñāna-vijñāna-tṛptātmā kūṭa-stho vijitendriyaḥ \|*  
*yukta ity ucyate yogī sama-loṣṭrāśma-kāñcanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. {He}/\[The** ***yogī*** **endowed with conclusive knowledge\] is satisfied by scriptural teachings taught and by** ***vijñāna*** **or realization, \[in other words realization of separate** ***ātmā*****,\] is fixed in his spiritual nature, is completely in control of the senses, and considers a lump of earth, stone and gold as transformations of matter— endowed with such conclusions, \[he is called** ***yukta*****.\]**

### Text 9

*suhṛn-mitrāry-udāsīna-madhyastha-dveṣya-bandhuṣu \|*  
*sādhuṣv api ca pāpeṣu sama-buddhir viśiṣyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. He who sees the natural well-wisher, the friend, the enemy, the neutral person, the mediator, the hateful person, the relative, the pious person and sinner as the same has attained excellence.**

### Text 10

*yogī yuñjīta satatam ātmānaṁ rahasi sthitaḥ \|*  
*ekākī yata-cittātmā nirāśīr aparigrahaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**{10. The** ***yogārūḍha*** **person, controlling his mind and body with detachment and lack of possessions, gradually spending more time alone, should aborb his mind in** ***samādhi*****.}**

**\[10. The** ***yogārūḍha*** **person, always situated in a lonely place, should absorb his mind in** ***samādhi*****. Whatever activities he performs to maintain the body, he rejects temporary acquisitions in them and becomes devoid of desire for results.\]**

### Texts 11-12

*śucau deśe pratiṣṭhāpya sthiram āsanam ātmanaḥ \|*  
*nāty-ucchritaṁ nāti-nīcaṁ cailājina-kuśottaram \|\|*  
*tatraikāgraṁ manaḥ kṛtvā yata-cittendriya-kriyaḥ \|*  
*upaviśyāsane yuñjyād yogam ātma-viśuddhaye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11-12. The necessary rules for yoga practice are as follows.**

**Establishing his own** ***kuśa āsana*****, not too high and not too low, in a clean place, covering it with** ***kuśa*** **grass, then deer skin, and a cloth on top, he should sit on the** ***āsana*****, placing it on a clean ground.**

**Sitting there, controlling the mind, senses and activities, concentrating the mind for purifying the heart, he should practice** ***yoga*****.**

### Texts 13-14

*samaṁ kāya-śiro-grīvaṁ dhārayann acalaṁ sthiraḥ \|*  
*saṁprekṣya nāsikāgraṁ svaṁ diśaś cānavalokayan \|\|*  
*praśāntātmā vigata-bhīr brahmacāri-vrate sthitaḥ \|*  
*manaḥ saṁyamya mac-citto yukta āsīta mat-paraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13-14. Keeping the body, head and neck straight, motionless and firm, gazing at the tip of the nose without looking in other directions, with peaceful mind, fearlessness, and celibacy, withdrawing his mind from sense objects, meditating on me, the four-armed Viṣṇu form, surrendering to Paramātmā, one should practice** ***yoga*****.**

### Text 15

*yuñjann evaṁ sadātmānaṁ yogī niyata-mānasaḥ \|*  
*śāntiṁ nirvāṇa-paramāṁ mat-saṁsthām adhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Practicing** ***yoga*** **in this way, the** ***yogī*** **stops the mental functions acting in relation to matter. If there is no lack of absorption in** ***bhakti,*** **{gradually} the** ***yogī*** **attains supreme peace of** ***nirvāṇa*** **situated in me i.e. liberation from matter and the spiritual nature.**

### Text 16

*nāty-aśnatas tu yogo ’sti na caikāntam anaśnataḥ \|*  
*na cāti-svapna-śīlasya jāgrato naiva cārjuna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16.** ***Yoga*** **is not possible for one who eats too much, or who does not eat, for one who is fond of sleep, or does not sleep, O Arjuna.**

### Text 17

*yuktāhāra-vihārasya yukta-ceṣṭasya karmasu \|*  
*yukta-svapnāvabodhasya yogo bhavati duḥkha-hā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. If one is regulated in eating, regulated in recreation, regulated in all activities, regulated in sleep and regulated in waking, his** ***yoga*** **destroys all suffering by the sequence of activities.**

### Text 18

*yadā viniyataṁ cittam ātmany evāvatiṣṭhate \|*  
*niḥspṛhaḥ sarva-kāmebhyo yukta ity ucyate tadā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. When functions of consciousness are restrained, when consciousness gives up absorption in matter and becomes fixed completely in spiritual objects or** ***ātmā-tattva,*** **then the person devoid of material** ***kāma*** **becomes absorbed in** ***yoga*****.**

### Text 19

*yathā dīpo nivāta-stho neṅgate sopamā smṛtā \|*  
*yogino yata-cittasya yuñjato yogam ātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. The consciousness of the** ***yogī*****, having controlled consciousness, is similar to a flame which does not move, being placed in a windless place.**

### Texts 20-25

*yatroparamate cittaṁ niruddhaṁ yoga-sevayā \|*  
*yatra caivātmanātmānaṁ paśyann ātmani tuṣyati \|\|*  
*sukham ātyantikaṁ yat tad buddhi-grāhyam atīndriyam \|*  
*vetti yatra na caivāyaṁ sthitaś calati tattvataḥ \|\|*  
*yaṁ labdhvā cāparaṁ lābhaṁ manyate nādhikaṁ tataḥ \|*  
*yasmin sthito na duḥkhena guruṇāpi vicālyate \|\|*  
*taṁ vidyād duḥkha-saṁyoga-viyogaṁ yoga-saṁjñitam \|*  
*sa niścayena yoktavyo yogo ’nirviṇṇa-cetasā \|\|*  
*saṅkalpa-prabhavān kāmāṁs tyaktvā sarvān aśeṣataḥ \|*  
*manasaivendriya-grāmaṁ viniyamya samantataḥ \|\|*  
*śanaiḥ śanair uparamed buddhyā dhṛti-gṛhītayā \|*  
*ātma-saṁsthaṁ manaḥ kṛtvā na kiñcid api cintayet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20-25. By such practice of yoga, the consciousness is withdrawn from material objects, becoming indifferent to those objects. Then one becomes situated in samādhi . In that state, seeing Paramātmā by the antaḥkaraṇa which has the form of Paramātmā, one experiences happiness arising from that.**

**Комментарий:**

The *darśana* scripture written by sage Patañjali is a scripture concerning pure *aṣṭāṅga-yoga.*

Not understanding its real meaning, the commentators explain as follows. “The followers of *Vedānta* say that *ātmā* in his nature of knowledge and bliss is liberation. This is illogical because if one is aware of bliss in the state of oneness, there will be destruction of oneness by duality in the form of knowledge and an object of knowledge.”

But Patañjali does not say this. He simply says in the scripture he has written:

*puruṣārtha-śūnyānāṁ pratiprasavaḥ*  
*kaivalya-svarūpa-pratiṣṭhā vā citi-śaktir iti*

Liberation is extinction of the guṇas in the form of the human goals, or it is consciousness established in itself.

When all the *guṇas* are devoid of the *puruṣārthas* of *dharma, artha, kāma* and *mokṣa,* they do not create momentary transformations. Then *kaivalya* or oneness of spiritual qualities manifests. By that there is establishment (*pratiṣṭhā*) of the *svarūpa* of *jīva*. That is called *citi-śakti*. If one looks deeply, Patañjali does not accept destructions of *ātmā’s* qualities in the final state. He only accepts an unchanging state of all the qualities. One should understand that *citi-śakti* means spiritual qualities. When the unchanging nature is established, the qualities of the *svarūpa* manifest. The condition of *ātmā* in combination with *prakṛti* is called *ātma-guṇa-vikāra*. He does not teach that when that disappears, *ātma-śakti, ātma-guṇa,* or the *dharma* of *ātmā* which is bliss also disappears. \[In the stage of liberation which is described,\] bliss devoid of material transformations is awakened. This bliss is the *svarūpa* of happiness. That is final result of *yoga*. It will be shown later that this is called *bhakti*.

There are two types of *samādhi: samprajñāta* and *asamprajñāta*. *Samprajñāta-samādhi* has many varieties such as *savitarka, savicāra*, etc. *Asamprajñāta-samādhi* has only one type. In this *asamprajñāta-samādhi* there is attainment of intense happiness devoid of contact with material senses, perceived by the intelligence in the form of *ātmā*. The consciousness of the *yogī* situated in pure happiness of *ātmā* does not divert from this *tattva* any longer. If the *jīva* cannot attain this state, the *jīva* has no auspiciousness in *aṣṭāṅga-yoga,* for when he becomes attracted to whatever intermediate results in the form of powers, he deviates from the happiness of *samādhi* which is the final goal of the *yogī’s* heart. There is fear of inauspiciousness in practicing *yoga* from these obstacles. But there is no fear like this in *bhakti-yoga.* This will later be explained.

The *yogī* does not think there is any happiness superior to the happiness attained in *samādhi*. Thus, while maintaining his body, all the temporary happiness that arises by contact of the senses with sense objects is accepted only for maintaining the body by considering that as insignificant. Tolerating all the great suffering such as accidents, pain, lack and death he enjoys the happiness of *samādhi* which he seeks. Afflicted by all the suffering he does not give up the highest happiness. He practices *yoga* with the firm determination that all the suffering that has come will not last very long and that it will quickly disappear.

He does not give up the practice of *yoga* with useless regret that there is delay in attaining the result of *yoga* because of some obstacle. In other words, he has great determination till attaining the result of *yoga*.

The first action related to *yoga* is that all the senses should be completely controlled by *yama, niyama, āsana, prāṇāyāma,* and by the mind after having removed completely the desires born from determination to attain results. *Pratyāhāra* is teaching detachment gradually by intelligence attained from an *aṅga* in the form of *dhāraṇā*.
