Controlling the mind completely by *dhyāna, dhāraṇā* and *pratyāhāra,* he will attain *ātma-samādhi.* He will not think of material sense objects any longer and, though thinking of sense objects for maintaining the body, he will not become attached to them. This has been taught. This is the inner action of *yoga*.

### Text 26

*yato yato niścarati manaś cañcalam asthiram \|*  
*tatas tato niyamyaitad ātmany eva vaśaṁ nayet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. The mind is by nature unsteady and fickle. Though it wanders here and there, one should restrain it with care and bring it under control of** ***ātmā*****.**

### Text 27

*praśānta-manasaṁ hy enaṁ yoginaṁ sukham uttamam \|*  
*upaiti śānta-rajasaṁ brahma-bhūtam akalmaṣam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. That** ***yogī*** **who has a pacified mind after destroying obstacles by this practice, who is devoid of passion, free of sin, and thus on the level of realization of Brahman, attains the highest happiness mentioned.**

*yuñjann evaṁ sadātmānaṁ yogī vigata-kalmaṣaḥ \|*  
*sukhena brahma-saṁsparśam atyantaṁ sukham aśnute \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. Thus constantly controlling his mind, the** ***yogī*****, free of all sin, easily experiences the intense bliss by contact with Brahman. He attains bliss in the form of cultivation of Para-brahman, whose** ***svarūpa*** **is** ***cit*****. This is** ***bhakti*****.**

### Text 29

*sarva-bhūta-stham ātmānaṁ sarva-bhūtāni cātmani \|*  
*īkṣate yoga-yuktātmā sarvatra sama-darśanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. I will in brief describe the nature of this happiness arising from contact with Brahman. The** ***yogī*** **who has attained** ***samādhi*** **has two types of dealings:** ***bhāva*** **(affection) and** ***kriyā*** **(action). His dealings in** ***bhāva*** **are as follows. He sees** ***ātmā*** **in all beings and sees all beings in** ***ātmā*****. His dealings in** ***kriyā*** **is seeing equally everywhere. In the next two verses I will explain** ***bhāva*** **and in one verse will explain** ***kriyā*****.**

### Text 30

*yo māṁ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṁ ca mayi paśyati \|*  
*tasyāhaṁ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. I belong to that person who sees me everywhere and sees everything in me. In other words, he surpasses** ***śānta-rati*** **and attains** ***prema*** **with a relationship between us of “I am his and he is mine.” When this relationship arises, I will not give to him destruction of everything in the form of dry** ***nirvāṇa*** **arising from not seeing me. Since he is my servant he will not be destroyed.**

### Text 31

*sarva-bhūta-sthitaṁ yo māṁ bhajaty ekatvam āsthitaḥ \|*  
*sarvathā vartamāno ’pi sa yogī mayi vartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. The meditation on the four-armed form of the Lord \[residing in everyone’s heart\] which was taught to the yogī for the duration of sādhana, by that, when he becomes devoid of thinking in terms of duality {inherent in the sādhana and at the time of perfection of the supreme tattva,} during samādhi in a state of nirvikalpa, the notion of oneness with the form of Śyāmasundara arises. The yogī who worships me situated in all beings, i.e. who performs bhakti by hearing and chanting, he, though performing karma at the time of duty, jñāna at the time of deliberation and samādhi at the time of yoga, is present in me, \[i.e. he attains liberation in the form of nearness to Kṛṣṇa.\]**

**Комментарий:**

In *Nārada-pañcarātra* it is said at the time of teaching *yoga*:

*dik-kālādy-anavacchinne kṛṣṇe ceto vidhāya ca*  
*tan-mayo bhavati kṣipraṁ jīvo brahmaṇi yojayet*

Placing the consciousness in Kṛṣṇa, who is continuous in space and time, absorbed in him, the *jīva* should quickly develop happiness on contact with Kṛṣṇa, Para-brahman.

*Bhakti* to Kṛṣṇa is the final result of *samādhi* in *yoga*.

### Text 32

*ātmaupamyena sarvatra samaṁ paśyati yo ’rjuna \|*  
*sukhaṁ vā yadi vā duḥkhaṁ sa yogī paramo mataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. I will speak of the nature of the** ***yogī’s*** **handling of** ***kriyā*****. Please listen. The highest** ***yogī*** **sees everything equally. Seeing equally means that he considers all** ***jīvas*** **as himself in dealing with them. He considers other** ***jīvas’*** **happiness as his own happiness and knows other** ***jīvas’*** **suffering as his own suffering. He desires happiness for all** ***jīvas*** **and accordingly carries out activities. That is called seeing equally.**

### Text 33

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*yo ’yaṁ yogas tvayā proktaḥ sāmyena madhusūdana \|*

**33. Arjuna said: O Madhusūdana! I cannot understand how this** ***yoga*** **that you have taught can be kept steady with equal intelligence. {Particularly, it is possible to maintain equal intelligence towards enemy and friend only for a few days. I cannot understand how to undertake this** ***yoga*** **endowed with such attitudes.}**

### Text 34

*cañcalaṁ hi manaḥ kṛṣṇa pramāthi balavad dṛḍham \|*  
*tasyāhaṁ nigrahaṁ manye vāyor iva su-duṣkaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. O Kṛṣṇa! You said that one should control the fickle mind by discriminating intelligence. But I see that the mind can agitate the discriminating intelligence intensely. Thus it seems that I cannot control the fickle mind, which is like the wind.**

**\[Particularly it is possible to maintain equal intelligence towards enemy and friend for a few days. I cannot understand how to perform this** ***yoga*** **with this mentality.\]**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*asaṁśayaṁ mahā-bāho mano durṇigrahaṁ calam \|*  
*abhyāsena tu kaunteya vairāgyeṇa ca gṛhyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. The Lord said: O mighty-armed! What you say is true. But the** ***yoga*** **scriptures particularly teach that it can be gradually controlled by practice \[of tasting the bliss of** ***ātmā*****\] and detachment \[from material objects\].**

### Text 36

*asaṁyatātmanā yogo duṣprāpa iti me matiḥ \|*  
*vaśyātmanā tu yatatā śakyo ’vāptum upāyataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36. I teach that one who does not try to control the** ***ātmā*** **or mind by practice and detachment, he cannot attain perfection of** ***yoga*** **described previously. But he who controls the mind by taking support of a proper process will definitely {perfect** ***yoga*****}/\[become succesful\].**

**Concerning proper method this alone should be said: he who practices intently concentrating the mind regulated by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **offered to the Lord and by meditation on me as its** ***aṅga,*** **and simultaneously accepts sense objects for maintaining the body, along with detachment, will gradually {perfect** ***yoga*****}/\[control the mind\].**

### Text 37

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*ayatiḥ śraddhayopeto yogāc calita-mānasaḥ \|*  
*aprāpya yoga-saṁsiddhiṁ kāṁ gatiṁ kṛṣṇa gacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**37. Hearing this, Arjuna said: O Kṛṣṇa! You said that by careful practice and detachment one can perfect** ***yoga*****.**

**But many persons, having faith in** ***yoga*** **teachings, progress a little but cannot become** ***sannyāsīs*****. Making a little attempt, the minds of all these persons deviate from** ***yoga*****, and become attracted to sense objects because of lack of practice and detachment. What is the destination of such people?**

### Text 38

*kaccin nobhaya-vibhraṣṭaś chinnābhram iva naśyati \|*  
*apratiṣṭho mahā-bāho vimūḍho brahmaṇaḥ pathi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. Without giving up** ***sakāma-karma,*** **one cannot attempt** ***yoga*****.** ***Sakāma-karma*** **is best for foolish people because by that they attain happiness in this life and Svarga in the next. The** ***jīva*** **which is inclined to** ***yoga*** **is far from** ***sakāma-karma*** **but cannot attain perfection in** ***yoga*** **because of reasons previously stated.**

**Thus he deviates from the path of attaining Brahman. Falling from both paths, will he be completely destroyed like a broken cloud?**

### Text 39

*etan me saṁśayaṁ kṛṣṇa chettum arhasy aśeṣataḥ \|*  
*tvad-anyaḥ saṁśayasyāsya chettā na hy upapadyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. Writers of scripture are not omniscient, but you, the supreme Lord are omniscient. Except for you no one can destroy this doubt. Thus, by mercy, destroy my doubt completely.**

### Text 40

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*pārtha naiveha nāmutra vināśas tasya vidyate \|*  
*na hi kalyāṇa-kṛt kaścid durgatiṁ tāta gacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. The Lord said: O Pārtha! The practitioner of yoga does not meet destruction in this life \[i.e. in the material world\] or the next \[i.e. in the spiritual world\]. One who has done the auspicious work of yoga does not meet an unfortunate end.**

**Комментарий:**

The essential point is that humans are divided into two groups: followers of rules (*vidhi*) and non-followers of rules. The conduct of all those who just satisfy their senses, and are not controlled by any rules, are without rules like animals. Whether civilized or uncivilized, whether a fool or scholar, whether weak or strong — the conduct of non-follower of rules is always equivalent to that of animals. By their actions they have no possibility of attaining auspiciousness.

Human followers of rules can be divided into three groups: *karmīs, jñānīs* and devotees. The *karmīs* are divided into *sakāma-karmīs* and *niṣkāma-karmīs*. The *sakāma-karmīs* seek out very temporary happiness i.e. desire impermanent happiness. They attain Svarga and progress in the world, but that happiness is all temporary. Thus they never attain what is auspicious for the *jīva*. The attainment of eternal bliss after release from matter is the *jīva’s* auspiciousness. In whatever section (of scriptures) that attainment of eternal bliss is not explained, such sections are meaningless.

When there is a goal of attaining bliss in *karma-kāṇḍa* it is called *karma-yoga*. By that *karma-yoga* the heart becomes purified. After that one attains *jñāna,* and then *dhyāna-yoga* and finally *bhakti-yoga*.

When one accepts suffering while giving up personal happiness in *sakāma-karma,* the *karmī* is called a *tapasvī*, performer of austerities. But all that austerity is no more than happiness of the senses and nothing else. By austerity the demons enjoy their senses after attaining the result of their austerity.

On surpassing the limit of sense satisfaction, the *jīva* easily attains *karma-yoga* which aims at auspiciousness. The *dhyāna-yogī* situated in *karma-yoga* or the *jñāna-yogī* have superior auspiciousness. All the states that the *jīva* attains by *sakāma-karma* are attained in a better way by the *aṣṭāṅga-yogī*.

### Text 41

*prāpya puṇya-kṛtāṁ lokān uṣitvā śāśvatīḥ samāḥ \|*  
*śucīnāṁ śrīmatāṁ gehe yoga-bhraṣṭo ’bhijāyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41. Those who fall from** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*** **are divided into two groups: those who fall after practicing a short time and those who fall after practicing a long time.**

**Those who fall after short practice live a long time in Svarga, etc., which is attained by those performing** ***sakāma puṇyas*** **and then take birth in a house of a** ***brāhmaṇa*** **following proper conduct or in a house of wealthy merchants.**

### Text 42

*atha vā yoginām eva kule bhavati dhīmatām \|*  
*etad dhi durlabhataraṁ loke janma yad īdṛśam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**42. Or if he has practiced a long time, he will be born in the family of** ***jñāna-yogīs*****. Know that a birth in such a pure family is more difficult to obtain, since, being born there, the** ***jīva*** **from the beginning easily progresses further due to superior association.**

### Text 43

*tatra taṁ buddhi-saṁyogaṁ labhate paurva-dehikam \|*  
*yatate ca tato bhūyaḥ saṁsiddhau kuru-nandana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**43. O son of the Kurus! Born in that situation, he attains the intelligence of his previous body. Thus by natural taste, he makes efforts again for perfecting** ***yoga*****.**

### Text 44

*pūrvābhyāsena tenaiva hriyate hy avaśo ’pi saḥ \|*  
*jijñāsur api yogasya śabda-brahmātivartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**44. By his nature, due to previous practice, the person, inquisitive about** ***yoga*** **scriptures, surpasses the path of** ***sakāma-karma*****, i.e. he attains a result superior to the results indicated on the path of** ***sakāma-karma*****.**

### Text 45

*prayatnād yatamānas tu yogī saṁśuddha-kilbiṣaḥ \|*  
*aneka-janma-saṁsiddhas tato yāti parāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**45. That** ***yogī*****, practicing with full effort, matures his** ***yoga*** **and becoming purified of all faults. Becoming free of sin finally after practicing for many births, the** ***yogī*** **attains liberation in the form of the highest goal. This is the** ***yogī’s*** **result beyond this world.**

### Text 46

*tapasvibhyo ’dhiko yogī jñānibhyo ’pi mato ’dhikaḥ \|*  
*karmibhyaś cādhiko yogī tasmād yogī bhavārjuna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**46. On examining nicely, it is seen that greater than the** ***tapasvī*** **with** ***sakāma-karma*** **is the** ***karma-yogī*****. Better than the {*****jñāna-yogī*****}/\[*****sāṅkhya-jñānī*****\] is the** ***yogī*****. Better than the** ***sakāma-karmī*** **is the** ***yogī*****.** ***Tapasya, jñāna*** **and** ***karma*** **are useless without** ***yoga*****. Therefore O Arjuna! Be a** ***yogī*****.**

### Text 47

*yoginām api sarveṣāṁ mad-gatenāntar-ātmanā \|*  
*śraddhāvān bhajate yo māṁ sa me yuktatamo mataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**47. The yogī who practices bhakti-yoga is superior to all yogīs . The person who worships me with faith is superior {to}/\[among\] all yogīs.**

**Комментарий:**

Among humans following rules, the *sakāma-karmīs* are not called *yogīs*. *Niṣkāma-karmīs, jñānīs, aṣṭāṅga-yogīs* and practitioners of *bhakti-yoga* are all *yogīs*. Actually, *yoga* is one only and not two. *Yoga* is a special path with steps.

Taking shelter of this path, the *jīva* treads on the path towards Brahman. The first step is *niṣkāma-karma-yoga*. Combining this with *jñāna* and *vairāgya* (detachment), it becomes *jñāna-yoga* as a second step. When this is combined with meditation on *īśvara*, it becomes the third step in the form of *aṣṭāṅga-yoga*. When this is combined with affection for the Lord, it become the fourth step, *bhakti-yoga*. Combining all these steps it becomes a huge staircase. It is called *yoga*.

In clearly explaining this *yoga,* all the divisions of *yoga* have been described. Those who aim for eternal auspiciousness take support of *yoga* alone. But progressing through each step, after first attaining steadiness in the step, finally one gives up that step. One must give it up in order to progress to the next higher step. \[The person who stops at one particular step, his *yoga* is not complete. Thus\] whatever step one becomes fixed in, that partial form of *yoga* with a particular label is his support. Thus, some are known as *karma-yogīs, jñāna-yogīs, aṣṭāṅga-yogīs* and *bhakti-yogīs*.

O Pārtha! He who performs *bhakti* to me as the final goal is better than the other three types of *yogīs*. Be this type of *yogī,* the *bhakti-yogī*.

In the sixth chapter, the final part of *niṣkāma-karma-yoga* is discussed. At the time of ascending in *niṣkāma-karma-yoga,* this *yoga* is predominated by *karma*. Having ascended, it produces a result in the form of *samādhi* on the supreme *tattva* by *aṣṭāṅga-yoga* on the path of *jñāna*, which has the form of seeing *ātmā*. Accepting material objects in a *yukta* way, gradually increasing meditation on Paramātmā, the mind withdraws from those objects. Rejecting the secondary results of *svarūpa-siddhi*, and yogic powers, one manifests spiritual happiness in the form of contact with Brahman. This is the final result of *niṣkāma-karma-yoga*. Those who fall before this *yoga* becomes complete, i.e. those who become attracted to other objects, or those who die, attain the described results of *yoga* over many births. Their previous acts are not in vain.

Thus, austerity on the path of *sakāma, sāṅkhya-jñāna* in the form of scriptural knowledge of twenty-four tattvas, and *sakāma-karma* are all insignificant. When one binds up these three tendencies by the chains of desire to see *ātmā*, one develops *niṣkāma-karma-yoga* devoid of desire for these insignificant results. This *yoga* is superior to those methods. This *yoga*, in different situations, takes three different forms. At the state of desiring elevation, it is *karma-yoga*. At the state of having attained elevation, it is *jñāna-yoga*. At its final stage, it is *bhakti-yoga*. In the chapter’s last verse, one other type of *bhakti-yoga* is described.

tāvat karmāṇi kurvīta na nirvidyeta yāvatā  
mat-kathā-śravaṇādau vā śraddhā yāvan na jāyate

One should perform one’s daily and periodic rites (nitya-naimittika karmas ) as long as one does not become detached from them or has not awakened his faith in hearing topics about me. (SB [11.20.9](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.9))

According to this statement from the Eleventh Canto of *Bhāgavatam*, it is determined that when a person develops faith in the topics of the Lord, a second type of *bhakti-yoga* arises. While doing karma when one becomes indifferent to results, the first type of *bhakti-yoga* appears. The second type of bhakti-yoga is superior. The first type of *bhakti-yoga* is called *bhakti-yoga* arising from indifference to matter and the second type of *bhakti-yoga* is called *bhakti-yoga* rising from faith. When that arises both types of *bhakti-yoga* take one form. *Bhakti-yoga* rising from faith is natural for the *jīva*. This will be explained in the middle six chapters.

## Chapter Seven - Vijñāna-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*mayy āsakta-manāḥ pārtha yogaṁ yuñjan mad-āśrayaḥ \|*  
*asaṁśayaṁ samagraṁ māṁ yathā jñāsyasi tac chṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: O Pārtha! In the first six chapters I spoke of** ***jñāna*** **and** ***yoga*** **the means of liberation, which depend on** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **for purifying the** ***antaḥkaraṇa*****. In the second six chapters I will speak about** ***bhakti-yoga*****. When you practice** ***yoga*** **which takes shelter of me, attaching your heart to me, you will attain complete knowledge related to me. {There is no doubt about this.}/\[Please listen as I speak that.\]** ***Brahma-jñāna*** **is not complete knowledge since it is not knowledge with details.**

**Brahman, my manifestation without qualities, the object of impersonal contemplation, manifests when one meditates on the impersonal devoid of qualities while giving up material qualities. It is not** ***nirguṇa*** **since it is merely** ***sāttvika-jñāna*** **beyond the body.** ***Bhakti*** **is a special function of the** ***nirguṇa*****. If one takes shelter of it, the** ***jīva*** **with** ***nirguṇa*** **eyes perceives my** ***nirguṇa svarūpa*****.**

### Text 2

*jñānaṁ te ’haṁ sa-vijñānam idaṁ vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ \|*  
*yaj jñātvā neha bhūyo ’nyaj jñātavyam avaśiṣyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. \[All the knowledge of me gained by the devotee previous to being attached to me is concerned with my powers. That is called** ***jñāna*****. After developing attachment to me, I teach to you completely** ***jñāna*** **with** ***vijñāna*****.\]**

**{All the knowledge concerning my** ***svarūpa*** **endowed with** ***cit*** **and** ***acit śaktis*** **is called** ***jñāna*****. Knowledge concerning details of my** ***svarūpa,*** **apart from these two** ***śaktis*** **is called** ***vijñāna*****. I teach to you completely** ***jñāna*** **with** ***vijñāna*****.}**

**Please listen. Knowing this, you have nothing else to know.**

### Text 3

*manuṣyāṇāṁ sahasreṣu kaścid yatati siddhaye \|*  
*yatatām api siddhānāṁ kaścin māṁ vetti tattvataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. The** ***jñānīs*** **and** ***yogīs*** **mentioned in the previous six chapters can easily attain** ***brahma-jñāna*** **by meditation. But knowledge of Bhagavān which is different from this object of meditation is rare for them.**

**Among uncountable** ***jīvas,*** **sometimes someone may be a human. Among thousands of humans someone makes an effort to attain auspiciousness. Among thousands of those who attain that auspiciousness, some understand in truth my** ***svarūpa*** **as Bhagavān.**

### Text 4

*bhūmir āpo ’nalo vāyuḥ khaṁ mano buddhir eva ca \|*  
*ahaṅkāra itīyaṁ me bhinnā prakṛtir aṣṭadhā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. Knowledge of the Lord’s powers and his** ***svarūpa*** **is called** ***bhagavat-jñāna*****. It may be described as follows. I am always a special** ***tattva*** **endowed with** ***śakti*** **and having a** ***svarūpa*****.**

**Brahman is a state devoid of qualities manifesting through my** ***śakti*****. It does not have a** ***svarūpa*****. It is a relational condition which arises on thinking of what is opposite to the created universe.**

**Paramātmā also is my special manifestation {though my** ***śakti*****} within the material world \[as my** ***aṁśa*****\]. As a result, Paramātmā is a special** ***tattva*** **related to the temporary universe and does not have “eternal”** ***svarūpa*****.**

**My** ***svarūpa*** **as Bhagavān is eternal. In that, I have two** ***śaktis*****. One is called** ***bahiraṅgā*** **or** ***māyā-śakti*****. It is called the** ***aparā śakti,*** **the mother of the universe. You will see enumeration of \[eight\]** ***tattvas*** **in this** ***aparā śakti*** **related to the material universe. By earth, water, fire, air and ether – the five** ***mahā-bhūtas*** **and sound, touch, form, taste and fragrance, i.e the five** ***tan-mātras*** **– these ten** ***tattvas*** **are to be understood.**

**{By** ***ahaṅkāra-tattva,*** **all the senses which are its effects and** ***mahat-tattva*** **its cause are to be understood. Intelligence and mind are listed separately. Among all the** ***tattvas,*** **among the various effects they are the chief. In truth, the two are one** ***tattva*****.}**

**\[The word** ***ahaṅkāra*** **refers to** ***ahaṅkāra*** **and the eleven senses that arise from it. The word** ***buddhi*** **refers to** ***mahat-tattva*****. The word** ***manaḥ*** **refers to** ***pradhāna*****. There are the twenty-four** ***tattvas*****.\]**

**All these arise from my** ***bahiraṅga-śakti*****.**

### Text 5

*apareyam itas tv anyāṁ prakṛtiṁ viddhi me parām \|*  
*jīva-bhūtāṁ mahā-bāho yayedaṁ dhāryate jagat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. Besides this, there is my** ***taṭastha prakṛti*** **which is called** ***parā prakṛti*****. This** ***prakṛti*** **has a conscious** ***svarūpa*** **and consists of** ***jīvas*****. From this** ***śakti*** **emanate all the** ***jīvas,*** **{who give consciousness to the material universe}/\[who accept the material world as the object of enjoyment\]. The spiritual world emanates from my** ***antaraṅga-śakti*****. The material universe arises from my** ***bahiraṅga-śakti.*** **The** ***jīva-śakti*** **is called** ***taṭastha,*** **as it is suitable for both worlds.**

### Text 6

*etad-yonīni bhūtāni sarvāṇīty upadhāraya \|*  
*ahaṁ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayas tathā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. The world of matter and** ***taṭastha*** **which is a combination of conscious and unconscious elements arises from these two** ***prakṛtis*****. Thus, I, the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān, am the root cause of creation and destruction of all universes.**

### Text 7

*mattaḥ parataraṁ nānyat kiñcid asti dhanañjaya \|*  
*mayi sarvam idaṁ protaṁ sūtre maṇi-gaṇā iva \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. O Dhanañjaya! There is no one superior to me. Just as pearls are strung on a thread, the whole universe is interwoven with me \[in the form of Viṣṇu\].**

### Text 8

*raso ’ham apsu kaunteya prabhāsmi śaśi-sūryayoḥ \|*  
*praṇavaḥ sarva-vedeṣu śabdaḥ khe pauruṣaṁ nṛṣu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. O son of Kuntī! I am the taste of water and the light of the sun and moon. I am** ***praṇava*** **in the** ***Vedas*****, sound in the ether and valor of men.**

### Text 9

*puṇyo gandhaḥ pṛthivyāṁ ca tejaś cāsmi vibhāvasau \|*  
*jīvanaṁ sarva-bhūteṣu tapaś cāsmi tapasviṣu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. I am the fragrance of the earth element, the heat in the sun, the life in all entities, and the austerity of the** ***tapasvīs.***

### Text 10

*bījaṁ māṁ sarva-bhūtānāṁ viddhi pārtha sanātanam \|*  
*buddhir buddhimatām asmi tejas tejasvinām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. I am the eternal, seed of all bodies, O son of Pṛthā. I am the intelligence of the intelligent people and the majesty of the majestic.**

### Text 11

*balaṁ balavatāṁ cāhaṁ kāma-rāga-vivarjitam \|*  
*dharmāviruddho bhūteṣu kāmo ’smi bharatarṣabha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. I am the strength of the strong which is devoid of lust and anger. I am that lust which is according to** ***dharma*** **i.e., association with one’s married woman to produce offspring, O best of the Bharata lineage.**

### Text 12

*ye caiva sāttvikā bhāvā rājasās tāmasāś ca ye \|*  
*matta eveti tān viddhi na tv ahaṁ teṣu te mayi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Know that all states in** ***sattva*****,** ***rajas*** **and** ***tamas*** **come from the effects of** ***guṇas*** **of my** ***prakṛti*****. I am independent of all** ***guṇas*****. They are all dependent on my** ***śakti*****.**

### Text 13

*tribhir guṇa-mayair bhāvair ebhiḥ sarvam idaṁ jagat \|*  
*mohitaṁ nābhijānāti mām ebhyaḥ param avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. My** ***aparā prakṛti*** **has three** ***guṇas: sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*****. By these three** ***guṇas*** **the whole world is bewildered. Because of this, the world cannot know me, \[Kṛṣṇa,\] the indestructible** ***svarūpa*** **independent of all the** ***guṇas*****.**

### Text 14

*daivī hy eṣā guṇa-mayī mama māyā duratyayā \|*  
*mām eva ye prapadyante māyām etāṁ taranti te \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. This māyā is my** ***śakti*****. Thus, it is naturally difficult to overcome for the weak** ***jīva*****. Those who surrender to my form as Bhagavān can cross over the ocean of māyā, \[i.e. one cannot overcome** ***māyā*** **by** ***karma*** **and** ***jñāna*** **or by surrender to** ***devatās*****\].**

### Text 15

*na māṁ duṣkṛtino mūḍhāḥ prapadyante narādhamāḥ \|*  
*māyayāpahṛta-jñānā āsuraṁ bhāvam āśritāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Taking shelter of demonic nature, the** ***duṣkṛta, mūḍha, narādhama*** **and person whose knowledge is covered by** ***māyā*** **do not surrender to me.**

**The first type, the** ***duṣkṛta*****, is a person living life completely beyond Vedic rules.**

**The moralistic but atheistic person is** ***mūḍha*** **(foolish) because he does not take shelter of me, who am the Lord of his morality.**

**The** ***narādhama*** **accepts me as an** ***aṅga*** **(limb) of morality, but not as the Lord of morality.**

**Those who are covered by** ***māyā*** **are those who worship Śiva, Brahmā and others but do not know that I possess** ***śaktis,*** **that the** ***jīva*** **has an eternal, conscious** ***svarūpa,*** **that the** ***jīva*** **has a temporary relational** ***svarūpa*** **with matter, and that the** ***jīva*** **has a relational** ***svarūpa*** **as my eternal servant, though they study scriptures like** ***Vedānta*****.**

**15. Four types of** ***duṣkṛta*** **—** ***mūḍha, narādhama*** **and person whose knowledge is covered by** ***māyā,*** **the** ***āsura-bhāva*** **— do not surrender to me, the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān.**

**The** ***mūḍha*** **is overwhelmed by sense objects and his mind is dulled by** ***karma*****. Not understanding the existence of a conscious entity, they are determined to increase material science.**

**The** ***narādhama*** **refers to people knowledgeable of material actions, proud of their knowledge and claiming imaginary gods. They are atheistic moralists devoid of inner elevation.**

**Those whose knowledge is stolen by illusion, though they accept a conscious entity, by being illusioned, they take shelter of wicked philosophies such as** ***kevalāvaita-vāda, śūnya-vāda*** **and** ***prakṛti-vāda*****. They do not accept that pure** ***bhakti*** **is eternal.**

**Those who take shelter of demonic nature (*****āsura-bhāva*****) are proud, self-centered, and controlled by their senses. They are mad after happiness in the world and consider devotees to be inferior. In summary, persons devoid of** ***sukṛti*** **in the form of devotee association at all times are called** ***duskṛtas*****.**

### Text 16

*catur-vidhā bhajante māṁ janāḥ sukṛtino ’rjuna \|*  
*ārto jijñāsur arthārthī jñānī ca bharatarṣabha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. \[When the** ***ārta, jijñāsu, arthārthī*** **and** ***jñānī,*** **become devoid of the faults of these mentalities by my mercy or my devotees’ mercy, and attain** ***sukṛti,*** **they worship me.\]**

**It is almost impossible for the** ***duṣkṛtas*** **to worship me since they have no method of elevation. By some sudden process sometimes someone among them attains my worship.**

**Among people with** ***sukṛti*** **following regulated life, four types of people are suitable for worshipping me. Those who indulge in** ***kāmya-karma,*** **becoming afflicted by suffering remember me. They are called** ***ārta*****. Sometimes** ***duṣkṛta*** **persons also remember me when in a situation of suffering.**

**When the previously mentioned** ***mūḍha*** **moralist, by inquiry about** ***tattva,*** **understands the necessity of the Lord, he eventually remembers me, in the form of inquiry (*****jijñāsuḥ*****).**

**When the previously mentioned** ***narādhama,*** **not satisfied with the Lord within morality, understands the Lord who controls morality, then he becomes a devotee following rules (*****vaidhi*****) and remembers me when he desires wealth (*****arthārthī*****).**

**When a person, understanding knowledge of Brahman and Paramātmā to be incomplete, takes shelter of pure knowledge of Bhagavān, he destroys the covering of** ***māyā*** **and surrenders to me as an eternal servant of the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān.**

**Thus, when the sufferer (*****ārta*****) rejects (somewhat) his material desire, when the inquisitive person (*****jijñāsuḥ*****) rejects the contamination of ordinary moralistic knowledge, when the desirer of wealth (*****arthārthī*****) rejects the contamination of desire to attain Svarga in afterlife and when the** ***jñānī*** **rejects the contamination of thinking that Bhagavān is temporary and the desire to merge in Brahman, the four types {of** ***jīvasi*****} become qualified for** ***bhakti*****. As long as some contamination remains, the** ***bhakti*** **of these persons is \[*****karma-*** **or** ***jñāna-*****\]*****pradhāna-bhūta bhakti: bhakti*** **predominating but mixed with other desires and processes. When the contamination ceases, they attain** ***kevala, akiñcanā*** **or** ***uttamā*** **(pure)** ***bhakti*****.**

### Text 17

*teṣāṁ jñānī nitya-yukta eka-bhaktir viśiṣyate \|*  
*priyo hi jñānino ’tyartham ahaṁ sa ca mama priyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. The** ***ārta, jijñāsu, artārthī*** **and** ***jñānī,*** **devoid of contamination, becoming devoted to me, become devotees. However, among them, the** ***jñānī,*** **giving up contamination of** ***jñāna,*** **attaining pure** ***jñāna,*** **engaging in** ***bhakti-yoga,*** **attains superiority over the other three types of devotees.**

**The purport of this is that, by nature, by practice of** ***jñāna,*** **the** ***svarūpa*** **of the conscious** ***jīva,*** **becomes pure to the extent that the** ***jīva*** **attains his** ***svarūpa*****. To this extent, the** ***karmīs,*** **even though purified of contamination of** ***karma,*** **do not become aspure as far as being situated in {their} own** ***svarūpa*****.**

**By association with devotees finally all become situated in their** ***svarūpas*****. Among the four types mentioned in the stage of** ***sādhana,*** **the** ***jñānī bhakta*** **endowed with pure devotion is my pure servant and I also hold him dear. Śukadeva and others are examples those who manifested** ***bhagavat-jñāna*****. The devotees who have attained pure** ***jñāna,*** **are purely spiritual servants of the Lord at the time of** ***sādhana*****. Any trace of materialism cannot enter them.**

### Text 18

*udārāḥ sarva evaite jñānī tv ātmaiva me matam \|*  
*āsthitaḥ sa hi yuktātmā mām evānuttamāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. Having accepted pure** ***bhakti,*** **the previously mentioned four types of qualified persons are all most generous. But the steadiness in {one’s}** ***ātmā*** **or steadiness in consciousness is stronger in the** ***jñāni-bhakta*****. He is situated in me, who am the highest goal, the shelter of consciousness. He is dearest to me and he controls me to the extreme.**

### Text 19

*bahūnāṁ janmanām ante jñānavān māṁ prapadyate \|*  
*vāsudevaḥ sarvam iti sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. All the** ***jīvas*** **after many births of** ***sādhana,*** **attain** ***jñāna,*** **or steadiness in consciousness. When they become steady in consciousness, to some degree they first take support of oneness of** ***jīva*** **and Brahman at the time of giving up matter. They then become indifferent to qualities, having disgust for material qualities. When they become situated slightly in the qualities of consciousness, they can understand the pure qualities of consciousness and become attracted to it. Attracted to it, they surrender to me, the highest form of consciousness. They consider that this material world is not independent, just one repulsive reflection of the consciousness entity. It has a relationship however with the Lord, Vāsudeva. Thus, everything is Vāsudeva-maya, filled with the Lord. Those who surrender to the Lord in this way are great souls and very rare.**

### Text 20

*kāmais tais tair hṛta-jñānāḥ prapadyante ’nya-devatāḥ \|*  
*taṁ taṁ niyamam āsthāya prakṛtyā niyatāḥ svayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. The** ***ārta, jijñāsu, arthārthī*** **and** ***jñānī*****, becoming free of their contamination, perform** ***bhakti*** **to me. Till the time that their contamination in the form of desire has not dissipated, they remain by nature** ***bahirmukha*****. Those who take shelter of my** ***svarūpa,*** **though being** ***kāmīs,*** **do not give shelter to a** ***bahirmukha*** **condition. In a short time, I will remove their desire.**

**But those who are opposed to me, and losing their knowledge by desire, worship** ***devatās,*** **givers of results of their desires, in order to attain quick results, they do not like me, a form of** ***viśuddha-sattva,*** **because, motivated by their individual modes of** ***tamas*** **and** ***rajas,*** **they worship various** ***devatās*****, following their respective, low rules.**

### Text 21

*yo yo yāṁ yāṁ tanuṁ bhaktaḥ śraddhayārcitum icchati\|*  
*tasya tasyācalāṁ śraddhāṁ tām eva vidadhāmy aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. As the** ***antaryāmī,*** **I give the person firm faith in those** ***devatās*** **whose forms he desires to worship with faith.**

### Text 22

*sa tayā śraddhayā yuktas tasyārādhanam īhate \|*  
*labhate ca tataḥ kāmān mayaiva vihitān hi tān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Endowed with the faith given by me, that person worships the** ***devatā*** **and attains from the** ***devatā*** **his desired objects, which are given only by me.**

### Text 23

*antavat tu phalaṁ teṣāṁ tad bhavaty alpa-medhasām \|*  
*devān deva-yajo yānti mad-bhaktā yānti mām api \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. The results obtained by these foolish worshippers of** ***devatās*** **are temporary since those who worship** ***devatās*** **attain the temporary** ***devatās*** **and finally are destroyed. Those who worship me, even with desires, attain me, the form of eternal results.**

### Text 24

*avyaktaṁ vyaktim āpannaṁ manyante mām abuddhayaḥ \|*  
*paraṁ bhāvam ajānanto mamāvyayam anuttamam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. Those who think that the impersonal aspect is supreme conclude that I have an invisible** ***svarūpa*** **devoid of qualities and manifest myself as some effect. Though they study many Vedānta scriptures, they are foolish, since they do not know my supreme, imperishable, most excellent** ***svarūpa*** **endowed with eternal qualities.**

### Text 25

*nāhaṁ prakāśaḥ sarvasya yoga-māyā-samāvṛtaḥ \|*  
*mūḍho ’yaṁ nābhijānāti loko mām ajam avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25*****.*** **One should not think that I was unmanifested and now I have manifested as the** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form of Śyāmasundara. This form of Śyāmasundara is eternal. Though shining personally as sun in the spiritual world, this form remains hidden from the eyes of ordinary people by a shadow in the form of** ***yogamāyā*****. Because of this, foolish people cannot know me, endowed with an imperishable** ***svarūpa*****.**

### Text 26

*vedāhaṁ samatītāni vartamānāni cārjuna \|*  
*bhaviṣyāṇi ca bhūtāni māṁ tu veda na kaścana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. With an eternal** ***sac-cid-ānanda svarūpa,*** **I know all things in the past and present. I know everything in the future as well. O Arjuna! Though understanding my two manifestations as Brahman and Paramātmā, people do not know my eternal form as Śyāmasundara of medium size to be eternal.**

### Text 27

*icchā-dveṣa-samutthena dvandva-mohena bhārata \|*  
*sarva-bhūtāni saṁmohaṁ sarge yānti parantapa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. The reason is that when the** ***jīva*** **is pure, only then he sees my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **by spiritual senses. When he is bound up and exists in the material creation, because of ignorance, he is bewildered by the illusion of dualities arising from desire and hatred. At that time there is no perception by knowledge.**

**By the strength of my** ***cit-śakti*** **I have manifested my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **in the material world and have become visible to material eyes \[of the bound** ***jīvas*****\]. However, being covered by** ***māyā,*** **having attained perception by ignorance, they consider my** ***svarūpa*** **to be temporary. This is understood to be their misfortune.**

### Text 28

*yeṣāṁ tv anta-gataṁ pāpaṁ janānāṁ puṇya-karmaṇām \|*  
*te dvandva-moha-nirmuktā bhajante māṁ dṛḍha-vratāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. Please hear how one attains qualification for attaining knowledgeable perception of my eternal** ***svarūpa*****. Those of demonic mentality and who are sinful do not have knowledgeable perception. Those who, having accepted living according to dharma and putting an end to sin in life by many** ***puṇya karmas*** **first accept** ***karma-yoga*** **and later,** ***jñāna,*** **and finally, by** ***dhyāna-yoga*** **realize my spiritual** ***tattva*** **through** ***samādhi*****. They can see my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **by knowledgeable perception \[arising through the** ***puṇya*** **of serving great devotees\]. Perception by knowledge is called knowledgeable perception (*****vidvat pratīti*****). Those people gradually become free of the dualities of dualism and monism, \[endowed with knowledge of** ***acintya-bhedābheda,*****\] worship me.**

### Text 29

*jarā-maraṇa-mokṣāya mām āśritya yatanti ye \|*  
*te brahma tad viduḥ kṛtsnam adhyātmaṁ karma cākhilam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. Birth and death take place in the material body. \[But\] the spiritual body of the** ***jīva*** **has no birth and death. Attaining that spiritual body, one attains the eternal quality of being my eternal servant. This is called liberation. Those who search for liberation devoid of birth and death by {*****sādhana-bhakti*****}/\[*****yoga-miśra-bhakti*****\] to me, having this engaged consciousness, can know** ***brahma-tattva, adhyātma-tattva,*** **and all of** ***karma-tattva*****.**

### Text 30

*sādhibhūtādhidaivaṁ māṁ sādhiyajñaṁ ca ye viduḥ \|*  
*prayāṇa-kāle ’pi ca māṁ te vidur yukta-cetasaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. Those who know {me along with} adhibhūta-tattva, adhidaiva-tattva, and adhiyajña-tattva can know me at the time of death, \[i.e. they attain sālokya**  
**with Paramātmā, my aṁśa, by the path of arcirādi\].**

**Комментарий:**

*Bhakti-yoga* arising from faith is of this type: by association with devotees, the *jīva* can know that Kṛṣṇa alone is the highest *tattva*. And that by his *cit-śakti* he performs his pastimes, by his *jīva-śakti* he manifests all *jīvas* and by *māyā-śakti,* binds the *bahir-mukha jīvas*. I am bound to matter due to my *bahir-mukha* nature. It is necessary that I attain Kṛṣṇa’s mercy by performing pure *bhakti*.

Desiring relief from suffering, desiring knowledge, desiring wealth, knowledge of Brahman, knowledge of Paramātmā, worship of the Lord with desire for freedom from old and death and attaining the abode of Paramātmā by that worship by the path of the *arcirādi*, i.e., attaining results of *sārṣṭi, sālokya, sāmīpya, sārūpya* and *sāyujya* are most insignificant for me. Rejecting all this, taking shelter of pure *bhakti* with hearing, chanting, etc., for attaining my *svarūpa* and *svabhāva* as the eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa, I will accomplish everything. This firm conviction is called faith. *Bhakti-yoga* arising from this faith is the basic conclusion of all scriptures. This is the essence of this chapter.

## Chapter Eight - Tāraka-brahma-yoga

### Text 1-2

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*kiṁ tad brahma kim adhyātmaṁ kiṁ karma puruṣottama \|*  
*adhibhūtaṁ ca kiṁ proktam adhidaivaṁ kim ucyate \|\|*  
*adhiyajñaḥ kathaṁ ko ’tra dehe ’smin madhusūdana \|*  
*prayāṇakāle ca kathaṁ jñeyo ’si niyatātmabhiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1-2. {Arjuna said: O Supreme Person! What is the real meaning of the six terms — Brahman,** ***adhyātma*****,** ***karma*****,** ***adhibhūta, adhidaiva*** **and** ***adhiyajña?*** **How are you to be known at the point of death by those with concentrated minds? Please explain this clearly.}**

**1. \[Arjuna said: O Supreme Person! Whom do Brahman,** ***adhyātma, karma, adhibhūta*** **and** ***adhidaiva*** **refer to?**

**2. Who is** ***adhiyajña*** **in the body and in what manner is he situated? How are you to be known at the point of death by those with concentrated minds? Please explain this clearly.\]**

### Text 3

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*akṣaraṁ brahma paramaṁ svabhāvo ’dhyātmam ucyate \|*  
*bhūta-bhāvodbhava-karo visargaḥ karma-saṁjñitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. {The Lord said: That** ***tattva*** **which is eternally without destruction or** ***akṣara,*** **devoid of any other state, is Para-brahman. The word Para-brahman is understood to be me, the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān endowed with eternal qualities. It does not mean Brahman devoid of form of the** ***jñānīs*** **or Paramātmā of the** ***yogīs*****.}**

**\[That** ***tattva*** **which is eternally without destruction or** ***akṣara,*** **devoid of any other state, is brahman (*****jīva*****), not Para-brahman. The word Para-brahman is understood to be me, the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān endowed with eternal qualities.\]**

**Комментарий:**

The word *adhyātmā* does not mean the eternal nature or qualities of the spiritual substance (Brahman). This indicates the pure *jīva* devoid of relationship with matter by *viśeṣa*.

From *karma*, the *jīva’s saṁsāra* producing a gross body arises through the various elements. For that reason, *karma* is called *visarga,* that which creates material bodies for beings.

### Text 4

*adhibhūtaṁ kṣaro bhāvaḥ puruṣaś cādhidaivatam \|*  
*adhiyajño ’ham evātra dehe deha-bhṛtāṁ vara \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4.** ***Adhibhūta*** **refers to the state which generates temporary objects (*****kṣara*****). The word** ***adhidaiva*** **means the** ***puruṣa,*** **the universal form, totality of deities like the sun, the** ***puruṣa*** **controlling sense knowledge. I am** ***adhiyajña,*** **the** ***antaryāmī*** **within the** ***ātmās*** **within all bodies.**

### Text 5

*anta-kāle ca mām eva smaran muktvā kalevaram \|*  
*yaḥ prayāti sa madbhāvaṁ yāti nāsty atra saṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. At the point of death, he who leaves the body while remembering me, attains a nature similar to mine.**

**Комментарий:**

He who remembers the Lord while understanding tattva at the time of death attains the state of the Lord \[after death. There is no doubt about this\].

### Text 6

*yaṁ yaṁ vāpi smaran bhāvaṁ tyajaty ante kalevaram \|*  
*taṁ tam evaiti kaunteya sadā tad-bhāva-bhāvitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. O son of Kuntī, one attains a state similar to the object of constant thought that one remembers while leaving the body.**

### Text 7

*tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu mām anusmara yudhya ca \|*  
*mayy arpita-mano-buddhir mām evaiṣyasy asaṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. Therefore, at all times remembering me as Para-brahman, perform fighting, which is prescribed according to your nature! By that your fixed intelligence and determined mind are offered to me and you will attain me.**

### Text 8

*abhyāsa-yoga-yuktena cetasā nānya-gāminā \|*  
*paramaṁ puruṣaṁ divyaṁ yāti pārthānucintayan \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Continuously contemplating the supreme person with mind absorbed in the practice of repeated remembrance without deviation, one attains the supreme person, i.e., by remembering the Lord there will be no \[more\] rebirth in temporary matter.**

### Texts 9-10

*kaviṁ purāṇam anuśāsitāram*  
*aṇor aṇīyāṁsam anusmared yaḥ \|*  
*sarvasya dhātāram acintya-rūpam*  
*āditya-varṇaṁ tamasaḥ parastāt \|\|*  
*prayāṇa-kāle manasācalena*  
*bhaktyā yukto yoga-balena caiva \|*  
*bhruvor madhye prāṇam āveśya samyak*  
*sa taṁ paraṁ puruṣam upaiti divyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9-10. I will speak of meditation on the supreme person. Please listen.**

**The** ***svarūpa*** **of the supreme person is omniscient, eternal, the controller, very subtle, the creator of everything, and inconceivable by material intelligence. He is eternally of medium form, a** ***tattva*** **endowed with color which reveals his** ***svarūpa*** **like the sun, because of his self-manifesting nature. He is beyond material** ***prakṛti*****. With unmoving mind at the time of death, placing the** ***prāṇa*** **between the brows due to previous practice of yoga, assisted by** ***bhakti*****, one will {go close to}/\[attain\] the attractive Lord.**

**Yoga teaches this essential method so that the consciousness will not be disturbed by the pains of death.**

### Text 11

*yad akṣaraṁ veda-vido vadanti*  
*viśanti yad yatayo vīta-rāgāḥ \|*  
*yad icchanto brahmacaryaṁ caranti*  
*tat te padaṁ saṅgraheṇa pravakṣye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. Along with the method, I will speak of the final goal—which the knowers of the** ***Veda*** **call** ***akṣara*****, which the** ***sannyāsīs*** **who have given up attachment attain and desiring which, the** ***brahmacārīs*** **practice celibacy.**

### Texts 12-13

*sarva-dvārāṇi saṁyamya mano hṛdi nirudhya ca \|*  
*mūrdhny ādhāyātmanaḥ prāṇam āsthito yoga-dhāraṇām \|\|*  
*om ity ekākṣaraṁ brahma vyāharan mām anusmaran \|*  
*yaḥ prayāti tyajan dehaṁ sa yāti paramāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12-13. He who leaves his body while withdrawing the senses from senses objects by** ***yoga-dhāraṇa,*** **\[by being unattached to sense objects\] concentrating the mind in the heart, \[by becoming detached in the heart,\] fixing the** ***prāṇa*** **at the brow, uttering “*****om ,*****” the root of the Vedas, and remembering me constantly, attains the supreme goal in the form of my planet, etc.**

### Text 14

*ananya-cetāḥ satataṁ yo māṁ smarati nityaśaḥ \|*  
*tasyāhaṁ sulabhaḥ pārtha nitya-yuktasya yoginaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. I have described the nature of prominent** ***bhakti*** **with** ***karma*** **\[and** ***jñāna*****\]*****,*** **i.e.,** ***karma-*****\[*****jñāna-*****\]*****miśra-bhakti,*** **starting from deliberation on** ***ārtas, jijñāsus, arthārthis*** **and** ***jñānīs*** **up to liberation from birth and death. I have spoken from verse 9 till now on the nature of** ***yoga-miśra-bhakti*** **i.e., prominent** ***bhakti*** **with** ***yoga*****. Among them, I have given some indications in order to give a realization of pure** ***bhakti*****. I will now speak about the** ***svarūpa*** **of pure** ***bhakti*****. Please listen.**

**Those devotees who with devoted heart, being constantly related to me, remember me alone, easily attain me. But I am difficult to attain by prominent** ***bhakti*** **with mixtures. This you should know.**

### Text 15

*mām upetya punar janma duḥkhālayam aśāśvatam \|*  
*nāpnuvanti mahātmānaḥ saṁsiddhiṁ paramāṁ gatāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. All the** ***bhakti-yogīs*****, \[great souls, having attained me,\] do not attain rebirth which is full of suffering and is temporary, since they have attained the highest goal. The quality of pure** ***bhakti*** **is complete dedication of heart to me (*****ananya-citta*****). Giving up aspirations of** ***yoga*** **and** ***jñāna,*** **the person who takes shelter of me exclusively performs pure** ***bhakti*****.**

### Text 16

*ā-brahma-bhuvanāl lokāḥ punar-āvartino ’rjuna \|*  
*mām upetya tu kaunteya punar-janma na vidyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. Beginning from Brahmaloka or Satyaloka all planets are temporary. All the jīvas in those planets take birth again. But the person who surrenders to me, the object of pure bhakti, does not take birth again.**

**Комментарий:**

It was said that karma-yogīs and aṣṭāṅga-yogīs {who take shelter of mixed bhakti} \[and saniṣṭha bhaktas \] do not take birth again. The purport of that is that {pure} bhakti is the final result or perfection of all these methods. Eventually after attaining pure bhakti they are delivered from repeated birth.

### Text 17

*sahasra-yuga-paryantam ahar yad brahmaṇo viduḥ \|*  
*rātriṁ yuga-sahasrāntāṁ te ’ho-rātra-vido janāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. A thousand** ***catur-yugas*** **of humans is one day of Brahmā and similarly a thousand** ***catur-yugas*** **is one night of Brahmā. Brahmā, after living for a hundred of such years, dies. The Brahmā who surrenders to the Lord is liberated. {Since this is Brahmā’s destination, the** ***sannyāsī*** **of that planet do not have fearlessness eternally}/\[Since this is Brahmā’s destination, where is fearlessness for a** ***sannyāsī*** **of that planet?\].**

### Text 18

*avyaktād vyaktayaḥ sarvāḥ prabhavanty ahar-āgame \|*  
*rātryā-game pralīyante tatraivāvyakta-saṁjñake \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. The** ***devatās,*** **animals and humans, situated in the three worlds, are more temporary than Brahmā in nature since at the end of Brahmā’s night they manifest from an unmanifest state. When night comes, they again merge into the unmanifest {*****tattva*****}. \[Here the word** ***avyakta*** **does not mean** ***pradhāna*** **but rather Brahmā’s state of sleep.\]**

### Text 19

*bhūta-grāmaḥ sa evāyaṁ bhūtvā bhūtvā pralīyate \|*  
*rātry-āgame ’vaśaḥ pārtha prabhavaty ahar-āgame \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. In this way, all the moving and non-moving entities, having \[repeatedly\] appeared during Brahmā’s day, disappear at the coming of his night. \[Dependent on karma, when day arrives again, they are born again.\]**

### Text 20

*paras tasmāt tu bhāvo ’nyo ’vyakto ’vyaktāt sanātanaḥ \|*  
*yaḥ sa sarveṣu bhūteṣu naśyatsu na vinaśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Superior to the unmanifest state is another unmanifest state which is eternal and superior, which is not destroyed when everything else is destroyed.**

### Text 21

*avyakto ’kṣara ity uktas tam āhuḥ paramāṁ gatim \|*  
*yaṁ prāpya na nivartante tad dhāma paramaṁ mama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. That unmanifest state which was described as** ***akṣara*** **is called the supreme goal for all beings. Know this to be my abode. The** ***jīva*** **who attains that abode does not return.**

### Text 22

*puruṣaḥ sa paraḥ pārtha bhaktyā labhyas tv ananyayā \|*  
*yasyāntaḥ-sthāni bhūtāni yena sarvam idaṁ tatam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. The supreme person, situated in that unmanifest state, is attained by pure** ***bhakti*****. O Pārtha! All beings exist within him. I, that person, enter into everything as the** ***antaryāmī*****.**

### Text 23

*yatra kāle tv anāvṛttim āvṛttiṁ caiva yoginaḥ \|*  
*prayātā yānti taṁ kālaṁ vakṣyāmi bharatarṣabha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. My pure devotees attain me without suffering. But those who do not attain pure** ***bhakti*** **to me and have attraction of** ***karma*** **and** ***jñāna,*** **can attain me only with much difficulty. Their time and path of going is limited by place and time. I will describe them. Please listen.**

**I will explain the time when** ***yogīs,*** **on dying, do not return, and the time when, on dying, they return.**

### Text 24

*agnir jyotir ahaḥ śuklaḥ ṣaṇ-māsā uttarāyaṇam \|*  
*tatra prayātā gacchanti brahma brahma-vido janāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. When the knowers of Brahman give up the body at the time of fire, light, auspicious day, the waxing fortnight and northern course of the sun (uttarāyaṇa), they attain Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

The words fire (agniḥ) and light (jyotiḥ) refer to thedevatā presiding over light. The word day (ahan) refers to the devatā in charge of the day. Śukla refers to the devatā  
presiding over the waxing fortnight. Uttarāyaṇa refers to the devatā in charge of uttarāyaṇa. Satisfaction of the mind and other senses at that time with those items is the cause of attaining Brahman. Dying at this time, the yogī does not return to this world.

### Text 25

*dhūmo rātris tathā kṛṣṇaḥ ṣaṇ-māsā dakṣiṇāyanam \|*  
*tatra cāndramasaṁ jyotir yogī prāpya nivartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. By** ***devatās*** **in charge of smoke, the night, the waning fortnight, the southern course of the sun, and the light of the moon, or by the action of the senses—the** ***karma-yogīs*** **\[by** ***karmas*** **such as** ***iṣṭāpūrta,*** **etc.,\] attain the path of return.**

### Text 26

*śukla-kṛṣṇe gatī hy ete jagataḥ śāśvate mate \|*  
*ekayā yāty anāvṛttim anyayāvartate punaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. These two paths of light and darkness are eternal paths in this world. By the path of light, one does not return, and by the path of darkness, one returns.**

### Text 27

*naite sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati kaścana \|*  
*tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu yoga-yukto bhavārjuna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Knowing the difference between these two paths, taking support of the path of** ***bhakti-yoga*** **beyond these two paths, the person with attentive mind is not bewildered at any time. Knowing the sufferings encountered on both paths, he takes support of pure** ***bhakti-yoga*****. O Arjuna! Take support of this** ***yoga*****.**

### Text 28

*vedeṣu yajñeṣu tapaḥsu caiva*  
*dāneṣu yat puṇya-phalaṁ pradiṣṭam\|*  
*atyeti tat sarvam idaṁ viditvā*  
*yogī paraṁ sthānam upaiti cādyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. Taking support of bhakti-yoga, you will not be cheated of any result. You will attain by bhakti-yoga the original and supreme place, surpassing the results of all types of jñāna and karma, of studying the Vedas, performing yajñas, austerities, and charity, etc.**

**Комментарий:**

When, performing my bhajana with pure faith in devotee association, anarthas cease, this faith transforms into niṣṭhā . Even before faith, all sins disappear. But contamination of thoughts related to material tattvas and who is to be worshipped remain. By performing bhajana in association with devotees, that disappears. The anarthas in the tattva of bhajana are sentiments mixed with jñāna , sentiments mixed with yoga and sentiments spoiled by bhukti and mukti. Being purified of all these anarthas, the tendency for bhakti becomes pure and one takes shelter of the Lord, the pure tattva. This is the essence of the eighth chapter.

## Chapter Nine - Rāja-gūhya-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*idaṁ tu te guhyatamaṁ pravakṣyāmy anasūyave \|*  
*jñānaṁ vijñāna-sahitaṁ yaj jñātvā mokṣyase ‘śubhāt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: O Arjuna! You are a person without envy. Therefore, I am teaching you the most secret knowledge endowed with the highest** ***vijñāna*****. Gathering that knowledge, become free from all inauspiciousness.**

**The knowledge of** ***adhyātmā*** **that I spoke in the second and third chapters was secret. What I spoke in the seventh and eighth chapters concerning Bhagavān, which produced** ***bhakti,*** **is more secret knowledge. Now what I will speak, pure** ***bhakti*****, is the most secret knowledge. By this, being free from inauspiciousness in the form of material** ***guṇas,*** **you will transcend the** ***guṇas*****.**

### Text 2

*rāja-vidyā rāja-guhyaṁ pavitram idam uttamam \|*  
*pratyakṣāvagamaṁ dharmyaṁ susukhaṁ kartum avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. Know this knowledge to be the king of knowledge, more secret than all secrets, most purifying, of the nature of direct realization of** ***ātmā,*** **accomplishing all** ***dharmas,*** **beyond the** ***guṇas*** **and {natural}/\[easy\].**

### Text 3

*aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣā dharmasyāsya parantapa \|*  
*aprāpya māṁ nivartante mṛtyu-saṁsāra-vartmani \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. Faith is the root of this knowledge since the** ***svarūpa*** **of this knowledge which is pure** ***rati*** **first arises as faith in the heart of the bound** ***jīva*****.**

**O Parantapa! All the** ***jīvas*** **in whom faith does not arise, being incapable of attaining knowledge which produces** ***rati*** **for the Lord in the form of** ***parama-dharma,*** **indifferent to me, fall onto the infinite path of** ***saṁsāra.***

### Text 4

*mayā tatam idaṁ sarvaṁ jagad avyakta-mūrtinā \|*  
*mat-sthāni sarva-bhūtāni na cāhaṁ teṣv avasthitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. A** ***svarūpa*** **with a form not visible to material senses, I pervade the whole world. All beings are situated in me, the** ***svarūpa*** **of consciousness.**

**I am not situated like how earth is situated in a pot i.e. the world is not my transformation or** ***vivarta*****. I am a** ***svarūpa*** **of pure, \[completely pervading\] consciousness. This world arises by the power of my** ***śakti*****. The world acts through my** ***śakti*****. \[But\] I am a separate** ***tattva,*** **situated as the** ***svarūpa*** **of complete consciousness.**

### Text 5

*na ca mat-sthāni bhūtāni paśya me yogam aiśvaram \|*  
*bhūta-bhṛn na ca bhūta-stho mamātmā bhūta-bhāvanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. I said that all beings are situated in me. This does not mean that all beings are situated in my pure** ***svarūpa*****, since everything is situated in the power of my** ***māyā-śakti*****. You cannot correlate this by intelligence of a** ***jīva*****. Understand it as my** ***aiśvarya-yoga,*** **an effect of my** ***śakti*****. Understand me as the supporter of all beings, situated in all beings and protector of all beings while determining that I have no difference between my body and myself. Though I am situated everywhere I am completely detached.**

### Text 6

*yathākāśa-sthito nityaṁ vāyuḥ sarvatra-go mahān \|*  
*tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni mat-sthānīty upadhāraya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. A material example related to such a subject is not satisfying. Thus bound** ***jīvas*** **cannot understand this** ***tattva*****. However, an example can show it to a small degree. I will explain that. Though you cannot completely conceive of it by deliberation, I will give an example.**

**Ether is an all-pervading substance. Situated in it, air or the movement of small particles goes everywhere. But ether, though the support of everything, is always unassociated with it. In that way though all beings arise and move about within my** ***śakti,*** **I, like ether, am unassociated with everything.**

### Text 7

*sarva-bhūtāni kaunteya prakṛtiṁ yānti māmikām \|*  
*kalpa-kṣaye punas tāni kalpādau visṛjāmy aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. O son of Kuntī, all things will enter my** ***prakṛti*** **at the time of destruction. At the time of creation, again I create everything by** ***prakṛti*****.**

### Text 8

*prakṛtiṁ svām avaṣṭabhya visṛjāmi punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*bhūta-grāmam imaṁ kṛtsnam avaśaṁ prakṛter vaśāt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. The world of living beings is subservient to my** ***prakṛti*****. They are under** ***prakṛti’s*** **control. By my desire I create them again and again. I create them by my** ***prakṛti*****.**

### Text 9

*na ca māṁ tāni karmāṇi nibadhnanti dhanañjaya \|*  
*udāsīna-vad āsīnam asaktaṁ teṣu karmasu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. But O conqueror of wealth! All these actions cannot bind me. I am unattached and indifferent to all these actions.**

**But actually, I am not indifferent. I am always attached to spiritual bliss. I, nourishing spiritual bliss, create all beings by my {*****bahiraṅga*****}** ***māyā*** **and** ***taṭastha śaktis*****. My** ***svarūpa*** **is not changed by them. Whatever all beings do under** ***māyā’s*** **control, by that (working through** ***śaktis*****), my pure pastimes of knowledge and bliss are nourished. In relation to material affairs, I am naturally seen to be indifferent.**

### Text 10

*mayādhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram \|*  
*hetunānena kaunteya jagad viparivartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10.** ***Prakṛti*** **is my** ***śakti*****. Under my shelter my** ***śakti*** **acts. By my glance at** ***prakṛti*** **from my desire related to my spiritual pastimes, I supervise all its actions.**

**Instigated by my glance,** ***prakṛti*** **produces the world of moving and nonmoving beings. Because of this, again and again the world manifests.**

### Text 11

*avajānanti māṁ mūḍhā mānuṣīṁ tanum āśritam \|*  
*paraṁ bhāvam ajānanto mama bhūta-maheśvaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. From what I said, you can understand that my** ***svarūpa*** **is composed of** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **and by my mercy my** ***śakti*** **does all actions. But I am independent of all those actions.**

**If I am seen in the world, it is just my mercy and the power of my** ***śakti*****. I am a being beyond all the material rules. Thus, though having a spiritual** ***svarūpa,*** **I manifest within the material world in my** ***svarūpa*****. The smallness, largeness, and invisibility—the unlimited particulars that man adores-- are just the effect of intelligence bound by** ***māyā*****. My supreme existence is not like that. My supreme situation is this: I am most extraordinary, and though having a** ***svarūpa*** **of medium size, by my** ***śakti*** **I am simultaneously all-pervading and smaller than the atom. The manifestation of my** ***svarūpa*** **occurs only by my** ***acintya-śakti*****.**

**Fools think that my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form is a human body and conclude that I accept a body as an** ***upādhi*****, bound by material laws. They do not understand that I am the great Lord of all beings in my** ***svarūpa*****.**

**Thus, by material vision they give me a meager existence. Those who have developed spiritual vision can understand that my** ***svarūpa*** **is an eternal** ***sac-cid-ānanda-tattva*****.**

### Text 12

*moghāśā mogha-karmāṇo mogha-jñānā vicetasaḥ \|*  
*rākṣasīm āsurīṁ caiva prakṛtiṁ mohinīṁ śritāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12*****.*** **If you ask how this material vision arises, please listen.**

**The hope,** ***karmas*** **and** ***jñāna*** **of fools, bewildered by Rākṣasa and demonic natures, is rendered useless. Their hearts are impelled to perform** ***karmas*** **by the hope of attaining some planet. Performing** ***karmas*** **to give insignificant results, they cannot attain pure** ***jñāna*****. If they sometimes seek** ***jñāna,*** **their** ***vidyā*** **disappears by corrupted** ***jñāna*** **in the form of philosophies of nondifference.**

**They think that my form is material and that I am** ***īśvara*****, a** ***tattva*** **inferior to Brahman. When their hearts become purified by worshipping me {as** ***sādhana*****}, they attain Brahman without qualities, {the** ***svarūpa*** **they desire}. The result is that the** ***jīva*** **is robbed of spiritual** ***prakṛti*** **by his Rākṣasa and demonic nature.**

### Text 13

*mahātmānas tu māṁ pārtha daivīṁ prakṛtim āśritāḥ \|*  
*bhajanty ananya-manaso jñātvā bhūtādim avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. O Pārtha! Those who attain spiritual vision are great souls. Taking shelter of the spiritual** ***prakṛti,*** **with concentrated mind, having no faith in** ***karma*** **which gives meager results and dry** ***jñāna*** **in the form of non-difference which destroys the self, they worship my** ***svarūpa*** **of Kṛṣṇa, the origin of all beings and indestructible, as the ultimate** ***tattva*****.**

### Text 14

*satataṁ kīrtayanto māṁ yatantaś ca dṛḍha-vratāḥ \|*  
*namasyantaś ca māṁ bhaktyā nitya-yuktā upāsate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. All the devotee great souls with spiritual vision,  constantly glorify my name, form, qualities and pastimes. They perform** ***bhakti*** **of nine types beginning with hearing and chanting. For attaining eternal servitorship to my** ***sac-cid-ānanda svarūpa,*** **they cultivate me, making firm vows \[such as Ekādaśī and Janmāṣṭāmi\] with body, mind, society and soul. Their hearts are not disturbed by material activities. Thus, while maintaining the body in** ***saṁsāra*****, they surrender to me through** ***bhakti-yoga*****.**

### Text 15

*jñāna-yajñena cāpy anye yajanto mām upāsate \|*  
*ekatvena pṛthaktvena bahudhā viśvato-mukham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. O Arjuna! The pure devotees are superior to the** ***ārta, jijñāsu, arthārthī*** **and** ***jñānī*****. Referring to them as** ***mahātmā,*** **I have shown that to you in various ways. Now, there are three types of devotees inferior to them, not mentioned previously. I will describe them. The learned call them worshippers of the self (*****ahaṅgrahopāsaka*****), worshippers of idols (*****pratīkopāsaka*****) and worshippers of the universal form (*****viśvarūpopāsaka*****).**

**Among these lesser three types, (1) the worshippers of the self are most prominent. They worship with the conception that they themselves are Bhagavān. This is one type of** ***yajña*** **of worshipping the Supreme Lord. Worshipping with the idea of nondifference, these worshippers of the self-worship {Brahman}/\[me\].**

**(2) Those who worship idols (*****devatās*****) are inferior to them. Knowing themselves as different from the Lord, they worship Sūrya, Indra, etc., as** ***vibhūtis*** **of the Lord.**

**(3) More foolish than them are those who worship the Lord as the universal form.**

**In this way three types of** ***jñāna-yajña*** **are seen.**

### Texts 16-19

*ahaṁ kratur ahaṁ yajñaḥ svadhāham aham auṣadham*  
*mantro ‘ham aham evājyam aham agnir ahaṁ hutam \|\|*  
*pitāham asya jagato mātā dhātā pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*vedyaṁ pavitram oṁkāra ṛk sāma yajur eva ca \|\|*  
*gatir bhartā prabhuḥ sākṣī nivāsaḥ śaraṇaṁ suhṛt \|*  
*prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ sthānaṁ nidhānaṁ bījam avyayam \|\|*  
*tapāmy aham ahaṁ varṣaṁ nigṛhṇāmy utsṛjāmi ca \|*  
*amṛtaṁ caiva mṛtyuś ca sad asac cāham arjuna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16-19. I am the sacrifices mentioned in the** ***śruti,*** **such as** ***agniṣṭoma,*** **and** ***smṛti*****, such as** ***vaiśvadeva*****. I am** ***svadhā,*** **I am** ***auṣadha,*** **I am the mantra, I am the ghee, I am the fire, I am the** ***homa,*** **I am the father of the universe, the mother, the creator, and the grandfather. I am the pure** ***oṁkāra*****, the** ***ṛg, Sāma*** **and** ***Yajur Veda*****. I am the goal of all, the maintainer, the master, the witness, the abode, the shelter, the friend, the birth, destruction, maintenance, the cause and indestructible seed. I am the heat in summer and the rain in the monsoon season. I disperse water and draw up water. I am immortality. I am death. I am cause and effect. O Arjuna! Meditating in this way, one can worship me as the universal form.**

### Text 20

*traividyā māṁ soma-pāḥ pūta-pāpā*  
*yajñair iṣṭvā svar-gatiṁ prārthayante \|*  
*te puṇyam āsādya surendra-lokam*  
*aśnanti divyān divi deva-bhogān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. If there is a trace of** ***bhakti*** **in these three types of worship then after worshipping me as the Supreme Lord, the** ***jīva*** **gradually giving up the respective contaminations, attains liberation in the form of pure** ***bhakti*** **to me. (1) By studying** ***bhakti,*** **the worshippers of the self who think of themselves as the Lord develop pure** ***bhakti*****. (2) Those who worship idols considering the various** ***devatās*** **as the Lord, giving up that conception by association with devotees, accept me as the Lord, the** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form. (3) The worshippers of the universal form, uncertain of the conception of Paramātmā, by gradual manifestion of my** ***svarūpa,*** **concentrate on my medium sized** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form.**

**But those among these three types of worshippers who are attached to** ***karma*** **and** ***jñāna*** **with aversion to the Bhagavān, do not attain eternally auspicious** ***bhakti*****. Those worshippers with a conception of non-difference eventually fall into the net of bad logic,** ***māyāvāda*****, because of aversion to the Lord.**

**Worshippers of idols (i.e.** ***devatās*** **as the Lord), bound by** ***karma*** **procedures written in** ***ṛg, Yajur*** **and** ***Sāma Vedas*****, become free of sin after having studied the three** ***Vedas*** **teaching** ***karma*** **by drinking** ***soma*****. After worshipping me by all** ***yajñas*****, they pray to attain Svarga. Gaining** ***puṇyas*****, they attain all enjoyment of the** ***devatās*** **on Svarga.**

### Text 21

*te taṁ bhuktvā svarga-lokaṁ viśālaṁ*  
*kṣīṇe puṇye martya-lokaṁ viśanti \|*  
*evaṁ trayī-dharmam anuprapannā*  
*gatāgataṁ kāma-kāmā labhante \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. After enjoying Svarga which gives great happiness, when the** ***puṇya*** **is destroyed, they return to the world of mortals. Those desiring enjoyment by following the three Vedas again and again are born and die.**

### Text 22

*ananyāś cintayanto māṁ ye janāḥ paryupāsate \|*  
*teṣāṁ nityābhiyuktānāṁ yoga-kṣemaṁ vahāmy aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Do not think that all these worshippers of the three Vedas, filled with material desire, will attain happiness and that my devotees suffer.**

**All my devotees contemplate on me with exclusive devotion. They accept all material objects which are not contrary to** ***bhakti-yoga*** **for maintaining the body. Therefore, they are constantly engaged in my service (*****nityābhiyuktānām*****). Being without desire, they offer everything to me. I also give all objects to them and protect everything they have.**

**The purport is that when they accept objects sanctioned by** ***bhakti-yoga*****, {by material vision,} they seem to be enjoying objects. In this respect my devotees are no different from the worshippers of idols (*****devatās*****) having material desires. But though the devotees have no material desires, I maintain what they have and supply what they lack. The special gain for my devotees is that they enjoy appropriately all objects by my mercy and attain eternal bliss in the end. The idol (*****devatā*****) worshippers, after enjoying happiness of the senses, again arrive in the field of karma. They do not have eternal happiness.**

**Though I am indifferent to all objects, since I am affectionate to devotees {I attain bliss by acts of helping devotees. In this, my devotees make no offense}/\[I do not take any offense of the devotees\] because they do not pray to me for anything. By my own initiative, I supply what they lack.**

### Text 23

*ye ’py anya-devatā-bhaktā yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ \|*  
*te ’pi mām eva kaunteya yajanty avidhi-pūrvakam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. Actually I alone with** ***sac-cid-ānanda svarūpa*** **am the Supreme Lord. No** ***devatā*** **is independent of me. I am always a spiritual** ***sac-cid-ānanda tattva*** **in my** ***svarūpa*****. Many people worship Sūrya and other** ***devatās*** **i.e., humans bound by the material world worship my forms of power reflected by** ***māyā’s guṇas*** **as** ***devatās*****. But if one deliberates, one sees that they are my** ***guṇāvatāras*****. Understanding their** ***tattva*** **and the** ***tattva*** **of my** ***svarūpa*****, they then worship all the** ***devatās*** **as my** ***guṇāvatāras*****.**

**Their worship is according to rules and approved as a step for advancement. Those who worship while thinking that the** ***devatās*** **are eternal, worship contrary to rules. Because of this, they do not attain eternal results.**

### Text 24

*ahaṁ hi sarva-yajñānāṁ bhoktā ca prabhur eva ca \|*  
*na tu mām abhijānanti tattvenātaś cyavanti te \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. I alone am the enjoyer and master of all** ***yajñas*****. Those who worship** ***devatās,*** **thinking they are independent of me are called worshippers of idols. They do not understand my** ***tattva.*** **Thus they fall from the truth because of worshipping falsely. But those who worship Sūrya and other** ***devatās*** **as my** ***vibhūtis*** **can finally be auspicious.**

### Text 25

*yānti deva-vratā devān pitṝn yānti pitṛ-vratāḥ \|*  
*bhūtāni yānti bhūtejyā yānti mad-yājino ‘pi mām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. Those who worship the various** ***devatās*** **as the Supreme Lord, taking shelter of impermanent objects and their qualities, attain the impermanence of the** ***devatās*** **they worship. Those who worship** ***pitṛs*** **attain the impermanent** ***pitṛ-loka*****. Those who worship** ***bhūtas*** **attain the impermanent world of** ***bhūtas*****. But those who worship me, endowed with a** ***svarūpa*** **of eternal spiritual** ***tattva,*** **attain me.**

**Thus I show no prejudice in rewarding results. My unwavering rule is to award karmic results to the** ***jīva*** **with indifference.**

### Text 26

*patraṁ puṣpaṁ phalaṁ toyaṁ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati \|*  
*tad ahaṁ bhakty-upahṛtam aśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. I accept with great affection whatever the pure minded devotees offer—a leaf, a flower, a fruit or water, with devotion. I do not accept the many items that the worshippers of** ***devatās*** **offer with effort and with mere temporary faith, since they worship me only when they are in trouble.**

### Text 27

*yat karoṣi yad aśnāsi yaj juhoṣi dadāsi yat \|*  
*yat tapasyasi kaunteya tat kuruṣva mad-arpaṇam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Those qualified for** ***bhakti*** **are of four types: the** ***ārta,*** **the** ***jijñāsu,*** **the** ***arthārthī*** **and the** ***jñānī*****. Before accepting** ***bhakti,*** **they had three types of** ***sādhana*** **previously: worship of the self, worship of idols and worship of the universal form. At the time of starting** ***bhakti,*** **they have four ways of dealing with the material world:** ***sakāma-karma-yoga, niṣkāma-karma-yoga, jñāna-yoga*** **and** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*****. Having explained all this, I explained the** ***svarūpa*** **of pure** ***bhakti*****.**

**O Arjuna! Now determine your own qualification. Appearing with the form of** ***dharma-vīra,*** **you are engaged in activities to nourish my pastimes. Thus, you cannot be classed among the detached (*****śānta*****) devotees or the** ***sakāma*** **devotees. Therefore, you should perform** ***bhakti*** **mixed with** ***niṣkāma-karma*** **and** ***jñāna*****. You must act as follows.**

**Whatever you do, whatever you enjoy, whatever you offer in sacrifice, whatever austerity you perform, offer all that to me.**

**Having done actions with other goals, persons dulled by** ***karmas*** **finally offer the results to me with material intentions. That is of no value at all. Offering** ***karmas*** **to me at the root, perform** ***bhakti*****.**

### Text 28

*śubhāśubha-phalair evaṁ mokṣyase karma-bandhanaiḥ \|*  
*sannyāsa-yoga-yuktātmā vimukto mām upaiṣyasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. {Engaged in** ***sannyāsa-yoga*** **in the form of renouncing all results of** ***karmas*** **such as fighting, becoming free from the bondage of auspicious and inauspicious results, attaining liberation, you will attain the** ***tattva*** **of my real** ***svarūpa*****.}**

**28. \[Engaged in** ***sannyāsa*** **in the form of offering all actions to me, becoming free from the bondage of auspicious and inauspicious results of all** ***karmas,*** **you will attain service related to my** ***svarūpa*****.\]**

### Text 29

*samo ‘haṁ sarva-bhūteṣu na me dveṣyo ‘sti na priyaḥ \|*  
*ye bhajanti tu māṁ bhaktyā mayi te teṣu cāpy aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. This is my secret: I conduct myself with equality towards all beings. I do not hate anyone, nor do I love anyone. This is my general rule. But my special rule is this: he who worships me with devotion becomes attached to me and I become attached to him.**

### Text 30

*api cet sudurācāro bhajate mām ananya-bhāk \|*  
*sādhur eva sa mantavyaḥ samyag vyavasito hi saḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. The person who worships me with devoted heart, though having sinful conduct, is considered to be a devotee since his determination is most proper.**

**Комментарий:**

One should understand the word “sinful conduct” (*sudurācāra*). The bound *jīva* has two types of conduct: relative (*sāmbandhika*) and related to his *svarūpa* (*svarūpa-gata*). Cleanliness, *puṇya,* nourishment and supply of what is lacking related to protecting the body, protecting society, upraising the mind, are all *sāmbandhika* conduct. The pure *jīva’s* conduct in the form of spiritual activities of worshipping me are *svarūpa-gata* conduct. They are also called unmixed or pure *bhakti*.

Even the pure *bhakti* of the *jīva* in a bound state keeps an unavoidable relationship with *sāmbandhika* conduct i.e., when the bound *jīva* develops *bhakti* in the form of pure worship, *sāmbandhika* actions must continue as long as the body remains. When *bhakti* arises, there is no other taste. As much as the tastes for *bhakti* increases, that much the taste for other things decreases. Until that material taste is completely destroyed however, one sometimes commits sinful acts while strongly manifesting a taste for other things.

But this will quickly be controlled by taste for Kṛṣṇa. The determination of the *jīvas* ascending the progressive staircase of *bhakti* is completely auspicious. If incidentally sinful conduct is seen sometimes, that sinful conduct will quickly go away. It does not contaminate the strong tendency of *bhakti* to me. This should be understood. {Though it is seen that a great devotee previously ate fish, or had illicit connection with women, he cannot be considered to be improper (*asādhu*).}

### Text 31

*kṣipraṁ bhavati dharmātmā śaśvac-chāntiṁ nigacchati\|*  
*kaunteya pratijānīhi na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. O Kaunteya! I promise that the** ***jīva*** **who has started the path of pure** ***bhakti*** **is never destroyed. Though in the first stage there remains some conduct of** ***adharma*** **because of his nature and circumstance, that** ***adharma*** **will quickly be destroyed {by** ***bhakti,*** **the supreme medicine}/\[by remembering the Lord with repentance, which destroys obstacles to worship\]. The person fixed in** ***svarūpa-gata*** **conduct in the form of eternal** ***dharma,*** **will attain the highest peace arising from** ***bhakti,*** **beyond the bondage of sin and piety.**

### Text 32

*māṁ hi pārtha vyapāśritya ye ’pi syuḥ pāpa-yonayaḥ \|*  
*striyo vaiśyās tathā śūdrās te ’pi yānti parāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. O Pārtha! Outcaste** ***mlecchas,*** **and fallen women such as prostitutes, as well as men of lower caste like** ***vaiśyas*** **and** ***śūdras,*** **when they take shelter of pure** ***bhakti*** **with excellence, quickly attain the supreme goal.**
