**For those who take shelter of my path of** ***bhakti,*** **there is no obstacle related to birth, caste, etc.**

### Text 33

*kiṁ punar brāhmaṇāḥ puṇyā bhaktā rājarṣayas tathā \|*  
*anityam asukhaṁ lokam imaṁ prāpya bhajasva mām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. When all the outcastes become qualified with pure** ***bhakti,*** **their natural tendency for sin cannot be an obstacle, since by the appearance of** ***bhakti,*** **all tendency to sin is quickly destroyed.**

**Then what doubt is there that pious** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and** ***kṣatriyas*** **by** ***svarūpa-gata*** **conduct related to** ***bhakti*** **will quickly destroy inauspiciousness in the form of results of** ***puṇya*****?**

**Thus having attained a position in this temporary and unhappy world, worship only me with excellence.**

### Text 34

*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru \|*  
*mām evaiṣyasi yuktvaivam ātmānaṁ mat-parāyaṇaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. Engage your mind in thinking of me. Engage your body in devotional worship and in surrendering to me. Becoming dedicated to me, though conducting all karmas such as fighting, you will certainly attain me.**

**Комментарий:**

Pure *bhakti* is the method for the *jīva* to attain the goal. The pure *jīva* is suitable for worshipping the Lord. The pure *tattva* of Kṛṣṇa’s form is the object of worship for the pure *jīva*. A long as one does not know these *tattvas*, one cannot perform the highest actions nicely. Pure *bhakti-yoga* free from mixtures of *jñāna, yoga* and *karma* has been described in the seventh and eighth chapters.

In the ninth chapter, purity of the object (*tattva*) to be worshipped is alone taught. When describing the purity of object of worship, it is shown along with a description of all the contaminations of this *tattva*. The eternal nature of the form of Bhagavān having a spiritual *svarūpa*, is shown by *vijñāna* for this purpose. The *jñānī*, *yogī* and performers of *yajña* worship Brahman and Paramātmā, forms of power of the Supreme Lord who is endowed with an eternal form. But the pure devotees worship only the eternal form of Kṛṣṇa, not worshipping the partial forms of the supreme tattva. Worship of various *devatās*, thinking them separate from Kṛṣṇa’s eternal svarūpa is done out of extreme ignorance since when one worships these *devatās*, one attains a fragmented result. In *bhakti-yoga* one should give up worshiping *devatās* and live one’s life while practicing the nine types of *bhakti*—hearing about, chanting about and remembering Kṛṣṇa’s *svarūpa*, with firm faith without other desires. If the fully dedicated devotee performs some sinful act, still he is superior to the *karmī, jñānī* and *yogī*. He is of proper behavior since in a few days when he becomes fixed in exclusive *bhakti*, no faults in his character will remain. My pure *bhakti* will produce this result. The pure devotee is not destroyed and does not fall since I am responsible for his livelihood. Taking support of pure *bhakti-yoga*, maintaining one’s life is an agreeable activity.

## Chapter Ten - Vibhūti-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*bhūya eva mahā-bāho śṛṇu me paramaṁ vacaḥ \|*  
*yat te ’haṁ prīyamāṇāya vakṣyāmi hita-kāmyayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: You have** ***prema*****. Desiring your benefit, I previously spoke concerning my** ***vibhūtis*** **briefly. I will now speak of this in detail. Please listen with attention.**

### Text 2

*na me viduḥ sura-gaṇāḥ prabhavaṁ na maharṣayaḥ \|*  
*aham ādir hi devānāṁ maharṣīṇāṁ ca sarvaśaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. I am the original cause of** ***devatās*** **and great sages. Thus, even the** ***devatās*** **and great sages cannot understand the powerful manifestations of my pastimes or how I appear in the material world in my** ***svarūpa*** **with human form.**

***Devatās*** **or great sages seek the truth about me by the power of their own intelligence. Making attempts to distinguish by material intelligence, they think the supreme** ***tattva*** **realized to some degree, to be some unmanifest form opposite of the material world, something unclear, with no qualities, with no form and dry Brahman.**

**But that is not the supreme** ***tattva*****. The** ***svarūpa*** **of the highest** ***tattva,*** **myself, is a** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form endowed with eternal** ***svarūpa,*** **always self-manifested by the power of** ***acintya-śakti,*** **endowed with faultless qualities.**

**My** ***svarūpa*** **reflected in the** ***aparā-śakti*** **is** ***īśvara*** **(Paramātmā). My indistinct form beyond limits of thought for the** ***jīva*** **bound by the** ***aparā-śakti*** **is indistinct Brahman. Thus,** ***īśvara*** **or Paramātmā and Brahman are my two manifestations characterized as forms which are associated with created things or the opposite of created things.**

**I personally sometimes appear in the material world in my** ***svarūpa*** **through my eternal** ***acintya-śakti*****. Endowed with their intelligence,** ***devatās*** **and great sages, not able to understand my** ***acintya-śakti,*** **bewildered by** ***māyā,*** **think that the manifestation of my** ***svarūpa*** **is** ***īśvara-tattva*** **(Paramātmā). Or thinking that Brahman is best, they seek to merge their** ***svarūpas*** **into it.**

**But all my devotees, knowing that it is not easy to understand the** ***acintya-tattva*** **by one’s meager knowledge, cultivate** ***bhakti*** **to me. Being softened with mercy by that, I give realization of my** ***svarūpa*** **to them by natural knowledge.**

### Text 3

*yo mām ajam anādiṁ ca vetti loka-maheśvaram \|*  
*asammūḍhaḥ sa martyeṣu sarva-pāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. He who knows me as the great lord of all beings, and without beginning, who understands by my mercy the superiority of my** ***sac-cid-ānanda svarūpa*** **and its beginningless nature, is freed from all sin in the form of intelligence contaminated by matter i.e., from all impure states.**

### Texts 4-5

*buddhir jñānam asammohaḥ kṣamā satyaṁ damaḥ śamaḥ \|*  
*sukhaṁ duḥkhaṁ bhavo ’bhāvo bhayaṁ cābhayam eva ca \|\|*  
*ahiṁsā samatā tuṣṭis tapo dānaṁ yaśo ’yaśaḥ \|*  
*bhavanti bhāvā bhūtānāṁ matta eva pṛthag-vidhāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4-5. {Those knowledgeable of scriptures cannot know my** ***tattva*** **by good intelligence. The reason is this.} Intelligence (ability to discern subtle meaning), knowledge (distinguishing** ***ātmā*** **from non-*****ātmā*****), freedom from illusion, tolerance, truthfulness, sense control, mind control, pleasure, pain, birth (*****bhava*****), death (*****abhāva*****), fear, fearlessness, non-violence, equanimity, satisfaction, austerity, charity, fame, and infamy are the conditions of all beings. {I am the cause of all these, but I am separate from them. If one knows my** ***tattva*** **of** ***acintya-bhedābheda,*** **nothing is unknown to that person. Just as the** ***śakti*** **and the** ***śaktimān*** **(possessor of** ***śakti*****) are nondifferent, so I, the** ***śaktimān,*** **am different from, though eternally nondifferent from, the universe made of all things and conditions arising from my** ***śakti*****.}/\[I create with different characteristics these conditions of all beings\]**

### Text 6

*maharṣayaḥ sapta pūrve catvāro manavas tathā \|*  
*mad-bhāvā mānasā jātā yeṣāṁ loka imāḥ prajāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. The seven great sages like Marīci, and before them, the four Kumāras and the fourteen Manus like Svāyambhuva, arose from Hiraṇyagarbha arising from my** ***śakti*****. The world became filled with their descendants or disciples.**

### Text 7

*etāṁ vibhūtiṁ yogaṁ ca mama yo vetti tattvataḥ \|*  
*so ’vikalpena yogena yujyate nātra saṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. He who knows these two subjects: my** ***svarūpa-jñāna,*** **the limit of** ***tattva-jñāna,*** **along with knowledge of my** ***vibhūtis*** **arising from my** ***śakti,*** **and who knows** ***bhakti-yoga,*** **the final limit of** ***kriyā-yoga,*** **performs** ***yoga*** **without doubts (*****avikalpa*****).**

### Text 8

*ahaṁ sarvasya prabhavo mattaḥ sarvaṁ pravartate \|*  
*iti matvā bhajante māṁ budhā bhāva-samanvitāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Know me to be the source of all spiritual and material things. Those who know this and worship me with pure** ***bhakti*** **(*****bhāva*****) are learned. Others are without knowledge.**

### Text 9

*mac-cittā mad-gata-prāṇā bodhayantaḥ parasparam \|*  
*kathayantaś ca māṁ nityaṁ tuṣyanti ca ramanti ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. The character of such exclusive devotees is this: Having completely offered their hearts and** ***prāṇa*** **to me, they speak with each other about the Lord, exchanging affection. By this hearing and chanting they attain happiness of** ***bhakti*** **at the stage of** ***sādhana,*** **and the happiness of amorous love and enjoyment in** ***mādhurya-rasa*** **within Vraja’s** ***rasas*** **on the path of** ***rāga*** **at the stage of perfection, i.e. on attaining the stage of** ***prema*****.**

### Text 10

*teṣāṁ satata-yuktānāṁ bhajatāṁ prīti-pūrvakam \|*  
*dadāmi buddhi-yogaṁ taṁ yena mām upayānti te \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. To those who worship me with affection by eternal** ***bhakti-yoga,*** **I give** ***yoga*** **of pure** ***prema*** **arising from pure knowledge. By that, they attain my abode of the highest bliss.**

### Text 11

*teṣām evānukampārtham aham ajñānajaṁ tamaḥ \|*  
*nāśayāmy ātma-bhāva-stho jñāna-dīpena bhāsvatā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. Ignorance cannot remain for those practicing** ***bhakti-yoga*****. Many think that those who reject the material and search for the real, attain real knowledge and that {if one just performs** ***bhakti,*** **one will not attain that rare knowledge.}/\[if one just cultivates** ***bhakti-yoga*** **how will one attain rare** ***jñāna*****?\]**

**O Arjuna! Here is the basic fact. The small** ***jīva,*** **by cultivating his own intelligence, cannot attain knowledge of the unlimited, real** ***tattva*****. However much he may deliberate, he cannot attain pure knowledge at all. But if I am merciful, then easily the small** ***jīva*** **can attain correct knowledge by the power of my** ***acintya-śakti*****.**

**Those who are my pure devotees, easily placing me in their mental functions, become illuminated by my extraordinary lamp of knowledge. By special mercy I become situated in their hearts and destroy completely the darkness born of ignorance arising from material association.**

**Qualification of the** ***jīva*** **for pure knowledge arises by cultivation of** ***bhakti*****. It cannot be attained by logic.**

### Texts 12-13

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*paraṁ brahma paraṁ dhāma pavitraṁ paramaṁ bhavān \|*  
*puruṣaṁ śāśvataṁ divyam ādi-devam ajaṁ vibhum \|\|*  
*āhus tvām ṛṣayaḥ sarve devarṣir nāradas tathā \|*  
*asito devalo vyāsaḥ svayaṁ caiva bravīṣi me \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12-13. Having heard the four verses which are the essence of the Gītā, for the purpose of making it easy to understand, Arjuna said:**

**O Lord! The sage among** ***devatās*** **Nārada, as well as Asita, Devala and Vyāsa and other sages, and you also, have established that you endowed with** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form are Para-brahman, the highest** ***svarūpa,*** **the highest purity, the highest person, eternal, the original Lord, unborn and all pervading.**

### Text 14

*sarvam etad ṛtaṁ manye yan māṁ vadasi keśava \|*  
*na hi te bhagavan vyaktiṁ vidur devā na dānavāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. O Keśava! With faith I accept all this to be true. Among the** ***devatās*** **and** ***dānavas,*** **no one knows your inconceivable manifestation.**

### Text 15

*svayam evātmanātmānaṁ vettha tvaṁ puruṣottama \|*  
*bhūta-bhāvana bhūteśa deva-deva jagat-pate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. O creator of all beings, the lord of all creators, the performer of pastimes, the master of all in this universe, the supreme person, you alone know your distinct** ***tattva*** **by your** ***cit-śakti*****. By human logic or** ***devatā*** **logic no one can understand that eternal** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form which existed before creation, and how it appears within matter with a form independent of material laws. The person who gains your mercy can understand it.**

### Text 16

*vaktum arhasy aśeṣeṇa divyā hy ātma-vibhūtayaḥ \|*  
*yābhir vibhūtibhir lokān imāṁs tvaṁ vyāpya tiṣṭhasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. By your mercy I can see manifested in my heart and before my eyes your true** ***svarūpa*****. By this I have been successful. But I want to know in detail all your** ***vibhūtis*** **by which you spread throughout the world. By your mercy speak this to me.**

### Text 17

*kathaṁ vidyām ahaṁ yogiṁs tvāṁ sadā paricintayan \|*  
*keṣu keṣu ca bhāveṣu cintyo ’si bhagavan mayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. \[The** ***yoga-māyā-śakti*** **is eternally present in you.\] O Lord! How are you to be understood by contemplation? In what ways are you to be contemplated?**

### Text 18

*vistareṇātmano yogaṁ vibhūtiṁ ca janārdana \|*  
*bhūyaḥ kathaya tṛptir hi śṛṇvato nāsti me ’mṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. O Janārdana, please tell me again in detail about your** ***vibhūtis*** **and your** ***yoga*****. Hearing about your sweet** ***tattva,*** **I have no satiation. Rather my thirst for hearing increases.**

### Text 19

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*hanta te kathayiṣyāmi divyā hy ātma-vibhūtayaḥ \|*  
*prādhānyataḥ kuru-śreṣṭha nāsty anto vistarasya me \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. The Lord said: O Arjuna! There is no end to my attractive** ***vibhūtis*****. I will speak to you about some of my chief** ***vibhūtis*****. Please listen.**

### Text 20

*aham ātmā guḍākeśa sarva-bhūtāśaya-sthitaḥ \|*  
*aham ādiś ca madhyaṁ ca bhūtānām anta eva ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. O conqueror of sleep! I will speak the** ***tattva*** **of my** ***svarūpa*****. The** ***tattva*** **of my relation is this: I am the** ***ātmā*** **of the universe. I am situated as the** ***antaryāmī purusa*****. \[I am the** ***antaryāmī*** **of** ***mūla-prakṛti*** **as Kāraṇodaśāyī. I am the** ***antaryāmī*** **of the** ***samaṣṭi-virāṭ*** **as Garbhodaśāyī. I am the** ***antaryāmī*** **of the** ***jīvas,*** **or** ***vyaṣṭi-virāṭ*** **as Kṣīrodaśāyī.\]**

**I am the beginning of all beings, their middle and their end.**

### Text 21

*ādityānām ahaṁ viṣṇur jyotiṣāṁ ravir aṁśumān \|*  
*marīcir marutām asmi nakṣatrāṇām ahaṁ śaśī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. Among Ādityas, I am Vāmana (*****viṣṇuḥ*****), and among lights, I am the many-rayed sun. Among the winds, I am Marīci. I am the moon amidst the stars.**

### Text 22

*vedānāṁ sāma-vedo ’smi devānām asmi vāsavaḥ \|*  
*indriyāṇāṁ manaś cāsmi bhūtānām asmi cetanā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Among all the** ***Vedas,*** **I am the** ***Sāma Veda,*** **and among the** ***devatās,*** **I am Indra. Among the senses, I am the mind. In all living creatures, I am** ***jñāna-śakti,*** **related to consciousness.**

### Text 23

*rudrāṇāṁ śaṅkaraś cāsmi vitteśo yakṣa-rakṣasām \|*  
*vasūnāṁ pāvakaś cāsmi meruḥ śikhariṇām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. Among the Rudras, I am Śiva and among the Yakṣas and Rākṣasas, I am Kuvera. Among the Vasus, I am Agni. Among the peaked mountains, I am Meru.**

### Text 24

*purodhasāṁ ca mukhyaṁ māṁ viddhi pārtha bṛhaspatim \|*  
*senānīnām ahaṁ skandaḥ sarasām asmi sāgaraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. Among head priests, I am Bṛhaspati. Among leaders of armies, I am Kārtikeya. Among reservoirs of water, I am the ocean.**

### Text 25

*maharṣīṇāṁ bhṛgur ahaṁ girām asmy ekam akṣaram \|*  
*yajñānāṁ japa-yajño ’smi sthāvarāṇāṁ himālayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. Among the great sages, I am Bhṛgu. Among words, I am** ***oṁ*****. Among sacrifices, I am** ***japa*****. Among immovable objects, I am the Himālayas.**

### Text 26

*aśvatthaḥ sarva-vṛkṣāṇāṁ devarṣīṇāṁ ca nāradaḥ \|*  
*gandharvāṇāṁ citrarathaḥ siddhānāṁ kapilo muniḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. Among the trees, I am the** ***pippala*****. Among the divine sages, I am Nārada. Among the Gandharvas, I am Citraratha (their king). Among perfected beings, I am Kapila.**

### Text 27

*uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṁ viddhi mām amṛtodbhavam \|*  
*airāvataṁ gajendrāṇāṁ narāṇāṁ ca narādhipam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Know that among horses, I am Uccaiḥśrava who rose at the churning of ocean. Among elephants, I am Airāvata. Among men, I am the emperor.**

### Text 28

*āyudhānām ahaṁ vajraṁ dhenūnām asmi kāma-dhuk \|*  
*prajanaś cāsmi kandarpaḥ sarpāṇām asmi vāsukiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. Among weapons, I am Vajra, the thunderbolt. Among cows, I am the Kāma-dhenu. I am Cupid the root** ***svarūpa*** **for producing offspring. Among snakes, I am Vāsuki.**

### Text 29

*anantaś cāsmi nāgānāṁ varuṇo yādasām aham \|*  
*pitṝṇām aryamā cāsmi yamaḥ saṁyamatām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. Among Nāgas, I am Ananta. Among inhabitants of water, I am Varuṇa. Among the** ***pitṛs*****, I am Aryamā. Among punishers, I am Yama.**

### Text 30

*prahlādaś cāsmi daityānāṁ kālaḥ kalayatām aham \|*  
*mṛgāṇāṁ ca mṛgendro ’haṁ vainateyaś ca pakṣiṇām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. Among the demons, I am Prahlāda. Among controllers, I am time. Among animals, I am the lion. Among birds, I am Garuḍa.**

### Text 31

*pavanaḥ pavatām asmi rāmaḥ śastrabhṛtām aham \|*  
*jhaṣāṇāṁ makaraś cāsmi srotasām asmi jāhnavī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. Among purifiers and the swift, I am the wind. Among holders of weapons, I am the special** ***jīva*** **invested with powers, Paraśurāma. Among the fishes, I am the** ***makara*****. Among rivers, I am the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 32

*sargāṇām ādir antaś ca madhyaṁ caivāham arjuna \|*  
*adhyātma-vidyā vidyānāṁ vādaḥ pravadatām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. I am the creation, destruction and maintenance of all elements such as ether involved in creation. Among all types of knowledge, I am knowledge of the soul, i.e., knowledge of one’s** ***svarūpa*****. Among types of debate such as** ***jalpa, vitaṇḍa,*** **etc., for establishing one’s view and defeating other views, I am** ***vāda,*** **i.e., discernment of truth.**

### Text 33

*akṣarāṇām a-kāro ’smi dvandvaḥ sāmāsikasya ca \|*  
*aham evākṣayaḥ kālo dhātāhaṁ viśvato-mukhaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. I am the letter “a” among all letters. Among compounds (*****samāsas*****), I am the** ***dvandva*****. Among destroyers, I am great time, Śiva. Among creators, I am Brahmā.**

### Text 34

*mṛtyuḥ sarva-haraś cāham udbhavaś ca bhaviṣyatām \|*  
*kīrtiḥ śrīr vāk ca nārīṇāṁ smṛtir medhā dhṛtiḥ kṣamā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. Among things that takes away I am death. Among all transformations of the body, I am birth. Among women I am Kīrti, Śrī, Vāk, Smṛti, Medhā, Dhṛti and Kṣamā, and Dharma’s wives such as Mūrti.**

### Text 35

*bṛhat-sāma tathā sāmnāṁ gāyatrī chandasām aham \|*  
*māsānāṁ mārga-śīrṣo ’ham ṛtūnāṁ kusumākaraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. Among the Sāmas, I am the Bṛhat Sāma. Among meters, I am Gāyatrī. Among months, I am Agrahāyaṇa. Among the seasons, I am the spring.**

### Text 36

*dyūtaṁ chalayatām asmi tejas tejasvinām aham \|*  
*jayo ’smi vyavasāyo ’smi sattvaṁ sattvavatām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36. I am gambling in the cheaters. Among those who endeavor, I am the endeavor. Among conquerors, I am their victory and effort. Among the strong, I am their strength.**

### Text 37

*vṛṣṇīnāṁ vāsudevo ’smi pāṇḍavānāṁ dhanañjayaḥ \|*  
*munīnām apy ahaṁ vyāsaḥ kavīnām uśanā kaviḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**37. Among the Vṛṣṇis, I am Balarāma. Among the Pāṇḍavas, I am Arjuna. Among the sages, I am Vyāsa. Among the wise, I am Śukrācārya.**

### Text 38

*daṇḍo damayatām asmi nītir asmi jigīṣatām \|*  
*maunaṁ caivāsmi guhyānāṁ jñānaṁ jñānavatām aham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. I am punishment among those who punish. In those who desire victory, I am proper policy. In secrets, I am silence. I am knowledge in those who have knowledge.**

### Text 39

*yac cāpi sarva-bhūtānāṁ bījaṁ tad aham arjuna \|*  
*na tad asti vinā yat syān mayā bhūtaṁ carācaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. I am that seed which causes all living entities to manifest, because no moving or non-moving entities can exist without me.**

### Text 40

*nānto ’sti mama divyānāṁ vibhūtīnāṁ parantapa\|*  
*eṣa tūddeśataḥ prokto vibhūter vistaro mayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. There is no end to my wonderful** ***vibhūtis,*** **O afflicter of enemies. I have in name only declared to you my** ***vibhūtis*****.**

### Text 41

*yad yad vibhūtimat sattvaṁ śrīmad ūrjitam eva vā \|*  
*tat tad evāvagaccha tvaṁ mama tejo ’ṁśa-saṁbhavam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41. Whatever object displays great majesty, excellence, or strength, know that it is my** ***vibhūti*****. It has arisen from just a fragment of power of my** ***prakṛti*****.**

### Text 42

*atha vā bahunaitena kiṁ jñātena tavārjuna \|*  
*viṣṭabhyāham idaṁ kṛtsnam ekāṁśena sthito jagat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**42. O Arjuna! What more can I say? In brief, my prakṛti is endowed with all śakti . By its various powers I enter and exist in all the world. Entering into material existence by material power and entering into the world of jīvas by the power of the jīvas, I exist with a relation to this created world.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous chapter, pure bhakti to Kṛṣṇa was taught. There is a doubt. Can one serve Kṛṣṇa by worshipping the various devatās?  To remove this doubt, the Lord says in this chapter that the various devatās such as Brahmā and Śiva are his vibhūtis only. I am the origin of everything. I am unborn, without beginning and am the Lord of all beings. If one understands this while considering the vibhūtis in this way, there will be no obstacle to pure bhakti. By my one aṁśa known as Paramātmā, I enter the whole universe and manifest all these vibhūtis. Understanding my vibhūti-tattva, the devotees, attaining bhagavat-jñāna, worship me in the form of Kṛṣṇa with pure bhakti . In verses 8 9, 10 and 11 of this chapter, I speak of pure bhakti and the result of worship. Worship of Kṛṣṇa, who is the very form of the vibhūtis, allows one to attain prema, the nitya-dharma of the jīva. This is the essence of this chapter.

## Chapter Eleven - Viśvarūpa-darśana-yoga

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca—*  
*mad-anugrahāya paramaṁ guhyam adhyātma-saṁjñitam \|*  
*yat tvayoktaṁ vacas tena moho ’yaṁ vigato mama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. Arjuna said: Having heard your highest, secret teachings concerning** ***adhyātmā tattva,*** **my bewilderment is gone. Not knowing your spiritual, inconceivable, supreme position, I was overcome with illusion, thinking of** ***adhyātmā tattva*** **in opposite terms. Now I have clearly understood that you always have your** ***svarūpa*** **and the manifestation of the universal form is just one portion of your** ***svarūpa*****.**

### Text 2

*bhavāpyayau hi bhūtānāṁ śrutau vistaraśo mayā \|*  
*tvattaḥ kamala-patrākṣa māhātmyam api cāvyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. O lotus-eyed one! I have understood \[in detail\] both** ***tattvas:*** **your position related (*****sāmbandhika*****) to creation and destruction of all beings and your indestructible, great position related to your** ***svarūpa*** **(*****svarūpa-gata*****).**

### Text 3

*evam etad yathāttha tvam ātmānaṁ parameśvara \|*  
*draṣṭum icchāmi te rūpam aiśvaraṁ puruṣottama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. O Supreme Person! O Supreme Lord! I perceive your** ***svarūpa*** **in truth. But I want to see your powerful form, how you suddenly situate your** ***svarūpa*** **in this world at the time of creation.**

### Text 4

*manyase yadi tac chakyaṁ mayā draṣṭum iti prabho \|*  
*yogeśvara tato me tvaṁ darśayātmānam avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. The** ***jīva*** **is a particle of consciousness and thus cannot perceive completely the actions of the all-pervading consciousness. I am a** ***jīva,*** **but by your mercy, though I have attained qualification for your** ***svarūpa-tattva,*** **I cannot measure your form of power beyond the contemplation of the** ***jīva*****. You are the master of** ***yoga*** **and my master. \[By your** ***acintya-śakti,*****\] show me your power of** ***yoga*** **which by nature is indestructible and spiritual in** ***svarūpa*****.**

### Text 5

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*paśya me pārtha rūpāṇi śataśo ’tha sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*nānā-vidhāni divyāni nānā-varṇākṛtīni ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. The Lord said: O son of Pṛthā! See my power of** ***yoga*****—my hundreds and thousands of wonderful forms, of various types.**

### Text 6

*paśyādityān vasūn rudrān aśvinau marutas tathā\|*  
*bahūny adṛṣṭa-pūrvāṇi paśyāścaryāṇi bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. O descendant of Bharata! See all the Ādityas, the Vasus, the Rudras, the two Aśvins and the Maruts. Behold many astonishing forms not seen before.**

### Text 7

*ihaika-sthaṁ jagat kṛtsnaṁ paśyādya sa-carācaram \|*  
*mama dehe guḍākeśa yac cānyad draṣṭum icchasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. This whole universe with its moving and non-moving entities and whatever you desire to see are situated in my** ***svarūpa*** **of power. Thus , O conqueror of sleep, see all that in one part of my** ***svarūpa*** **of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 8

*na tu māṁ śakyase draṣṭum anenaiva sva-cakṣuṣā \|*  
*divyaṁ dadāmi te cakṣuḥ paśya me yogam aiśvaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. You are my devotee. Thus, you can see my** ***svarūpa*** **as Kṛṣṇa by your eye of unconditioned** ***prema*****. My form filled with powers of my** ***yoga*** **is** ***sāmbandhika*** **(related to this world). It is thus not necessary to see this with eyes of unconditioned** ***prema*****. The eye which sees gross matter cannot see my form of power. That eye which is not gross, but still covered by** ***upādhi*** **is called a** ***divya-cakṣu*****. I give you this** ***divya-cakṣu*****. By that you may see my form of power. Persons who have attained** ***divya-cakṣu*** **filled with logic have more affection naturally for the form of power covered by** ***upādhis*** **than my unconditioned form of Kṛṣṇa because their eye of unconditioned** ***prema*** **is closed.**

### Text 9

*sanjaya uvāca*  
*evam uktvā tato rājan mahā-yogeśvaro hariḥ \|*  
*darśayām āsa pārthāya paramaṁ rūpam aiśvaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. Sañjaya said to Dhṛtarāṣṭra: O King, having spoken thus, Kṛṣṇa, the great master of** ***yoga,*** **showed to Arjuna his supreme, form of power.**

### Text 10

*aneka-vaktra-nayanam anekādbhuta-darśanam \|*  
*aneka-divyābharaṇaṁ divyānekodyatāyudham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. This form was astonishing to see. It had many mouths and eyes, many wonderful ornaments, holding high many wondrous weapons.**

### Text 11

*divya-mālyāmbara-dharaṁ divya-gandhānulepanam \|*  
*sarvāścarya-mayaṁ devam anantaṁ viśvato-mukham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. That form wore astonishing garlands and dress, astonishing perfumes and ointments. {It was shining, greatly amazing, infinite, with faces in every direction.}/\[It was greatly amazing, with unlimited forms situated everywhere.\]**

### Text 12

*divi sūrya-sahasrasya bhaved yugapad utthitā \|*  
*yadi bhāḥ sadṛśī sā syād bhāsas tasya mahātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. If a thousand suns rose simultaneously, that effulgence would be somewhat similar to the effulgence of that great universal form.**

### Text 13

*tatraika-sthaṁ jagat kṛtsnaṁ pravibhaktam anekadhā \|*  
*apaśyad deva-devasya śarīre pāṇḍavas tadā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. Arjuna saw infinite universes — together in one place and divided in various ways — in the Lord’s body.**

### Text 14

*tataḥ sa vismayāviṣṭo hṛṣṭa-romā dhanañjayaḥ \|*  
*praṇamya śirasā devaṁ kṛtāñjalir abhāṣata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. Then, overcome with wonder, hairs standing on end, Arjuna, bowing his head before the Lord and folding his hands in prayer, spoke.**

### Text 15

*arjuna uvāca*  
*paśyāmi devāṁs tava deva dehe*  
*sarvāṁs tathā bhūta-viśeṣa-saṅghān \|*  
*brahmāṇam īśaṁ kamalāsana-stham*  
*ṛṣīṁś ca sarvān uragāṁś ca divyān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Arjuna said: O Lord! In your body I see all the** ***devatās,*** **as well as the totality of all the beings. {I see Brahmā, seated on a lotus, Śiva, all the sages, and snakes.}/\[I see the** ***antaryāmī*** **of Brahmā, seated on a lotus (i.e. Garbhodaśāyī), all the sages, and snakes.\]**

### Text 16

*aneka-bāhūdara-vaktra-netraṁ*  
*paśyāmi tvā sarvato ’nanta-rūpam \|*  
*nāntaṁ na madhyaṁ na punas tavādiṁ*  
*paśyāmi viśveśvara viśva-rūpa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. O lord of the universe! O universal form! I see in your body an infinite form spread everywhere, with many arms, stomachs, mouths and eyes. I do not see a beginning, middle or end of this form of yours.**

### Text 17

*kirīṭinaṁ gadinaṁ cakriṇaṁ ca*  
*tejo-rāśiṁ sarvato dīptimantam \|*  
*paśyāmi tvāṁ durnirīkṣyaṁ samantād*  
*dīptānalārka-dyutim aprameyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. I see your form in all directions with various crowns, clubs,** ***cakras*****—a mass of light shining everywhere, difficult to behold, radiant like the blazing of fire and sun, and immeasurable.**

### Text 18

*tvam akṣaraṁ paramaṁ veditavyaṁ*  
*tvam asya viśvasya paraṁ nidhānam \|*  
*tvam avyayaḥ śāśvata-dharma-goptā*  
*sanātanas tvaṁ puruṣo mato me \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. You are the supreme, indestructible** ***tattva*** **to be known. You are supreme shelter of this universe. You are indestructible, the protector of eternal dharma and the eternal person.**

### Text 19

*anādi-madhyāntam ananta-vīryam*  
*ananta-bāhuṁ śaśi-sūrya-netram \|*  
*paśyāmi tvāṁ dīpta-hutāśa-vaktraṁ*  
*sva-tejasā viśvam idaṁ tapantam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. You are without beginning, middle or end, possessed of infinite strength, infinite arms, with the sun and moon for your eyes, with a mouth of blazing fire, burning this universe with your heat.**

### Text 20

*dyāvā-pṛthivyor idam antaraṁ hi*  
*vyāptaṁ tvayaikena diśaś ca sarvāḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvādbhutaṁ rūpam idaṁ tavograṁ*  
*loka-trayaṁ pravyathitaṁ mahātman \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Though you are one, you are spread in the space between heaven and earth. I see this astonishing and ferocious form of yours. The three worlds have become terrified, O great soul.**

### Text 21

*amī hi tvā sura-saṅghā viśanti*  
*kecid bhītāḥ prāñjalayo gṛṇanti \|*  
*svastīty uktvā maharṣi-siddha-saṅghāḥ*  
*stuvantitvāṁ stutibhiḥ puṣkalābhiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. The host of** ***devatās*** **enter into you in surrender. Some in fear praise you with folded hands. Shouting “Svasti!” the host of great sages praises you with excellent verses.**

### Text 22

*rudrādityā vasavo ye ca sādhyā*  
*viśve ’śvinau marutaś coṣma-pāś ca \|*  
*gandharva-yakṣāsura-siddha-saṅghā*  
*vīkṣante tvāṁ vismitāś caiva sarve \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. The Rudras, Ādityas, Vasus, Sadhyas, Viśvadevas, Aśvins, Maruts,** ***pitṛs*****, Gandharvas, Yakṣas, Asuras, and the** ***siddhas*** **all look at you in astonishment.**

### Text 23

*rūpaṁ mahat te bahu-vaktra-netraṁ*  
*mahābāho bahu-bāhūru-pādam \|*  
*bahūdaraṁ bahu-daṁṣṭrā-karālaṁ*  
*dṛṣṭvā lokāḥ pravyathitās tathāham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. O mighty-armed one, seeing this form of yours with many mouths and eyes, many arms, many thighs and feet, many stomachs, fearful with many teeth, the world, like me, is filled with terror.**

### Text 24

*nabhaḥ-spṛśaṁ dīptam aneka-varṇaṁ*  
*vyāttānanaṁ dīpta-viśāla-netram \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā hi tvāṁ pravyathitāntar-ātmā*  
*dhṛtiṁ na vindāmi śamaṁ ca viṣṇo \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. O Viṣṇu, spread in the universe! Seeing you touch the heavens, shining in many colors, with gaping mouth, and wide, blazing eyes, \[becoming fearful and agitated\] I am unable to maintain composure and peaceful mind.**

### Text 25

*daṁṣṭrā-karālāni ca te mukhāni*  
*dṛṣṭvaiva kālānala-sannibhāni \|*  
*diśo na jāne na labhe ca śarma*  
*prasīda deveśa jagan-nivāsa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. Seeing your mouth with sharp teeth, like the fire of cosmic destruction, I cannot recognize the directions and I cannot determine how to feel comfort. O master of the** ***devas,*** **abode of the universe, be pleased with me!**

### Texts 26-27

*amī ca tvāṁ dhṛtarāṣṭrasya putrāḥ*  
*sarve sahaivāvani-pāla-saṅghaiḥ \|*  
*bhīṣmo droṇaḥ sūta-putras tathāsau*  
*sahāsmadīyair api yodha-mukhyaiḥ \|\|*  
*vaktrāṇi te tvaramāṇā viśanti*  
*daṁṣṭrākarālāni bhayānakāni \|*  
*kecid vilagnā daśanāntareṣu*  
*sandṛśyante cūrṇitair uttamāṅgaiḥ \|\|*

**26-27. All these sons of Dhṛtarāṣṭra along with the host of rulers of the earth, Bhīṣma, Droṇa and Karṇa, together with our prominent warriors, enter swiftly into your fearful mouths with sharp teeth. Some, with their heads ground up, are stuck between your teeth.**

*yathā nadīnāṁ bahavo ’mbu-vegāḥ*  
*samudram evābhimukhā dravanti \|*  
*tathā tavāmī nara-loka-vīrā*  
*viśanti vaktrāṇy abhivijvalanti \|\|*

*tavāpi vaktrāṇi samṛddha-vegāḥ \|\|*

*lelihyase grasamānaḥ samantāl*  
*lokān samagrān vadanair jvaladbhiḥ \|*  
*tejobhir āpūrya jagat samagraṁ*  
*bhāsas tavogrāḥ pratapanti viṣṇo \|\|*

*ākhyāhi me ko bhavān ugra-rūpo*

*na hi prajānāmi tava pravṛttim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. Tell me who you are, with this terrifying form. O best of the** ***devas,*** **I offer respects to you. Be pleased. I do not understand your purpose. I desire to know you in detail.**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*kālo ’smi loka-kṣaya-kṛt pravṛddho*

**32. The Lord said: I have manifested as time, for destroying the increased worlds. I will destroy all these enemy warriors. You are not the doer in this act of destruction. I am the doer.**

**32. The Lord said: I have manifested as increased time, for destroying the worlds. I will destroy all warriors on both sides, excluding the Pāṇḍavas.**

### Text 33

*tasmāt tvam uttiṣṭha yaśo labhasva*  
*jitvā śatrūn bhuṅkṣva rājyaṁ samṛddham \|*  
*mayaivaite nihatāḥ pūrvam eva*  
*nimitta-mātraṁ bhava savyasācin \|\|*

**33. Since this destruction does not depend on you, standing to fight, you should enjoy a prosperous kingdom and gain fame born of victory. I will destroy everyone. O Arjuna! You should become just an instrument for that.**

*droṇaṁ ca bhīṣmaṁ ca jayadrathaṁ ca*

*yudhyasva jetāsi raṇe sapatnān \|\|*

### Text 35

*sañjaya uvāca*  
*etac chrutvā vacanaṁ keśavasya*  
*kṛtāñjalir vepamānaḥ kirīṭī \|*  
*namaskṛtvā bhūya evāha kṛṣṇaṁ*  
*sa-gadgadaṁ bhīta-bhītaḥ praṇamya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. Sañjaya said to Dhṛtarāṣṭra: O King! Hearing all the words of the Lord, Arjuna, frightened, with folded hands, trembling, offered his respects repeatedly and spoke to Kṛṣṇa in a faltering voice.**

### Text 36

*arjuna uvāca*  
*sthāne hṛṣīkeśa tava prakīrtyā*  
*jagat prahṛṣyaty anurajyate ca \|*  
*rakṣāṁsi bhītāni diśo dravanti*  
*sarve namasyanti ca siddha-saṅghāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36. Arjuna said: O controller of the senses, the world rejoices on hearing your glorification and becomes attracted to you. Rightly also, the Rākṣasas, frightened, flee in all directions, and the** ***siddhas*** **bow down to you. This is suitable action on their part.**

### Text 37

*kasmāc ca te na nameran mahātman*  
*garīyase brahmaṇo ’py ādi-kartre \|*  
*ananta deveśa jagan-nivāsa*  
*tvam akṣaraṁ sad asat tatparaṁ yat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**37*****.*** **O great soul! You are the best. Why should they not offer respects to you, the first creator, superior to Brahmā? O infinite one, Lord of the** ***devas,*** **abode of the universe, you are the infallible, above cause and effect.**

**37. O great soul! You are superior to Brahmā and are the original creator. Why should they not offer respects to you? O infinite one, Lord of the** ***devas*****, abode of the universe, you are superior to** ***prakṛti*** **a form of cause and effect (*****sad asat*****) and to the** ***akṣara jīva-tattva*****.**

### Text 38

*tvam ādi-devaḥ puruṣaḥ purāṇas*  
*tvam asya viśvasya paraṁ nidhānam \|*  
*vettāsi vedyaṁ ca paraṁ ca dhāma*  
*tvayā tataṁ viśvam ananta-rūpa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. You are the original** ***deva,*** **the eternal person, {the final place for the universe after destruction}/\[the only shelter for this universe\]. You are the knower, the object of knowledge, the {form}/\[abode\] beyond the** ***guṇas*** **\[known as Paravyoma\]. O infinite form! This whole universe is pervaded by you.**

### Text 39

*vāyur yamo ’gnir varuṇaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ*  
*prajāpatis tvaṁ prapitāmahaś ca \|*  
*namo namas te ’stu sahasra-kṛtvaḥ*  
*punaś ca bhūyo ’pi namo namas te \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. You are Vāyu, Yama, Agni, Varuṇa, Candra, {Prajāpati, and Brahmā}/\[and the** ***prajāpati*** **Brahmā\]. I offer respects to you a thousand times, and again I offer respects to you.**

### Text 40

*namaḥ purastād atha pṛṣṭhatas te*  
*namo ’stu te sarvata eva sarva*  
*ananta-vīryāmita-vikramas tvaṁ*  
*sarvaṁ samāpnoṣi tato ’si sarvaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. I offer respects from the front, from the back, from all sides. O person with infinite power! You are endowed with immeasurable** ***śakti*****. You pervade the universe and therefore you are everything.**

### Texts 41-42

*sakheti matvā prasabhaṁ yad uktaṁ*  
*he kṛṣṇa he yādava he sakheti \|*  
*ajānatā mahimānaṁ tavedaṁ*  
*mayā pramādāt praṇayena vāpi \|\|*  
*yac cāvahāsārtham asat-kṛto ’si*  
*vihāra-śayyāsana-bhojaneṣu \|*  
*eko ’tha vāpy acyuta tat-samakṣaṁ*  
*tat kṣāmaye tvām aham aprameyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41-42. I have addressed you, “O Kṛṣṇa, O Yādava, O friend,” according to social etiquette, since I was not aware of your greatness related to the universal form. Thus I have uttered all this out of negligence. Joking with you while playing, resting and eating, I have disrespected you. Sometimes alone and sometimes with friends, I have done this. Please forgive these thousands of offenses.**

### Text 43

*na tvat-samo ’sty abhyadhikaḥ kuto ’nyo \|*  
*loka-traye ’py apratima-prabhāva \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**43. You are the father of the world. You are most deserving of worship and the principal teacher. No one is equal to you, what to speak of being greater than you. You have unequalled power in the three worlds.**

### Text 44

*tasmāt praṇamya praṇidhāya kāyaṁ*  
*prasādaye tvām aham īśam īḍyam \|*  
*piteva putrasya sakheva sakhyuḥ*  
*priyaḥ priyāyārhasi deva soḍhum \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**44. You indeed are the Lord of the** ***jīvas*** **and the most worthy of service. Bowing down, prostrating my body, I request that you be pleased. The** ***jīva*** **and you are eternally bound in relationships of friendship, parental affection and conjugal** ***rasa*****. You should mercifully accept that the** ***jīvas,*** **eternally your servants, treat you as an equal in their relationships.**

### Text 45

*adṛṣṭa-pūrvaṁ hṛṣito ’smi dṛṣṭvā*  
*bhayena ca pravyathitaṁ mano me \|*  
*tad eva me darśaya deva rūpaṁ*  
*prasīda deveśa jagan-nivāsa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**45. Seeing this universal form which was not seen before, my curiosity has been satisfied. But it does not give joy to the minds and eyes of the devotees. Therefore, seeing this, my mind is disturbed with fear. O abode of the universe! O lord of the** ***devatās!*** **Show me your** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form with four arms.**

### Text 46

*kirīṭinaṁ gadinaṁ cakra-hastam*  
*icchāmi tvāṁ draṣṭum ahaṁ tathaiva \|*  
*tenaiva rūpeṇa catur-bhujena*  
*sahasra-bāho bhava viśva-mūrte \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**46. Now I wish to see your four-armed form, having a crown on the head, holding in the hand club,** ***cakra*** **and other weapons. From that form alone you manifest this universal form with a thousand arms when you are situated in the universe.**

**O Kṛṣṇa! Without doubt I have understood that your** ***sac-cid- ānanda*** **form with two arms is the supreme** ***tattva,*** **attractive to all** ***jīvas*** **and eternal. Your four-armed form of Nārāyaṇa, a form for pastimes of power manifested from the two-armed form, exists eternally. When the universe is created, the universal form arises from this four-armed form. My curiosity has been satisfied by this supreme knowledge.**

### Text 47

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*mayā prasannena tavārjunedaṁ*  
*rūpaṁ paraṁ darśitam ātma-yogāt \|*  
*tejo-mayaṁ viśvam anantam ādyaṁ*  
*yan me tvad-anyena na dṛṣṭa-pūrvam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**47. The Lord said: O Arjuna! Being pleased with you, I have shown to you this best form included within the material universe by my power. No one except you has previously seen this unlimited, original, brilliant form.**

### Text 48

*na veda-yajñādhyayanair na dānair*  
*na ca kriyābhir na tapobhir ugraiḥ \|*  
*evaṁ-rūpaḥ śakya ahaṁ nṛ-loke*  
*draṣṭuṁ tvad-anyena kuru-pravīra \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**48. O hero of the Kurus! No one, by study of the Vedas, by sacrifice, by charities, by rituals, or by severe austerities, has seen this universal form generated from my power in this world. Only you have seen this form.**

***Jīvas*** **who attain the status of** ***devatās*** **can see and remember this celestial form with celestial eyes and celestial minds. Those who are bound to foolish perception in this material world cannot see this celestial form. But my devotees surpassing foolishness and celestial status, are situated in my eternal spiritual** ***tattva*****. Thus, like you, seeing this universal form they are not satisfied. They desire to see my eternal form which is spiritual.**

### Text 49

*mā te vyathā mā ca vimūḍha-bhāvo*  
*dṛṣṭvā rūpaṁ ghoram īdṛṅ mamedam \|*  
*vyapeta-bhīḥ prīta-manāḥ punas tvaṁ*  
*tad eva me rūpam idaṁ prapaśya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**49. You should not become bewildered or confused on seeing this terrifying form. My devotees love peace and favor my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form. Their hearts become pained on seeing this fierce form of mine. But foolish people respect meditation on this universal form. Thus, so that you do not experience pain and bewilderment of the universal form, I give you a blessing. My sweet devotees need not relate to this universal form.**

**But you are my friend who nourishes my pastimes. You must be an assistant in all my pastimes. Such pain is not suitable for you. Thus giving up fear, being affectionate, see my eternal form.**

### Text 50

*sañjaya uvāca*  
*ity arjunaṁ vāsudevas tathoktvā*  
*svakaṁ rūpaṁ darśayām āsa bhūyaḥ \|*  
*āśvāsayām āsa ca bhītam enaṁ*  
*bhūtvā punaḥ saumyavapur mahātmā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**50. Sañjaya said to Dhṛtarāṣṭra: Having spoken to Arjuna thus, the great soul Vāsudeva showed him his personal four-handed form. Then again, appearing in his two-handed form, magnanimous Kṛṣṇa gave comfort to the fearful Arjuna.**

### Text 51

*arjuna uvāca*  
*dṛṣṭvedaṁ mānuṣaṁ rūpaṁ tava saumyaṁ janārdana \|*  
*idānīm asmi saṁvṛttaḥ sa-cetāḥ prakṛtiṁ gataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**51. Seeing the sweetest two-armed form, Arjuna said: O Janārdana, seeing this pleasant human form of yours, I have gained steadiness of heart and the nature of a devotee.**

### Text 52

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*su-durdarśam idaṁ rūpaṁ dṛṣṭavān asi yan mama \|*  
*devā apy asya rūpasya nityaṁ darśana-kāṅkṣiṇaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**52. The Lord said: O Arjuna! This form of mine which you have just seen is very difficult to see. Even the** ***devatās*** **such as Brahmā and Śiva are always eager to see this eternal form.**

**If you say that since everyone sees this human form, how is it difficult to see, I will speak the truth about this. Please listen.**

**There are three types of perception in people who see my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form of Kṛṣṇa: knowledgeable perception, ignorant perception and logical perception. (1) By foolish, ignorant perception, people accept my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **as temporary and as taking shelter of material qualities. They cannot understand the supreme position of my** ***svarūpa*****.**

**(2) By logic or celestial perception, those who think they have knowledge and think that impersonal Brahman, a state opposite of the universe, think that this form takes shelter of material qualities. Or by celestial perception,** ***devatās,*** **accepting that this perception is temporary, think that the universal form, which spreads throughout the universe, is the eternal** ***tattva*****. They conclude that my human form is just some means for worship.**

**But (3) by knowledgeable perception, the devotees endowed with spiritual eyes, attain direct realization of me, as a human form which is the abode of** ***sac-cid-ānanda*****. This direct vision is rare even for** ***devatās*****. Among the** ***devatās,*** **Brahmā and Śiva are my devotees. Thus, they desire to see this form.**

**Since you have taken shelter of pure** ***sakhya-bhakti,*** **by my mercy you, having seen the universal form, can know the superiority of my eternal form.**

### Text 53

*nāhaṁ vedair na tapasā na dānena na cejyayā \|*  
*śakya evaṁ-vidho draṣṭuṁ dṛṣṭavān asi māṁ yathā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**53. Not through the Vedas, not through austerities, not through charities, nor through performance of sacrifices, is it possible to see that eternal human form that you saw through** ***vijñāna*****.**

### Text 54

*bhaktyā tv ananyayā śakya aham evaṁ-vidho ’rjuna \|*  
*jñātuṁ draṣṭuṁ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṁ ca paraṅtapa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**54. Only by exclusive** ***bhakti*** **it is possible to know me, see me, or directly meet me, O Arjuna.**

### Text 55

*mat-karma-kṛn mat-paramo mad-bhaktaḥ saṅga-varjitaḥ \|*  
*nirvairaḥ sarva-bhūteṣu yaḥ sa mām eti pāṇḍava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**55. He who serves me without deceit, and who, devoid of desire for results of karma and jñāna, sees bhakti in all activities, and who is merciful to all beings, attains me in my svarūpa as Kṛṣṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

In this chapter, it is shown that the form of Kṛṣṇa, more than the form of Viṣṇu, time or the viśva-rūpa should be taken shelter of. Except the svarūpa form the devotee has no need for all the relative forms. The form of Kṛṣṇa alone which is an ocean of all sweet rasas is the only shelter of the highest sweet bhāva. That is the essence of this chapter.

## Chapter Twelve - Bhakti-yoga

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca*  
*evaṁ satata-yuktā ye bhaktās tvāṁ paryupāsate \|*  
*ye cāpy akṣaram avyaktaṁ teṣāṁ ke yoga-vittamāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. Arjuna said: O Kṛṣṇa! In all that you have taught me I have understood that there are two types of** ***yogīs*****. One type of** ***yogī,*** **binding all physical and social activities to the chains of dependence on exclusive** ***bhakti*****, worships you with pure** ***bhakti*****. The other type of** ***yogī,*** **after accepting as necessary all physical and social activities by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga,*** **takes support of your** ***adhyātmika-yoga*****, having unmanifest and indestructible** ***svarūpa*****. Which is the better of these two types of** ***yogīs?***

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*mayy āveśya mano ye māṁ nitya-yuktā upāsate \|*  
*śraddhayā parayopetās te me yuktatamā matāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. The Lord said: The devotee who, with the assistance of** ***nirguṇa*** **faith, makes his whole life full of** ***bhakti*** **and absorbs his mind in me is best of all** ***yogīs*****.**

### Texts 3-4

*ye tv akṣaram anirdeśyam avyaktaṁ paryupāsate \|*  
*sarvatra-gam acintyaṁ ca kūṭa-stham acalaṁ dhruvam \|\|*  
*sanniyamyendriya-grāmaṁ sarvatra sama-buddhayaḥ \|*  
*te prāpnuvanti mām eva sarva-bhūta-hite ratāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3-4. But those who worship the indestructible, indescribable, unmanifest, all-pervading, inconceivable, unchanging, unmoving, fixed and impersonal** ***svarūpa,*** **by regulating their senses, looking on all things equally, being intent on the welfare of all beings, also attain me \[in a form predominated by powers,\] after much difficulty. There is no object to be worshipped except me. Thus by whatever means one strives for the highest entity, the person attains me.**

### Text 5

*kleśo ’dhikataras teṣām avyaktāsakta-cetasām \|*  
*avyaktā hi gatir duḥkhaṁ dehavadbhir avāpyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. The difference between the jñāna-yogī and bhakti-yogī is that the bhakti-yogī, after cultivating the supreme entity easily at the time of practice, attains the supreme entity at the time of results without fear.**

**Комментарий:**

The *jñāna-yogī* fixed always on the unmanifest *tattva,* experiences difficulty in contemplating the opposite of matter at the time of practice. Contemplation of the opposite, contemplating the opposite of normal perception, produces distress for the *jīva*. At the time of results, there is no fearlessness. As he cannot recognize my eternal *svarūpa* before surpassing *sādhana,* even his final goal produces distress.

The *jīva* is an eternal, conscious entity. If he merges into an unmanifest state, his designated state is destroyed. If his real *svarūpa,* manifests, then while giving up the opposite of his *svarūpa,* identification with the Lord, there is difficulty. The *jīva* who starts meditation on the unmanifest, because of having a body, attains result in the form of distress during *sādhana* and on attaining the goal.

Actually, the *jīva* has a conscious *svarūpa* and is characterized by a spiritual body. One should know that the unmanifest state is contrary to the *jīva’s svarūpa* and produces distress. *Bhakti-yoga* produces auspiciousness for the *jīva*. When *jñāna* becomes independent of *bhakti,* it produces inauspiciousness everywhere. Thus the *adhyātmā-yoga* accomplished after worshipping a formless, changeless, all-pervading, impersonal *svarūpa* is not recommended.

### Texts 6-7

*ye tu sarvāṇi karmāṇi mayi sannyasya mat-parāḥ \|*  
*ananyenaiva yogena māṁ dhyāyanta upāsate \|\|*  
*teṣām ahaṁ samuddhartā mṛtyu-saṁsāra-sāgarāt \|*  
*bhavāmi na cirāt pārtha mayy āveśita-cetasām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6-7*****.*** **I quickly deliver from the ocean of deathly** ***saṁsāra*** **those whose minds are absorbed in me, who worship and meditate upon my eternal form by exlusive** ***bhatki-yoga*** **related to me, who take support of my** ***svarūpa*** **as Bhagavān making all physical and social activities completely subservient to** ***bhakti*** **i.e., I free them from** ***māyā’s saṁsāra*** **in their bound state and when the bondage of** ***māyā*** **is destroyed, I protect them from the death in the form of the conception that they are one with Brahman.**

**Those who are attached to the unmanifest attain no help from thinking of nondifference. This (helplessness) is the cause of inauspiciousness. I promise that “as people surrender to me, I respond to them”. By this it must be understood that the unmanifest** ***svarūpa*** **of those who meditate on the unmanifest, merges into me. What is the harm to me? But on attaining his goal, the** ***jīva*** **absorbed in nondifference remains far from the excellence of his** ***svarūpa*****.**

### Text 8

*mayy eva mana ādhatsva mayi buddhiṁ niveśaya \|*  
*nivasiṣyasi mayy eva ata ūrdhvaṁ na saṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Concentrating your mind on my eternal** ***svarūpa*** **as Bhagavān, remember me. Engage your intelligence, which discriminates, upon me. Become situated in the** ***tattva*** **of Bhagavān. By that you will attain unconditioned** ***prema,*** **the highest result of** ***sādhana-bhakti*****.**

### Text 9

*atha cittaṁ samādhātuṁ na śaknoṣi mayi sthiram \|*  
*abhyāsa-yogena tato mām icchāptuṁ dhanañjaya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. Know that what I have said about unconditioned** ***prema*** **to be a matter of the** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **fixed on me. When doing** ***sādhana,*** **it is necessary to repeatedly practice. If you are unable to concentrate your mind fully on me, then it is better that you repeatedly practice.**

**9. If you are unable to concentrate your mind fully on me with natural attraction, then it is better that you make efforts to attain me by repeated practice according to rules.**

**Комментарий:**

The meaning is this. The sādhana for prema, the highest puruṣārtha is of two types: rāga-mārga and vidhi-mārga. The sādhana based on greed for the activities of the rāgātmika devotees on seeing them is called rāgānuga-bhakti . Sādhana performed by firm faith (in rules) is called vaidhī bhakti. Those who do not have natural rāga should perform vaidhi-bhakti sādhana.

### Text 10

*abhyāse ’py asamartho ’si mat-karma-paramo bhava \|*  
*mad-artham api karmāṇi kurvan siddhim avāpsyasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. If you cannot repeatedly practice, then {offer your** ***karmas*** **to me}/\[become dedicated to activities related to me\]. Doing that, gradually you will attain practice and then finally achieve perfection in the form of steadiness of the heart fixed on my** ***tattva*** **endowed with qualities.**

### Text 11

*athaitad apy aśakto ’si kartuṁ mad-yogam āśritaḥ \|*  
*sarva-karma-phala-tyāgaṁ tataḥ kuru yatātmavān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. If you cannot offer** ***karmas*** **to me, then while controlling your mind, give up the results of the** ***karmas*****.**

**11. If you are unable to perform activities related to me, then while controlling your mind, perform Vedic** ***karmas*** **while giving up all the results.**

### Text 12

*śreyo hi jñānam abhyāsāj jñānād dhyānaṁ viśiṣyate \|*  
*dhyānāt karma-phala-tyāgas tyāgāc chāntir anantaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. {O Arjuna! The method of attaining unconditioned** ***prema*** **is only** ***sādhana-bhakti*****. That** ***bhakti-yoga*** **is of two types: fixing the** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **on the Lord and performing external activities. There are three types of** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **actions: filled with remembrance (*****dhyāna*****), filled with contemplation (*****jñāna*****) and filled with repeated practice (*****abhyāsa*****). But those who have dull minds find these three internal types of service difficult. The activities of the external senses such as hearing and chanting are the external actions. They are easy for everyone.**

**Contemplation or intelligence fixed on me is excellent knowledge. It is superior to practice. After practicing with effort, one can meditate but when contemplation, the result of practice, arises, meditation is easily accomplished. Meditation is superior to knowledge (contemplation) since when meditation is steady, the happiness of Svarga or desire for the happiness of liberation are removed. When these two desires are given up, peace, in the form of detachment from sense objects except my form and qualities, manifests.}**

**\[Those who are incapable should take shelter of practice in the form of** ***vaidha-bhakti*** **rather than** ***rāga-bhakti*****. If one is incapable of** ***vaidha-bhakti,*** **then activities of** ***jñāna*** **in the form of the true nature of** ***ātmā,*** **are better. If he is incapable of such** ***jñāna*****, he should perform meditation on his** ***ātmā*** **as its** ***sādhana*****, with words like “I am that” (*****tat tvam asi*****). For a person who is incapable of such meditation, performance of** ***karma-yoga*** **is better. By renouncing the results of** ***karmas*** **a person performing** ***kāmya-karmas*** **attains peace. The purport is this. There are two paths for attaining pure bhakti: the direct path and the gradual path. When greed and faith arise,** ***sādhana*** **with hearing and chanting through association of devotees is the direct path.**

**The gradual path is the ordinary method. First one gives up** ***kāmya-karmas.*** **Second, one takes shelter of** ***karma-yoga*****. Third, one meditates as a part of** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*****. Fourth, one attains true knowledge of** ***ātmā*****. Fifth, one attains** ***sādhana-bhakti*** **arising from true knowledge of Paramātmā.\]**

### Texts 13-14

*adveṣṭā sarva-bhūtānāṁ maitraḥ karuṇa eva ca \|*  
*nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ sama-duḥkha-sukhaḥ kṣamī \|\|*  
*santuṣṭaḥ satataṁ yogī yatātmā dṛḍha-niścayaḥ \|*  
*mayy arpita-mano-buddhir yo mad-bhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13-14. The {*****śānta*****}** ***bhakta*** **is naturally without hatred towards any being i.e., if anyone hates him, he does not hate them. Rather, he is friendly towards all. He is merciful, thinking how to save the** ***jīvas*** **wandering on the wrong path from a bad destination. He is non-possessive of the material body or devoid of** ***ahaṅkāra*****. Though harassed by others he is not agitated due to considering it to be the result of** ***prārabdha*****. Thus, he is tolerant. Maintaining his body with what comes of its own accord, he is always satisfied. He is fixed in** ***yoga,*** **in the form of steadiness directed towards the desired goal, following the rules of the method. With firm conviction, he tries at all times to attain unconditioned** ***prema*****. \[He whose mind and intelligence are offered to me in this way is my devotee and dear to me.\]**

### Text 15

*yasmān nodvijate loko lokān nodvijate ca yaḥ \|*  
*harṣāmarṣa-bhayodvegair mukto yaḥ sa ca me priyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. {The** ***śānta-bhakta*****}/\[He\] who does not agitate others, nor is agitated by others, who is free from material emotions such as joy, anger, fear and anxiety, is dear to me.**

### Text 16

*anapekṣaḥ śucir dakṣa udāsīno gata-vyathaḥ \|*  
*sarvārambha-parityāgī yo mad-bhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. My devotee, who is devoid of expectations from his work, is clean, skillful in all situations, detached from other persons, without distress, and who devoid of desire for results in all undertakings, is dear to me.**

### Text 17

*yo na hṛṣyati na dveṣṭi na śocati na kāṅkṣati \|*  
*śubhāśubha-parityāgī bhaktimān yaḥ sa me priyaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. The devotee who neither hopes to attain material results or rejoices in it, neither laments nor hankers on not attaining material results, who gives up all sinful and pious actions, is dear to me.**

### Texts 18-19

*samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca tathā mānāpamānayoḥ \|*  
*śītoṣṇa-sukha-duḥkheṣu samaḥ saṅga-vivarjitaḥ \|\|*  
*tulya-nindā-stutir maunī santuṣṭo yena kenacit \|*  
*aniketaḥ sthira-matir bhaktimān me priyo naraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18-19. Being equal to enemy and friend, being equal in respect or disrespect, in cold or heat, happiness or distress, being free from attachments, being equal in criticism or praise, silent, satisfied with whatever is there, unattached to house, and steady in mind, having attained all these, a devotee is naturally dear to me.**

### Text 20

*ye tu dharmāmṛtam idaṁ yathoktaṁ paryupāsate \|*  
*śraddadhānā mat-paramā bhaktās te ’tīva me priyāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. But those who honor the sweet** ***dharma*** **described by me \[from the second verse of this chapter,\] with faith in me, are my devotees and most dear to me.**

**{*****Jīvas*** **should take shelter of this path of progress explained by me. By this path of gradual development, the** ***jīva*** **will attain unconditioned** ***prema*****.}**

**Which is the better of the two:** ***nirviśeṣa-vāda*** **or** ***saviśeṣa-vāda?*** **To resolve this doubt, in this chapter it is concluded that those who search for me on the impersonal path, being free of material** ***viśeṣa*** **(qualities) by** ***karma-yoga,*** **containing in it meditation, explained in the first six chapters, wandering about on a very difficult path, and after attaining association with pure devotees by a desire to benefit all beings, they give up attempts at impersonalism (*****nirviśeṣa*****) and finally attain me endowed with spiritual viśeṣa (qualities). Those who, developing faith by devotee-association, taking shelter of guru, attaining** ***niṣṭhā, ruci, āsakti*** **and** ***bhāva*** **by** ***sādhana-bhakti*** **of hearing and chanting etc., become absorbed in me. Their path is proper. Therefore, pure** ***bhakti*** **is better.**

**Комментарий:**

Till one has attains devotee-association, the path of karma-yoga previously mentioned is recommended. In that, by karma-yoga,  meditation, and ātma-jñāna,  
gradually bhakti will arise after knowledge of Paramātmā. The gradual path is not necessary for those who, by devotee association, develop faith in topics of the Lord and a greed for the pastimes of the greatest devotees. After taking support of bhakti-yoga described in the second six chapters, they attain all success. They maintain their lives by the pure method indicated in bhakti and I personally assist them. This is the purport of this chapter.

## Chapter Thirteen - Prakṛti-puruṣa-viveka-yoga. Distinction of Matter and the Lord.

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca*  
*prakṛtiṁ puruṣaṁ caiva kṣetraṁ kṣetra-jñam eva ca*  
*etad veditum icchāmi jñānaṁ jñeyaṁ ca keśava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. {Arjuna said: O Keśava, I desire to know about** ***prakṛti, puruṣa*****, the field and knower of the field, as well as the process of knowing and the object of knowledge.}**

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*idaṁ śarīraṁ kaunteya kṣetram ity abhidhīyate \|*  
*etad yo vetti taṁ prāhuḥ kṣetra-jña iti tad-vidaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2*****.*** **{The Lord said:}/\[Kṛṣṇa said to Arjuna who asked about** ***prakṛti, puruṣa, kṣetra, kṣetra-jña, jñāna*** **and** ***jñeya*****:\]**

**O Arjuna! In order to teach you clearly about the tattva of** ***bhakti,*** **whose** ***svarūpa*** **is the highest secret, first I explained all about** ***karma*** **of the bound** ***jīva*** **and the** ***svarūpa*** **of** ***ātmā*****. I also spoke about the** ***svarūpa*** **of pure** ***bhakti*****. By that the deliberation of the three processes of** ***karma, jñāna*** **and** ***bhakti*** **has been completed. I will now discuss special renunciation and** ***jñāna*** **by deliberation on** ***vijñāna*****. Because of hearing this, you will become strongly fixed in the** ***tattva*** **of pure** ***bhakti*****.**

**When I spoke the four root verses of** ***Bhāgavatam*** **to Brahmā, then by the following statement, I gave teachings on** ***jñāna, vijñāna, rahasya*** **(*****prema,*** **the** ***prayojana*****) and** ***tad-aṅga*** **(*****sādhana-bhakti,*** **the** ***abhidheya*****):**

***jñānaṁ me paramaṁ guhyaṁ yad vijñāna-samanvitaṁ \|***  
***sa-rahasyaṁ tad-aṇgaṁ ca gṛhāṇa gaditaṁ mayā \|\|***

**If one does not understand these four topics thoroughly, then the** ***rahasya*** **will not arise. While teaching** ***vijñāna*** **to you, I offer intelligence suitable for the** ***rahasya*****. When pure** ***bhakti*** **arises, causeless** ***jñāna*** **and** ***vairagya*** **will naturally arise.**

**While undertaking** ***bhakti,*** **you should realize these two secondary results. O Kaunteya! The body is called** ***kṣetra*** **(field). He who knows this field is called** ***kṣetra-jña*** **(the knower of the field).**

### Text 3

*kṣetra-jñaṁ cāpi māṁ viddhi sarva-kṣetreṣu bhārata \|*  
*kṣetra-kṣetrajñayor jñānaṁ yat taj jñānaṁ mataṁ mama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. In deliberation on the field and knower of the field one can see three** ***tattvas:*** **the Lord (*****īśvara*****), the** ***jīva*** **and matter. Just as in each body there is one knower of the field, the** ***jīva*****, know that in the same way the chief knower in the whole material universe is the** ***īśvara*****. I am the knower of all fields in the form of Paramātmā through my controlling** ***śakti*****. Those who, deliberating on the** ***tattvas*** **of the field and knower of the field, understand the three** ***tattvas*** **have knowledge which is called** ***vijñāna*****.**

### Text 4

*tat kṣetraṁ yac ca yādṛk ca yad-vikāri yataś ca yat \|*  
*sa ca yo yat-prabhāvaś ca tat samāsena me śṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. What is that field, what are its types, what are its transformations, where does it come from, who is the knower of the field and what is his influence? I will speak on this briefly. Please listen.**

### Text 5

*ṛṣibhir bahudhā gītaṁ chandobhir vividhaiḥ pṛthak \|*  
*brahma-sūtra-padaiś caiva hetumadbhir viniścitaiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. {The** ***tattva*** **of the field}/\[The true nature of the field and its knower\] has been described by many** ***ṛṣis*** **in the** ***smṛti*** **scriptures. It is described in many ways here and there by many verses of the** ***Vedas.*** **This** ***tattva*** **was defined by the** ***Vedānta-sūtras*** **in conclusive statements with logic.**

### Texts 6-7

*mahā-bhūtāny ahaṅkāro buddhir avyaktam eva ca \|*  
*indriyāṇi daśaikaṁ ca pañca cendriya-gocarāḥ \|\|*  
*icchā dveṣaḥ sukhaṁ duḥkhaṁ saṅghātaś cetanā dhṛtiḥ \|*  
*etat kṣetraṁ samāsena sa-vikāram udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6-7. From the statements of sages, of the** ***Vedas*** **and** ***Vedānta-sūtras*** **twenty-four** ***tattvas*** **have been understood to make the field:**

**The five gross elements — earth, water, fire, air, ether;** ***ahaṅkāra*****, {*****mahat-tattva,*** **and its cause** ***prakṛti*** **(*****avyakta*****)}/\[intelligence and** ***pradhāna*** **(*****avyakta*****) composed of the three** ***guṇas*****\], the eye, ear, nose, tongue, and skin and others such as voice, hands and feet of the ten external senses, the internal sense called the mind and the five sense objects form, taste, smell, touch and sound.**

**Reviewing these twenty-four** ***tattvas,*** **one knows the nature of the field. The transformations—desire, hatred, happiness, suffering, the conglomerate (*****saṅghāta*****) called the functions of the body, which is a transformation of the five gross elements, {mental functions (*****cetanā*****) which are an** ***ābhāsa*** **of consciousness}/\[subtle-body’s functions, which are filled with knowledge, and are the basis of the** ***jīva*** **having a conscious** ***svarūpa*****\], and determination are known as the transformations of the field. These are also included in the field.**

### Texts 8-12

*amānitvam adambhitvam ahiṁsā kṣāntir ārjavam \|*  
*ācāryopāsanaṁ śaucaṁ sthairyam ātma-vinigrahaḥ \|\|*  
*indriyārtheṣu vairāgyam anahaṅkāra eva ca \|*  
*janma-mṛtyu-jarā-vyādhi-duḥkha-doṣānudarśanam \|\|*  
*asaktir anabhiṣvaṅgaḥ putra-dāra-gṛhādiṣu \|*  
*nityaṁ ca sama-cittatvam iṣṭāniṣṭopapattiṣu \|\|*  
*mayi cānanya-yogena bhaktir avyabhicāriṇī \|*  
*vivikta-deśa-sevitvam aratir jana-saṁsadi \|\|*  
*adhyātma-jñāna-nityatvaṁ tattva-jñānārtha-darśanam \|*  
*etaj jñānam iti proktam ajñānaṁ yad ato ’nyathā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8-12. Freedom from pride; lack of ostentation; non-violence; forbearance; sincerity; worship of the teacher or serving guru; purity; steadiness; control of the mind; detachment from enjoyment of sense objects; lack of identification with body; understanding that all states of existence such as birth, death, old age and disease give rise to suffering; non-attachment, in other words no attachment to sons, etc., and indifference to the happiness and suffering of sons, etc.; equanimity of mind in the face of desirable or undesirable events; devotion to me with undeviating attention; resorting to solitary places with distaste for places with crowds \[of wicked people\]; constant engagement in knowledge of the self; and always keeping in mind the goal of liberation—these twenty items are declared by the ignorant to be transformations of the field. They are actually the** ***svarūpa*** **of direct spiritual knowledge. If one takes shelter of this, one attains knowledge of pure** ***tattva*****. They are not a transformation of the field. They are rather like a medicine that destroys the transformations of the field. Among the twenty items pure, steady** ***bhakti*** **to me alone must be taken shelter of. While the other nineteen items purify the field as an accompanying result and finally, after destroying the impurities of the field of the** ***jīva,*** **manifest the eternally perfect field. Know these nineteen items, the throne for** ***bhakti-devī*** **to be** ***jñāna*** **or** ***jñāna*** **accompanied by** ***vijñāna*****. Everything else is ignorance.**

### Text 13

*jñeyaṁ yat tat pravakṣyāmi yaj jñātvāmṛtam aśnute \|*  
*anādi mat paraṁ brahma na sat tan nāsad ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. O Arjuna! I have spoken about the** ***tattva*** **of the field and the knower of the field. The field is the body. I spoke of its** ***svarūpa,*** **its transformations and the procedure for removing the transformation. I also spoke of the knowers of the field, the** ***jīva*** **and Paramātmā. \[I spoke of the nature of the field and knowledge of the nature of the knower of the field, called** ***vijñāna*****.\] I will speak about what is to be known (*****jñeya*****) by that** ***vijñāna*****. Please listen.**

**The object to be known is without beginning, takes shelter of me, and is called Brahman \[which refers to the** ***jīva*****\], beyond cause and effect. Understanding this \[*****tattva*** **or** ***svarūpa*****\], one experiences eternal life in the form of** ***bhakti*** **to me.**

### Text 14

*sarvataḥ pāṇi-pādaṁ tat sarvato ’kṣi-śiro-mukham \|*  
*sarvataḥ śrutimal loke sarvam āvṛtya tiṣṭhati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. As the rays take shelter of the sun and become radiant, in the same way {Brahman, the** ***svarūpa*** **of my power, attains the limits of greatness}/\[Paramātmā, the** ***svarūpa*** **of my power, spreads everywhere.\] This** ***tattva*** **of {Brahman}/\[Paramātmā\] is the dwelling place (shelter) of unlimited** ***jīvas*** **from Brahma to the ant. It spreads everywhere with unlimited hands and feet, unlimited eyes, heads, mouths, and ears.**

### Text 15

*sarvendriya-guṇābhāsaṁ sarvendriya-vivarjitam \|*  
*asaktaṁ sarva-bhṛc caiva nirguṇaṁ guṇa-bhoktṛ ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. That great** ***tattva*** **illuminates all the senses but is devoid of all material senses. He is without attachment but maintains everything in the form of Viṣṇu. He is without** ***guṇas*** **of matter (*****nirguṇaṁ*****) and also tastes the six qualities indicated by the word** ***bhaga*****, which are beyond the three** ***guṇas*****.**

### Text 16

*bahir antaś ca bhūtānām acaraṁ caram eva ca \|*  
*sūkṣmatvāt tad avijñeyaṁ dūra-sthaṁ cāntike ca tat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. That** ***tattva*** **is outside and inside all beings. From him alone arise all moving and non-moving beings. He is difficult to understand because of his subtle nature. He is far away and also simultaneously very near.**

### Text 17

*avibhaktaṁ ca bhūteṣu vibhaktam iva ca sthitam \|*  
*bhūta-bhartṛ ca taj jñeyaṁ grasiṣṇu prabhaviṣṇu ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. He is understood as divided among all beings, but he is indivisible. Though situated with each** ***jīva*** **as the individual** ***puruṣa,*** **he is the Supreme Lord of all beings as the totality of the universe. He is the maintainer of all beings, their destroyer and the tattva that cause their birth.**

### Text 18

*jyotiṣām api taj jyotis tamasaḥ param ucyate \|*  
*jñānaṁ jñeyaṁ jñāna-gamyaṁ hṛdi sarvasya viṣṭhitam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. He is the supreme light among all lights. That is, he is the revealer. He has an unmanifested** ***svarūpa*** **beyond all darkness. He is knowledge, attainable by knowledge and object of knowledge. He is situated in everyone’s heart.**

### Text 19

*iti kṣetraṁ tathā jñānaṁ jñeyaṁ coktaṁ samāsataḥ \|*  
*mad-bhakta etad vijñāya mad-bhāvāyopapadyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. O Arjuna! I have spoken to you in brief about the field, knowledge and the object of knowledge. This is known as “*****jñāna*** **along with** ***vijñāna*****”. Attaining this knowledge, the devotees attain unconditioned** ***prema-bhakti*** **to me.**

**Those who are not devotees, taking shelter of** ***abheda-vāda*** **of useless groups, are cheated of real knowledge. Knowledge is nothing but purification of the consciousness of the** ***jīva*****, who is the shelter of** ***bhakti,*** **the throne for Bhakti-devī.**

**This will be further clarified when discussing** ***puruṣottama-tattva.***

### Text 20

*prakṛtiṁ puruṣaṁ caiva viddhy anādī ubhāv api \|*  
*vikārāṁś ca guṇāṁś caiva viddhi prakṛti-saṁbhavān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20*****.*** **What is the result of knowledge of the field and the knower of the field? I will explain this. Three** ***tattvas*** **will be seen in the state of the** ***jīva*** **bound by matter:** ***prakṛti, puruṣa*** **and Paramātmā. The whole field is** ***prakṛti*** **and the** ***jīva*** **is the** ***puruṣa*****. My Paramātmā form manifests within the two.** ***Prakṛti*** **and** ***puruṣa*** **are without beginning. They exist previous to material time. They are not born within material time. They manifest from my** ***śakti*** **in spiritual {unbroken} time, from me, the** ***svarūpa*** **of supreme existence. Material** ***prakṛti*** **was merged in me. At the time of activity, taking shelter of material time, it manifested.**

**The** ***jīva*** **is a** ***tattva*** **arising from my eternal** ***śakti*****. Because of being averse to me, it enters into material** ***prakṛti*****. The** ***jīva*** **is a real** ***tattva*** **of pure consciousness. By my superior** ***śakti,*** **a** ***taṭastha-dharma*** **is placed in it. It is also suitable for material** ***prakṛti*****. You cannot understand by intelligent logic or limited knowledge how the** ***cit-tattva*** **became bound by matter since my** ***acintya-śakti*** **is not dependent on your knowledge. All that is necessary to know is that all transformations of the bound** ***jīva*** **and all** ***guṇas*** **arise from material** ***prakṛti*****. They are not a natural** ***dharma*** **of the** ***jīva*****.**

### Text 21

*kārya-kāraṇa-kartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtir ucyate \|*  
*puruṣaḥ sukha-duḥkhānāṁ bhoktṛtve hetur ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. The material body (*****kārya*****), senses (*****kāraṇa*****) and the doership are the qualities of** ***prakṛti*****. Thus** ***prakṛti*** **is their cause. Because of its material identity caused by the** ***taṭastha*** **nature of the** ***jīva,*** **the** ***jīva*** **becomes the experiencer of happiness and suffering. The pure** ***jīva*** **is not the experiencer but accepts to be the experiencer from its** ***taṭastha*** **nature, caused by material identity in** ***prakṛti*** **in its state of bondage.**

### Text 22

*puruṣaḥ prakṛti-stho hi bhuṅkte prakṛti-jān guṇān \|*  
*kāraṇaṁ guṇa-saṅgo ’sya sad-asad-yoni-janmasu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Giving up purity of Vaikuṇṭha, the pure** ***jīva,*** **due to its** ***taṭāstha*** **nature, becoming situated in** ***prakṛti,*** **experiences all the** ***guṇas*** **arising from** ***prakṛti*****. Because of association with** ***prakṛti’s guṇas,*** **the** ***jīva*** **takes birth in high and low bodies.**

### Text 23

*upadraṣṭānumantā ca bhartā bhoktā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*paramātmeti cāpy ukto dehe’smin puruṣaḥ paraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. The** ***jīva*** **is my eternal friend. His** ***taṭastha*** **nature, when situated in a pure condition, attains favorability to me. That nature of** ***taṭastha*** **is his free will. When by that he attains pure** ***prema*** **for me, his nature as a** ***jīva*** **(*****jaiva-dharma*****) becomes successful. When the** ***jīva*** **enters the field of** ***prakṛti*** **by misuse of his nature, I accompany him in the form of Paramātmā.**

**Therefore, I am known as the supreme person with the name Paramātmā, in the form of the great lord, the instructor of all actions of the** ***jīva,*** **his permitter, his maintainer, and protector in the material body. I give the results of all actions undertaken by the bound** ***jīva*****.**

### Text 24

*ya evaṁ vetti puruṣaṁ prakṛtiṁ ca guṇaiḥ saha \|*  
*sarvathā vartamāno ’pi na sa bhūyo ’bhijāyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. He who knows the** ***puruṣa-tattva*** **devoid of** ***guṇas*** **and** ***prakṛti-tattva*** **endowed with** ***guṇas,*** **in this system, does not take birth again, though present in the material world. Having taken shelter of real** ***dharma,*** **\[having turned towards me,\] he attains my supreme abode by my mercy.**

### Text 25

*dhyānenātmani paśyanti kecid ātmānam ātmanā \|*  
*anye sāṅkhyena yogena karma-yogena cāpare \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. O Arjuna! There are two types of bound** ***jīvas*** **related to the Lord: the averse (*****bahirmukha*****) and the favorable (*****antarmukha*****). Atheists, materialists, sceptics and pure moralists are all averse to the Lord. The** ***sāṅkhya-yogīs*** **who are absorbed in nondifference are counted among the averse.**

**The inquisitive endowed with faith after some time, the** ***karma-yogīs*** **and the devotees are the favorable. The devotees are the best of all, since they meditate on Paramātmā by taking spiritual shelter of the** ***tattva*** **of** ***ātmā*** **beyond** ***prakṛti*****. The followers of** ***sāṅkhya*** **who are inquisitive about the Lord are in second place. Reviewing** ***prakṛti*** **with its twenty-four elements, knowing that the** ***jīva,*** **the twenty-fifth element, has a pure spiritual** ***svarūpa,*** **they then perform** ***bhakti*** **to Bhagavān, the twenty-sixth element. All the** ***karma-yogīs*** **are in a lesser position than them. By** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **they attain opportunity to reflect on the Lord.**

### Text 26

*anye tv evam ajānantaḥ śrutvānyebhya upāsate \|*  
*te ’pi cātitaranty eva mṛtyuṁ śruti-parāyaṇāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. Those who are less than them (i.e. the** ***karma-yogīs*** **of the previous verse) become endowed with faith later and thus who are inquisitive accept the** ***tattva*** **here and there, \[hearing from those who glorify the Lord, and begin to worship the Lord\]. They \[also surpass death, i.e.\] attain** ***bhakti*** **finally, through association with devotees and studying proper scriptures.**

### Text 27

*yāvat saṅjāyate kiñcit sattvaṁ sthāvara-jaṅgamam \|*  
*kṣetra-kṣetrajña-saṁyogāt tad viddhi bharatarṣabha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Know that all entities, whether moving or non-moving appear from the combination of field and knower of the field, O best of Bharata’s lineage.**

### Text 28

*samaṁ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tiṣṭhantaṁ parameśvaram \|*  
*vinaśyatsv avinaśyantaṁ yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. Though situated in all beings equally, the Supreme Lord as Paramātmā does not accept the transitory qualities of temporary objects. He who knows Paramātmā in this way can know him in truth.**

### Text 29

*samaṁ paśyan hi sarvatra samavasthitam īśvaram \|*  
*na hinasty ātmanātmānaṁ tato yāti parāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. Accepting the qualities of** ***prakṛti,*** **the different situations of the bound** ***jīvas*** **occur. Among them one who by discrimination understands that I am equal to all in ruling over them, does not degrade his existence as a** ***jīva*** **by a mind following a wrong path.**

### Text 30

*prakṛtyaiva ca karmāṇi kriyamāṇāni sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*yaḥ paśyati tathātmānam akartāraṁ sa paśyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. He who sees that** ***prakṛti,*** **transforming into the body and senses, \[inspired by the Lord gives results of karma,\] performs all actions and sees that I, the pure** ***ātmā,*** **do nothing at all, sees himself as the non-doer in all actions.**

### Text 31

*yadā bhūta-pṛthag-bhāvam eka-stham anupaśyati \|*  
*tata eva ca vistāraṁ brahma saṁpadyate tadā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. When the discriminating person sees that all the separate moving and nonmoving living beings are situated in one** ***prakṛti*** **\[at the time of dissolution\] and that they arise from that one** ***prakṛti*** **at the time of creation, becomes devoid of seeing differences in** ***prakṛti*****.
