He, then becoming fixed in the pure spiritual** ***tattva,*** **attains oneness with Brahman in terms of attaining a spiritual form.**

**Attaining this realization of non-difference, how the** ***jīva*** **sees Paramātmā, the seer, I will explain later.**

### Text 32

*anāditvān nirguṇatvāt paramātmāyam avyayaḥ \|*  
*śarīra-stho ’pi kaunteya na karoti na lipyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. Endowed with Brahman, the** ***jīva*** **can see that {*****Paramātmā*****}/\[*****ātmā*****\] is indestructible, without beginning and devoid of** ***guṇas*****. Though he is situated in the body {along with the** ***jīvātmā*****}, he is not contaminated by the qualities of the field {unlike the conditioned** ***jīva*****. The** ***jīva*** **endowed with Brahman also is not contaminated, having taken shelter of that knowledge.} Though not contaminated, hear how the** ***jīva*** **acts in the field.**

### Text 33

*yathā sarva-gataṁ saukṣmyād ākāśaṁ nopalipyate \|*  
*sarvatrāvasthito dehe tathātmā nopalipyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. Just as the subtle ether, which spreads everywhere, is not contaminated by other objects, the discriminating** ***jīva*** **endowed with Brahman is not contaminated though situated in all bodies, \[since he follows the qualities of Paramātmā.\]**

### Text 34

*yathā prakāśayaty ekaḥ kṛtsnaṁ lokam imaṁ raviḥ \|*  
*kṣetraṁ kṣetrī tathā kṛtsnaṁ prakāśayati bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34. Just as one sun lights up the whole world, this** ***ātmā,*** **the knower of the field, illuminates the whole field \[by his quality of consciousness,\] O descendent of Bharata.**

### Text 35

*kṣetra-kṣetrajñayor evam antaraṁ jñāna-cakṣuṣā \|*  
*bhūta-prakṛti-mokṣaṁ ca ye vidur yānti te param \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. All the actions of material prakṛti are the field. Paramātmā and the two types of jīvas are the knowers of the field. He who knows the difference between the field and knower of the field by the eye of knowledge according to system written in this chapter and knows about liberation of all entities from the tendency to be attached to prakṛti, {easily knows Bhagavān, the supreme tattva among the field and knowers of the field.}/\[easily attains the supreme tattva, the spiritual world, beyond prakṛti.\]**

**Комментарий:**

After discussing the nine types of bhakti, such as hearing and chanting after taking shelter of the feet of guru, the faithful person attains *niṣṭhā* after destroying all his *anarthas*.

Lack of distinction between what is spiritual and material is the main *anartha* among all of them. With the aim of giving instructions on knowledge which will destroy that *anartha*, the Lord describes in this chapter while discussing knowledge of the *kṣetra* and the knower of the *kṣetra*, the *kṣetra* composed of twenty-four items: the gross elements, *ahaṅkāra, buddhi, avyakta,* all the senses and the five sense objects as well as desire, hatred, happiness, suffering, the combination, the function of the mind, seat of consciousness, and determination, which are transformations of the *kṣetra*. Separate from this are two persons, the *jīva*, whose *svarūpa* is a conscious particle, suitable for a wealth of Brahman in the form of being my servant, and Paramātmā, my all-pervading aṁśa. These are the knowers of the *kṣetra*. The combination of the *kṣetra* and the *jīva*, knower of the *kṣetra*, is *saṁsāra*. While seeing Paramātmā one destroys the *anarthas* which obstruct one from realizing one’s *svarūpa* through *tattva-jñāna* or knowledge of the relationship of the *kṣetra* with the knower of the *kṣetra*. This is the *tattva* following the *aṅga* of *smaraṇa*.

## Chapter Fourteen - Guṇa-traya-vibhāga-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*paraṁ bhūyaḥ pravakṣyāmi jñānānāṁ jñānam uttamam \|*  
*yaj jñātvā munayaḥ sarve parāṁ siddhim ito gatāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: From the seventh to twelfth chapters I spoke all about the supreme tattva. I will again speak how by knowledge one can attain the highest knowledge of Bhagavān. Understanding this knowledge, sages like Sanaka and others who were fixed in jñāna, attained the supreme type of bhakti.**

### Text 2

*idaṁ jñānam upāśritya mama sādharmyam āgatāḥ \|*  
*sarge ’pi nopajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. Knowledge is generally endowed with** ***guṇas*****. Knowledge beyond the** ***guṇas*** **(*****nirguṇa*****) is called the highest knowledge. When the** ***jīva*** **takes shelter of this** ***nirguṇa*** **knowledge, he attains similarity (*****sādharmya*****) to me \[i.e., he becomes endowed with my eight eternal qualities\]. People with material intelligence think that if one rejects material qualities, material form and material situations, the** ***jīva*** **becomes devoid of qualities, form and situation. They do not know that just as in the material world there is difference among objects by qualities called** ***viśeṣa,*** **so, surpassing the material world, in my Vaikuṇṭha, there is pure** ***viśeṣa*** **there. By that** ***viśeṣa,*** **spiritual qualities, spiritual form and spiritual conditions are established eternally. That is called my** ***nirguṇa-sādharmya*****. Surpassing the** ***saguṇa*** **world by** ***nirguṇa*** **knowledge, one attains** ***nirguṇa*** **Brahman. After that attainment arise spiritual qualities. When that happens, at the time of creation, that** ***jīva*** **does not take birth in the material world and at the time of destruction does not experience the pain of having his self destroyed.**

### Text 3

*mama yonir mahad brahma tasmin garbhaṁ dadhāmy aham \|*  
*saṁbhavaḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ tato bhavati bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. The main principle of material** ***prakṛti*** **is the womb of the universe. I impregnate that Brahman, the womb of the universe called {*****prakṛti*****}/\[*****pradhāna*****\]. From that, all the beings arise. I place \[semen in the form of\] the** ***jīvas*** **endowed with** ***taṭastha-svarūpa*** **into** ***prakṛti,*** **called Brahman, the material extension of my spiritual** ***prakṛti*****. From this, all beings such as Brahmā are born.**

### Text 4

*sarva-yoniṣu kaunteya mūrtayaḥ saṁbhavanti yāḥ \|*  
*tāsāṁ brahma mahad yonir ahaṁ bīja-pradaḥ pitā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. That womb in the form of Brahman is the mother of all the forms manifested in wombs of** ***devatās,*** **animals and others and I, the causal form of consciousness, am the seed-giving father of all of them.**

### Text 5

*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti guṇāḥ prakṛti-saṁbhavāḥ \|*  
*nibadhnanti mahā-bāho dehe dehinam avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. The** ***guṇas*** **of** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*** **arise from** ***prakṛti*** **which produces the universe. From** ***taṭastha-prakṛti*** **all the** ***jīvas*** **are born into the womb of material** ***prakṛti*****. The three** ***guṇas*** **bind the** ***jīvas,*** **who are conscious in** ***svarūpa*** **and indestructible and who are the possessors of bodies.**

### Text 6

*tatra sattvaṁ nirmalatvāt prakāśakam anāmayam \|*  
*sukha-saṅgena badhnāti jñāna-saṅgena cānagha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. S*****attva-guṇa*** **of** ***prakṛti*** **is relatively pure, produces knowledge and is devoid of sin. S*****attva-guṇa*** **binds the conscious** ***jīva*** **by attachment to knowledge and happiness.**

### Text 7

*rajo rāgātmakaṁ viddhi tṛṣṇā-saṅga-samudbhavam \|*  
*tan nibadhnāti kaunteya karma-saṅgena dehinam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. Know that** ***rajas*** **has the quality of desire, born from attachment and longing. O Kaunteya!** ***Rajoguṇa*** **binds the** ***jīva*** **by attachment to action.**

### Text 8

*tamas tv ajñāna-jaṁ viddhi mohanaṁ sarva-dehinām \|*  
*pramādālasya-nidrābhis tan nibadhnāti bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Know that** ***tamas*** **is the** ***guṇa*** **born of ignorance and bewilders all** ***jīvas*** **who possess a body.** ***Tamoguṇa*** **binds the** ***jīva*** **by inattention, laziness, and sleep.**

### Text 9

*sattvaṁ sukhe sañjayati rajaḥ karmaṇi bhārata \|*  
*jñānam āvṛtya tu tamaḥ pramāde sañjayaty uta \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9.** ***Sattva*** **binds the** ***jīva*** **to happiness,** ***rajoguṇa*** **binds the** ***jīva*** **to action.** ***Tamoguṇa*** **bind the** ***jīva*** **to inattention.**

### Text 10

*rajas tamaś cābhibhūya sattvaṁ bhavati bhārata \|*  
*rajaḥ sattvaṁ tamaś caiva tamaḥ sattvaṁ rajas tathā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. When** ***sattva-guṇa*** **becomes strong,** ***rajas*** **and** ***tamas*** **are defeated. When** ***rajoguṇa*** **is strong,** ***sattva*** **and** ***tamas*** **are defeated. When** ***tamoguṇa*** **is strong,** ***sattva*** **and** ***rajas*** **are defeated. In this way one should know the situation of each** ***guṇa*** **and their mutual relationships.**

### Text 11

*sarva-dvāreṣu dehe ’smin prakāśa upajāyate \|*  
*jñānaṁ yadā tadā vidyād vivṛddhaṁ sattvam ity uta \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. By increase of** ***sattva*** **there is increase of knowledge or** ***prakāśa*** **in the senses or doors of the material body. This is knowledge arising from the senses.**

### Text 12

*lobhaḥ pravṛttir ārambhaḥ karmaṇām aśamaḥ spṛhā \|*  
*rajasy etāni jāyante vivṛddhe bharatarṣabha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Those whose** ***rajas*** **increases, their greed, endeavor, eagerness to act, and desire increase.**

### Text 13

*aprakāśo ’pravṛttiś ca pramādo moha eva ca \|*  
*tamasy etāni jāyante vivṛddhe kuru-nandana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. Absence of discernment, absence of effort, inattention and absorption in the false appear with the increase of** ***tamas*****, O son of the Kurus.**

### Text 14

*yadā sattve pravṛddhe tu pralayaṁ yāti deha-bhṛt \|*  
*tadottama-vidāṁ lokān amalān pratipadyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. When a person with predominance of** ***sattva*** **dies, he attains the planets of the worshippers of Hiraṇyagarbha Brahmā, which gives happiness.**

### Text 15

*rajasi pralayaṁ gatvā karma-saṅgiṣu jāyate*  
*tathā pralīnas tamasi mūḍha-yoniṣu jāyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. He who dies in the mode of** ***rajas*** **is born in a family {of** ***brāhmaṇas*****} attached to** ***karmas*****. He who dies in the mode of** ***tamas*** **is born in the wombs of the foolish such as animals.**

### Text 16

*karmaṇaḥ sukṛtasyāhuḥ sattvikiṁ nirmalaṁ phalam*  
*rajasas tu phalaṁ duḥkham ajñānaṁ tamasaḥ phalam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. The result of actions in** ***sattva*** **is said to be {*****sāttvika*****} purity. The result of actions in** ***rajas*** **is sorrow. The result of actions in** ***tamas*** **is unconsciousness and ignorance*****.***

### Text 17

*sattvāt sañjāyate jñānaṁ rajaso lobha eva ca*  
*pramāda-mohau tamaso bhavato ’jñānam eva ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. Knowledge arises from** ***sattva*****. Greed arises from** ***rajas*****. Inattention, confusion and ignorance arise from** ***tamas.***

### Text 18

*ūrdhvaṁ gacchanti sattva-sthā madhye tiṣṭhanti rājasāḥ*  
*jaghanya-guṇa-vṛtti-sthā adho gacchanti tāmasāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. Those situated in** ***sattva*** **go to higher planets such as Satyaloka. Those in** ***rajas*** **remain in the world of humans. Those of the lowest** ***guṇa*****,** ***tamas*****, go downwards to hell.**

### Text 19

*nānyaṁ guṇebhyaḥ kartāraṁ yadā draṣṭānupaśyati*  
*guṇebhyaś ca paraṁ vetti mad-bhāvaṁ so ’dhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. When the** ***jīva,*** **realizing by subtle vision that the** ***guṇas*** **do everything and that there is no other doer except the** ***guṇas,*** **knows the nature of the Lord beyond the** ***guṇas,*** **he attains pure** ***bhakti*** **in the form of** ***bhāva*** **for me.**

### Text 20

*guṇān etān atītya trīn dehī deha-samudbhavān \|*  
*janma-mṛtyu-jarā-duḥkhair vimukto ’mṛtam aśnute \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Surpassing the three** ***guṇas*** **of** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*** **by being fixed in** ***nirguṇa,*** **the** ***jīva*** **endowed with a body, becoming free of the suffering arising from the body in the form of birth, death, old age and disease, experiences sweetness in the form of** ***nirguṇa prema*** **(*****amṛtam*****).**

### Text 21

*arjuna uvaca*  
*kair liṅgais trīn guṇān etān atīto bhavati prabho \|*  
*kim-ācāraḥ kathaṁ caitāṁs trīn guṇān ativartate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. Hearing this, Arjuna said: O Lord! What are the symptoms of a person beyond the** ***guṇas?*** **What is his conduct? How does he exist, having surpassed the** ***guṇas*** **by his** ***sādhana?***

### Text 22

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*prakāśaṁ ca pravṛttiṁ ca moham eva ca pāṇḍava \|*  
*na dveṣṭi saṁpravṛttāni na nivṛttāni kāṅkṣati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Hearing Arjuna’s three questions, Lord Kṛṣṇa-candra said:**

**In answer to your first question concerning the symptoms of a person beyond the** ***guṇas,*** **the answer is that he is devoid of hatred and longing. The bound** ***jīva,*** **situated in the material world, exists amidst the three** ***guṇas*** **of** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*****. Only when he attains complete liberation does the** ***jīva*** **destroy the three** ***guṇas*****. As long as he does not attain liberation in the form of destruction of the subtle body by the will of the Lord, giving up hatred and longing should be known as the only means of attaining a state of** ***nirguṇa*****. While the body exists, knowledge, activity and illusion (which arise from** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*** **respectively), will be connected necessarily with the body. But you should not like or dislike them when they appear or disappear. A person with these two qualities is** ***nirguṇa*****. Those who by action and by selfish insistence engage in material life or those who practice detachment while performing actions, knowing the material world to be false, are never beyond the** ***guṇas*****.**

### Text 23

*udāsīna-vad āsīno guṇair yo na vicālyate \|*  
*guṇā vartanta ity evaṁ yo ’vatiṣṭhati neṅgate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. In answer to the second question about conduct, the conduct is as follows. The** ***guṇas*** **act on the body, mind and actions. Engaging the** ***guṇas*** **in action, the person, separates himself from them since his** ***svarūpa*** **is consciousness, and is not contaminated by them, like an indifferent person.**

### Texts 24-25

*sama-duḥkha-sukhaḥ sva-sthaḥ sama-loṣṭāśma-kāñcanaḥ \|*  
*tulya-priyāpriyo dhīras tulya-nindātma-saṁstutiḥ \|\|*  
*mānāpamānayos tulyas tulyo mitrāri-pakṣayoḥ \|*  
*sarvārambha-parityāgī guṇātītaḥ sa ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24-25. Though by his bodily actions he attains suffering, happiness, earth, rock, gold, pleasing and repulsive objects, criticism and praise, he remains neutral to them. He treats them all equally, being situated in his consciousness (*****sva-sthaḥ*****). Giving up respect, disrespect, enemy and friend in worldly dealings, he considers them to be unrelated to his consciousness. Giving up all attachment and renunciation, he is known as being beyond the** ***guṇas*****.**

### Text 26

*māṁ ca yo ’vyabhicāreṇa bhakti-yogena sevate \|*  
*sa guṇān samatītyaitān brahma-bhūyāya kalpate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. Your third question was “How does the person devoid of the three** ***guṇas*** **exist?” The answer is this. Serving me by knowledge and action whose goal is pure** ***bhakti,*** **by steady** ***bhakti-yoga,*** **he attains the state of Brahman--which means similarity to me (*****brahma-bhūyāya*****).**

### Text 27

*brahmaṇo hi pratiṣṭhāham amṛtasyāvyayasya ca \|*  
*śāśvatasya ca dharmasya sukhasyaikāntikasya ca \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. One may say, “The result of all types of** ***sādhana*** **of the** ***jīva*** **is the wealth of Brahman. How then a person situated in Brahman experiences** ***nirguṇa prema*** **for you?” Then please listen.**

**I am by nature Bhagavān in an eternal state beyond the** ***guṇas*****. When I place the seeds of consciousness of** ***taṭastha-śakti*** **in my material** ***śakti,*** **the first manifestation of the first mentioned** ***śakti*** **is called my nature as Brahman nature. When the** ***jīva*** **bound by matter becomes elevated by absorbing himself in** ***jñāna,*** **he attains my abode of Brahman, the first limit in the state of being** ***nirguṇa*****.Previous to attaining its limit, he attains the state devoid of qualities, in the form of rejection of material qualities. Situated in that state, {when he takes shelter of pure** ***bhakti-yoga,*****} that state without qualities is removed and it becomes full of spiritual qualities. In this way Sanaka and Vāmadeva on the path of** ***jñāna,*** **absorbed in Brahman without qualities, attained the sweetness of** ***nirguṇa bhakti*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who, because of having low intentions of desiring liberation, are not properly situated in Brahman, they do not attain *nirguṇa bhakti* in the end. Actually, I, the *nirguṇa svarūpa* of qualities, am the shelter or *pratiṣṭhā* of Brahman, the final goal of the *jñānīs*. Deathlessness (*amṛtasya*), indestructibility (*avyayasya*), eternity (*śāśvatasya*), the eternal dharma called *prema* (*dharmasya*), and the rasa of Vraja in the form of complete happiness (*ekāntikasya sukhasya*) take shelter of the *svarūpa* of Kṛṣṇa, the *nirguṇa tattva* endowed with qualities.

Thirst for material objects is the second *anartha*. The *jīva* by his nature is without material *guṇas* but by contact with matter becomes almost endowed with *guṇas*. He becomes bound by the three *guṇas* of *sattva, rajas* and *tamas*. Thirst for material objects arises from these three *guṇas*. After taking support of a mentality of going beyond the three *guṇas* one should destroy this thirst for material objects. When one attains devotee-association at the time of reflecting on the nine types of *bhakti* such as hearing and chanting, the thirst for material objects is destroyed and by engaging in serving devotees, the heart becomes steady on the path of *bhakti*. From verse 22 of this chapter till the end, the method of attaining freedom from the three *guṇas* is explained. The only method of attaining freedom from the three *guṇas* for the pure devotees is the practice of withdrawing the heart from material objects by making a vow to see the object in relation to the Lord, serving *mahāprasāda*, smelling tulasī *mahāprasāda*, seeing the deity and pastime places, and hearing the stories of devotees and pastimes, forms, and names of the Lord as activities of serving the Lord’s feet. This is the purport of this chapter.

## Chapter Fifteen - Puruṣottama-yoga

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ūrdhva-mūlam adhaḥ-śākham aśvatthaṁ prāhur avyayam \|*  
*chandāṁsi yasya parṇāni yas taṁ veda sa veda-vit \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. The Lord said: O Arjuna! If you think that it is proper to take shelter of** ***saṁsāra*** **while taking support of Vedic statements, I say, listen.**

**This world made of** ***karmas*** **is a special** ***aśvattha*** **tree. It has no end or destruction for persons who take shelter of** ***karmas*****. The statements of the** ***Vedas*** **which propound** ***karmas*** **are its leaves. Its roots are upwards. Its branches are spread out below.**

**{This tree is thus based upon me, the highest** ***tattva*****}/\[Above the tree is** ***mahat-tattva*****. From Satyaloka it is established\], giving results of** ***karma*** **to the** ***jīva*****. He who knows the temporary nature of the tree knows the truth.**

### Text 2

*adhaś cordhvaṁ prasṛtās tasya śākhā*  
*guṇa-pravṛddhā viṣaya-pravālāḥ \|*  
*adhaś ca mūlāny anusantatāni*  
*karmānubandhīni manuṣya-loke \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. The many branches of the tree, taking shelter of** ***tamoguṇa*****, grow downwards. Some of the branches, taking shelter of** ***rajoguṇa,*** **spread out horizontally. Some branches, taking support of** ***sattva-guṇa,*** **spread upwards. All of them are nourished by the three** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****. The material sense objects are the shoots on the branches. The mass of sub-trunks, like those of banyan tree, spread downwards while searching for results of karma.**

### Texts 3-4

*na rūpam asyeha tathopalabhyate*  
*nānto na cādir na ca saṁpratiṣṭhā \|*  
*aśvattham enaṁ su-virūḍha-mūlam*  
*asaṅga-śastreṇa dṛḍhena chittvā \|\|*  
*tataḥ padaṁ tat-parimārgitavyaṁ*  
*yasmin gatā na nivartanti bhūyaḥ \|*  
*tam eva cādyaṁ puruṣaṁ prapadye*  
*yataḥ pravṛttiḥ prasṛtā purāṇī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3-4. The real form of this tree is difficult for humans to understand since its beginning, its end, and its shelter cannot be perceived. Cutting down this temporary** ***aśvattha*** **tree having strong roots, using the axe of detachment, a person should search out the real entity. Situated in this true** ***tattva,*** **the** ***jīva*** **will no longer take birth. From this original person, long-standing actions of** ***saṁsāra*** **spread out. If you want to destroy these actions, you should surrender to the original person.**

### Text 5

*nirmāna-mohā jita-saṅga-doṣā*  
*adhyātma-nityā vinivṛtta-kāmāḥ \|*  
*dvandvair vimuktāḥ sukha-duḥkha-saṁjñair*  
*gacchanty amūḍhāḥ padam avyayaṁ tat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. \[Those who know how to surrender,\] who are free from delusion and pride, who are devoid of the fault of attachment, who are absorbed in reflecting on the eternal and non-eternal, who have renounced material desire, who are free of dualities of happiness and distress, attain that indestructible goal.**

### Text 6

*na tad bhāsayate sūryo na śaśāṅko na pāvakaḥ \|*  
*yad gatvā na nivartante tad dhāma paramaṁ mama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. The sun, moon and fire cannot illuminate my indestructible abode. Having attained that abode, there is no more cessation of the** ***jīva’s*** **bliss.**

**The main principle is this. There are two states of the** ***jīva: saṁsāra*** **and liberation. In the state of** ***saṁsāra*** **the** ***jīva*** **is contaminated by material attachment because of identifying himself with the body. In the liberated state the pure** ***jīva*** **constantly tastes my pure love \[in spiritual pastimes\]. To attain this state, the person situated in** ***saṁsāra*** **must cut down the** ***aśvattha*** **tree of** ***saṁsāra*** **by the weapon of detachment. Attachment to objects of matter is called** ***saṅga*****. Even though situated in matter, the person who is able to give up attachment (*****saṅga*****) to matter has a** ***nirguṇa*** **nature and he attains pure,** ***nirguṇa bhakti*****.**

**Association with the spiritual is called** ***asaṅga*****. Thus the** ***jīva*** **in** ***saṁsāra,*** **by giving up material attachment and taking shelter of devotee association, can cut down** ***saṁsāra*** **at its root. Those who practice** ***vairāgya*** **just by wearing the marks of** ***sannyāsa*** **do not destroy** ***saṁsāra.*** **If, giving up all other desires, a person takes support of my** ***bhakti*** **endowed with the highest** ***rasa,*** **liberation in the form of destruction of** ***saṁsāra*** **becomes a secondary result for the** ***jīva*****. Thus the** ***bhakti*** **taught in the twelfth chapter is the only goal of the** ***jīva*** **desiring auspiciousness.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous chapter all types of knowledge were described as having guṇas and pure knowledge which is a servant of bhakti was described as being without guṇas. In this chapter, all types of vairāgya are shown to have guṇas and vairāgya of everything except bhakti, which is an accompanying result of bhakti, is shown to be devoid of guṇas.

### Text 7

*mamaivāṁśo jīva-loke jīva-bhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*manaḥ-ṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi prakṛti-sthāni karṣati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. If you ask why the** ***jīva*** **has these two states, then listen. I am complete** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **Bhagavān. I have two types of** ***aṁśas: svāṁśa*** **and** ***vibhinnāṁśa*****. As my** ***svāṁśas,*** **I perform pastimes as Rāma, Nṛsimha and others. As my** ***vibhinnāṁśas,*** **I manifest** ***jīvas*** **as my eternal servants. In my** ***svāṁśa*** **manifestation I maintain completely my sense of ‘I’. In manifestation of** ***vibhinnāṁśas,*** **my identity as the Supreme Lord does not remain. As** ***vibhinnāṁśas,*** **the** ***jīvas*** **manifest their own sense of ‘I’ as their nature.**

**The** ***tattva*** **of** ***jīvas*** **as** ***vibhinnāṁśas*** **have two states: liberated and in bondage. In both states the** ***jīva*** **is eternal (*****sanātana*****). The liberated** ***jīva*** **is under my shelter completely and devoid of relationship with** ***prakṛti*****.**

**The bound** ***jīva*** **carries the six senses—the mind and five external senses situated in** ***prakṛti*** **as his** ***upādhi*****—thinking them to be himself.**

### Text 8

*śarīraṁ yad avāpnoti yac cāpy utkrāmatīśvaraḥ \|*  
*gṛhītvaitāni saṁyāti vāyur gandhān ivāśayāt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. It is not that, when one dies, one’s state of bondage ends. The** ***jīva*** **attains a gross body according to karma and, when the time comes, gives it up according to karma. When going from one body to another, the** ***jīva*** **goes along with impressions of karmas related to the body. As wind takes fragrance from flowers or sandalwood, which are repositories of scent, and goes elsewhere, the** ***jīva*** **leaves one gross body and goes to another gross body, taking the senses along with the subtle body.**

### Text 9

*śrotraṁ cakṣuḥ sparśanaṁ ca rasanaṁ ghrāṇam eva ca \|*  
*adhiṣṭhāya manaś cāyaṁ viṣayān upasevate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. Having obtained another gross body, taking shelter of mind and the external senses such as ear, eye, skin, tongue and nose, the bound** ***jīva*** **serves sense objects.**

### Text 10

*utkrāmantaṁ sthitaṁ vāpi bhuñjānaṁ vā guṇānvitam \|*  
*vimūḍhā nānupaśyanti paśyanti jñāna-cakṣuṣaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. The foolish do not perceive when the** ***jīva*** **is leaving the body, when he is residing in it or when he is enjoying the sense objects. Those who are situated in pure knowledge, deliberating on all this, conclude that the** ***jīva’s*** **state of bondage is full of suffering.**

### Text 11

*yatanto yoginaś cainaṁ paśyanty ātmany avasthitam \|*  
*yatanto ’py akṛtātmāno nainaṁ paśyanty acetasaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. Endeavoring** ***yogīs*** **see this movement of the bound** ***jīva*** **in terms of the** ***tattva*** **of** ***ātmā*****. Those** ***sannyāsīs*** **with impure hearts do not understand the** ***tattva*** **of the** ***jīva*** **in the absence of deliberation on spiritual** ***tattva*****.**

### Text 12

*yad āditya-gataṁ tejo jagad bhāsayate ’khilam \|*  
*yac candramasi yac cāgnau tat tejo viddhi māmakam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. If it is said that the** ***jīva*** **situated in** ***saṁsāra*** **is incapable of discerning anything except matter how will he be able to discern the spiritual? In answer to this, please listen.**

**My spiritual existence shines even in the material world. Taking support of that, it is possible to gradually purify the heart and destroy materialism. The light which reveals the whole world existing in the sun, moon and fire is my light and no one else’s.**

### Text 13

*gām āviśya ca bhūtāni dhārayāmy aham ojasā \|*  
*puṣṇāmi cauṣadhīḥ sarvāḥ somo bhūtvā rasātmakaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. Entering the earth, I support all beings by my** ***śakti*****. I nourish all plants such as rice, in the form of the moon, full of sweet juice.**

### Text 14

*ahaṁ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā prāṇināṁ deham āśritaḥ \|*  
*prāṇāpāna-samāyuktaḥ pacāmy annaṁ catur-vidham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. Entering into the body of living beings as the fire of digestion, I digest four types of food—chewed, savored, licked and sucked—in combination with** ***prāṇa*** **and** ***apāna*****.**

**{I am Brahman according to the statement** ***sarvaṁ khalv idaṁ brahma:*** **everything is Brahman.}**

### Text 15

*sarvasya cāhaṁ hṛdi sanniviṣṭo*  
*mattaḥ smṛtir jñānam apohanaṁ ca \|*  
*vedaiś ca sarvair aham eva vedyo*  
*vedānta-kṛd veda-vid eva cāham \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. I am situated as** ***īśvara,*** **in the hearts of all** ***jīvas*****. From me, according to** ***jīva’s karma,*** **come remembrance, knowledge and forgetfulness. Thus I am not only the Brahman pervading the universe but the Paramātmā who gives results of karma, being situated in the** ***jīva’s*** **heart. Not only am I to be worshipped by the** ***jīva*** **in the form of Brahman or Paramātmā but am the teacher of the** ***jīvas*** **and bestower of eternal auspiciousness to the** ***jīvas*****. I, Bhagavān, am to be known by all the Vedas. I am the maker of Vedānta and the knower of Vedānta. Thus I am the deliverer of the bound** ***jīva*** **by three manifestations to render auspiciousness to all** ***jīvas:*** **as Brahman within** ***prakṛti,*** **as Paramātmā within the heart of the** ***jīvas*** **and as Bhagavān, bestower of the highest goal.**

### Text 16

*dvāv imau puruṣau loke kṣaraś cākṣara eva ca \|*  
*kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭa-stho ’kṣara ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. If you say that you understand there is one** ***prakṛti*** **but cannot understand about the numerous conscious beings, then listen.**

**Actually, in this world there are only two** ***puruṣas*** **called** ***kṣara*** **and** ***akṣara*****.**

**{The conscious** ***jīva*****, a** ***vibhinnāṁśa,*** **is the** ***kṣara puruṣa*****. The** ***jīva*** **is called the** ***kṣara-puruṣa*** **because of his** ***taṭashta*** **nature which makes him prone to fall from his** ***svarūpa*****. Those who never perish from their** ***svarūpa*** **are called** ***akṣara puruṣa,*** **they are** ***svāṁśa-tattvas*****. The** ***akṣara puruṣa*** **is also called** ***kuṭastha puruṣa*****.**

**There are three types of** ***akṣara puruṣas*** **who are** ***kuṭastha*****. When the universe is created, the** ***akṣara puruṣa*** **seen in the** ***svarūpa*** **of all-pervading existence, in a condition opposite to all material qualities is Brahman. Brahman is a special** ***tattva*** **related to the world. It is not an independent** ***tattva*****. The manifestation which gives shelter to all the** ***jīvas*** **of conscious** ***svarūpa*** **in the world, which manifests to some degree the pure consciousness** ***tattva*** **is called Paramātmā. He is not independent, being a special** ***tattva*** **related to the material world.}/**

**\[The conscious** ***jīva,*** **a** ***vibhinnāṁśa,*** **is of two types:** ***kṣara*** **and** ***akṣara*****. The bound** ***jīva*** **with many temporary states is the** ***kṣara puruṣa*****. The** ***jīva*** **in one state without these changing states is** ***akṣara*** **or the liberated** ***puruṣa*****. Brahmā and other entities down to plants are** ***kṣara*** **and the** ***kuṭastha puruṣa*** **is always in one state and thus** ***akṣara*****.\]**

### Text 17

*uttamaḥ puruṣas tv anyaḥ paramātmety udāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*yo loka-trayam āviśya bibharty avyaya īśvaraḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. {The second** ***akṣara puruṣa*** **is the form of Paramātmā. In general, he is superior to Brahman who is in the form of** ***akṣara puruṣa*****.}/\[Beyond the** ***kṣara*** **and** ***akṣara puruṣas*****, described previously, is the** ***uttama puruṣa*****.\] He is** ***īśvara*** **and, entering into the three worlds, resides as its maintainer.**

### Text 18

*yasmāt kṣaram atīto ’ham akṣarād api cottamaḥ \|*  
*ato ’smi loke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. {The third, most excellent** ***akṣara puruṣa*** **is called Bhagavān. I am that** ***bhagavat-tattva*****. I am superior to** ***jīva,*** **the** ***kṣara-puruṣa*** **and superior to Brahman and Paramātmā, the** ***akṣara puruṣas*****. Thus, I am called the supreme** ***puruṣa*** **in the world and Vedas.**

**Thus know this as the conclusion: there are two** ***puruṣas: kṣara*** **and** ***akṣara,*** **and the** ***akṣara*** **puruṣa has three manifestations: the general manifestation Brahman, the excellent manifestation Paramātmā and the most excellent manifestation Bhagavān.}**

**\[I am beyond and more excellent than the** ***kṣara*** **and** ***akṣara puruṣas.*** **Thus I am glorified as ‘*****puruṣottama*****’ in the world and** ***Vedas*****.\]**

### Text 19

*yo mām evam asammūḍho jānāti puruṣottamam \|*  
*sa sarva-vid bhajati māṁ sarva-bhāvena bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. He who, not bewildered by various philosophies, knows my** ***sac-cid-ānanda svarūpa*** **as** ***puruṣottama-tattva,*** **is the knower of everything and is able to worship me with complete devotion \[in** ***dāsya, sakhya, vātsalya*** **and** ***mādhurya rasas*****\].**

### Text 20

*iti guhyatamaṁ śāstram idam uktaṁ mayānagha \|*  
*etad buddhvā buddhimān syāt kṛta-kṛtyaś ca bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. O sinless Arjuna! This** ***puruṣottama-yoga*** **is the most secret teaching. Understanding this, the intelligent** ***jīva*** **becomes successful. O Bhārata! On understanding this** ***yoga,*** **having taking shelter of** ***bhakti*****, you will destroy all faults arising from material objects.**

***Bhakti*** **is a \[spiritual, eternal,\] special function. In order to bring about its beautiful action, the purity of the** ***jīva*** **as the shelter (*****āśrāya*****) of bhakti and the complete manifestation of Bhagavān as the subject (*****viṣaya*****) of** ***bhakti*** **is very necessary. {As much as one thinks of Brahman and Paramātmā as Bhagavān,}/\[As much as one does not have pure intelligence in** ***bhagavat-tattva*****,\] one does not attain actions of pure** ***bhakti*****. \[On the other hand\] if one thinks of the Lord as Puruṣottama,** ***bhakti*** **is conducted in a pure manner.**

**Комментарий:**

At the time of sādhana-bhakti, it is necessary to destroy four huge anarthas on the strength of remembering, an aṅga of pure bhakti, and association of devotees. Among them, the third anartha is weakness of heart, in the form of attachment to saṁsāra. Desiring enjoyment of māyā because of the independence the Lord has given to the pure jīva is the first weakness of heart. Later, while wandering in saṁsāra, the attachment to material objects is the second weakness of heart. From these two types of weakness of heart arise all other anarthas. In the first five verses pure renunciation, the characteristic which destroys this weakness is described. From the sixth verse till the end of the chapter arrangements for seeing the tattva of Puruṣottama with the assistance of yukta-vairāgya arising from bhakti is described.

## Chapter Sixteen - Daivāsura-sampad-vibhāga-yoga

### Text 1-3

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*abhayaṁ sattva-saṁśuddhir jñāna-yoga-vyavasthitiḥ \|*  
*dānaṁ damaś ca yajñaś ca svādhyāyas tapa ārjavam \|\|*  
*ahiṁsā satyam akrodhas tyāgaḥ śāntir apaiśunam \|*  
*dayā bhūteṣv aloluptvaṁ mārdavaṁ hrīr acāpalam \|\|*  
*tejaḥ kṣamā dhṛtiḥ śaucam adroho nātimānitā \|*  
*bhavanti saṁpadaṁ daivīm abhijātasya bhārata \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1-3. The Lord said: You may wonder in your mind about the arrangement for attaining knowledge after practicing** ***sāttvika dharma*****. What is its nature? With the intention of removing this doubt, I say that the** ***aśvattha*** **tree of** ***saṁsāra*** **has two fruits. One fruit produces deep bondage for the** ***jīva*** **and the other fruit produces liberation from** ***saṁsāra*****. The** ***jīva*** **is** ***śuddha-sattva*** **by nature. In his state of bondage his** ***śuddha-sattva*** **nature becomes secondary.**

**For the** ***jīva,*** **fearlessness is purification of his consciousness. For purification of consciousness, the scriptures make arrangements for** ***jñāna-yoga*****. All the arrangements of** ***karmas*** **which aims at this purified consciousness are all called** ***daivī sampat*****. All the actions which obstruct this purification of consciousness are called** ***āsurī sampat*****.**

**\[Fearlessness, purification of consciousness,** ***jñāna-yoga,*****\] charity, sense control,** ***yajña,*** **study of the** ***Vedas*****, austerity, sincerity, non-violence, truth, absence of anger, lack of possessiveness, peace, not criticizing others, mercy to living beings, absence of greed, gentleness, humility, steadiness, strength, forgiveness, fortitude, cleanliness, avoiding injury to others, and lack of pride—these twenty-six items are called** ***daivī sampat*****. {If one takes birth at an auspicious time, one attains this** ***sampat*****.}/\[If one takes birth with auspicious desires, one attains this** ***sampat*****.\]**

### Text 4

*dambho darpo ’timānaś ca krodhaḥ pāruṣyam eva ca \|*  
*ajñānaṁ cābhijātasya pārtha saṁpadam āsurīm \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. False pretense, pride, attachment, anger, cruelty and lack of discriminating power \[— these six\] are the** ***āsurī sampat*** **of the person born {at an inauspicious time.}/\[with inauspicious desires.\]**

### Text 5

*daivī saṁpad vimokṣāya nibandhāyāsurī matā \|*  
*mā śucaḥ saṁpadaṁ daivīm abhijāto ’si pāṇḍava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5. The** ***daivī sampat*** **(qualities) leads to liberation and the** ***āsurī sampat*** **(qualities) leads to bondage. O Arjuna! After performing** ***varṇāśrama-dharma,*** **by** ***jñāna-yoga,*** **purification of consciousness will arise. You have attained** ***daivī sampat*** **through your** ***kṣatriya-varṇa*****, because destroying relatives in a war of** ***dharma*** **and shooting arrows according to scriptures is not counted as** ***āsurī sampat*****. Thus hearing these instructions, give up lamentation.**

### Text 6

*dvau bhūta-sargau loke ’smin daiva āsura eva ca \|*  
*daivo vistaraśaḥ prokta āsuraṁ pārtha me śṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. O son of Pṛthā! There are two types of created beings in this world—the divine and the demonic. I have described the divine (*****daivī sampat*****) at length. I will speak about the demonic (*****āsurī sampat*****). Please listen.**

### Text 7

*pravṛttiṁ ca nivṛttiṁ ca janā na vidur āsurāḥ \|*  
*na śaucaṁ nāpi cācāro na satyaṁ teṣu vidyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. Demonic people do not understand the difference between what should be done and what should not be done. They are not attentive to cleanliness, regulated activities, or truthfulness.**

### Text 8

*asatyam apratiṣṭhaṁ te jagad āhur anīśvaram \|*  
*aparaspara-saṁbhūtaṁ kim anyat kāma-haitukam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Those of demonic nature say the world is false, without a foundation, without a creator. They conclude that there is no cause for the creation of the universe through cause and effect. It is without a cause, though there is effect. There is no necessity of a Supreme Lord for producing the world. If there is a Lord, he creates out of material desire. He is not suitable for our worship.**

### Text 9

*etāṁ dṛṣṭim avaṣṭabhya naṣṭātmāno ’lpa-buddhayaḥ \|*  
*prabhavanty ugra-karmāṇaḥ kṣayāya jagato ’hitāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. Taking shelter of this viewpoint, being devoid of** ***ātmā-tattva,*** **unintelligent, performing violent actions, these men with demonic nature are born to bring destruction to the world.**

### Text 10

*kāmam āśritya duṣpūraṁ dambha-māna-madānvitāḥ \|*  
*mohād gṛhītvāsad-grāhān pravartante ’śuci-vratāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. Taking shelter of insatiable lust, filled with false pretense, pride and passion, and practicing vows in an impure state, they engage in all sorts of false doctrines out of bewilderment.**

### Text 11

*cintām aparimeyāṁ ca pralayāntām upāśritāḥ \|*  
*kāmopabhoga-paramā etāvad iti niścitāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. Taking shelter of unlimited thoughts and worries till death, they conclude that the goal is to enjoy sense objects to the utmost*****.***

### Text 12

*āśā-pāśa-śatair baddhāḥ kāma-krodha-parāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*īhante kāma-bhogārtham anyāyenārtha-sancayān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Bound by hundreds of shackles of desire, surrendered to lust and anger, they strive to amass heaps of wealth through illegal means in order to enjoy sense objects.**

### Text 13

*idam adya mayā labdham idaṁ prāpsye manoratham \|*  
*idam astīdam api me bhaviṣyati punar dhanam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. They think, “I have gained this wealth today. I have fulfilled my desire. I have this wealth now. This wealth will also be mine.”**

### Text 14

*asau mayā hataḥ śatrur haniṣye cāparān api \|*  
*īśvaro ’ham ahaṁ bhogī siddho ’haṁ balavān sukhī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. I have killed this enemy. I will kill other enemies also quickly. I am the lord. I am the enjoyer. I am perfect, strong and happy.**

### Text 15

*āḍhyo ’bhijanavān asmi ko ’nyo ’sti sadṛśo mayā \|*  
*yakṣye dāsyāmi modiṣya ity ajñāna-vimohitāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. I am wealthy and have many followers. Who is equal to me? I shall perform sacrifices, give charity and enjoy with women. In this way, bewildered by ignorance, they speak.**

### Text 16

*aneka-citta-vibhrāntā moha-jāla-samāvṛtāḥ \|*  
*prasaktāḥ kāma-bhogeṣu patanti narake’śucau \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. Misled by many thoughts, covered by a net of illusion, and extremely attached to enjoying objects, they fall into unclean hells such as Vaitaraṇi.**

### Text 17

*ātma-saṁbhāvitāḥ stabdhā dhana-māna-madānvitāḥ \|*  
*yajante nāma-yajñais te dambhenāvidhi-pūrvakam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. Respected only by themselves, unsubmissive, filled with wealth, pride and intoxication, they perform worship for show, through** ***yajña*** **in name only, without following scriptural rules.**

### Text 18

*ahaṅkāraṁ balaṁ darpaṁ kāmaṁ krodhaṁ ca saṁśritāḥ \|*  
*mām ātma-para-deheṣu pradviṣanto ’bhyasūyakāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. Surrendered to ego, strength, arrogance, lust, and anger, they hate me, Paramātmā, situated in their own and others’ bodies, and find fault with the qualities of the devotees.**

### Text 19

*tān ahaṁ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṁsāreṣu narādhamān \|*  
*kṣipāmy ajasram aśubhān āsurīṣv eva yoniṣu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. I constantly cast those hateful, cruel, and lowest of humans, into repeated birth and death, into the inauspicious wombs of demons. They increase their demonic nature by actions arising from their natures.**

### Text 20

*āsurīṁ yonim āpannā mūḍhā janmani janmani \|*  
*mām aprāpyaiva kaunteya tato yānty adhamāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Taking birth as demons birth after birth, these fools, not attaining me, go to the lowest position, O son of Kuntī.**

### Text 21

*trividhaṁ narakasyedaṁ dvāraṁ nāśanam ātmanaḥ \|*  
*kāmaḥ krodhas tathā lobhas tasmād etat trayaṁ tyajet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. The three gates leading to hell, which destroy the self are of three types — lust, anger and greed. Therefore, the best people should give up these three.**

### Text 22

*etair vimuktaḥ kaunteya tamo-dvārais tribhir naraḥ \|*  
*ācaraty ātmanaḥ śreyas tato yāti parāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Freeing himself from the gates to hell, a person performs actions to benefit ātmā . When he does that, he attains the supreme goal.**

**Комментарий:**

The meaning is that, taking support of a regulated life, the very form of means of purification of consciousness (mentioned at the beginning of the chapter), performing dharma constantly, one will attain the highest goal, kṛṣṇa-bhakti. The main principle of the methods and goals of karma and jñāna mentioned in scriptures is that when one maintains a relation with pure karma and jñāna, one attains the position of fearlessness in the form of purification of consciousness. That is liberation, whose svarūpa is to be the servant of Bhakti-devī.

### Text 23

*yaḥ śāstra-vidhim utsṛjya vartate kāma-kārataḥ \|*  
*na sa siddhim avāpnoti na sukhaṁ na parāṁ gatim \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. The rules of scripture is to act according to** ***sva-dharma*****. He who give up these rules and acts according to his material desires does not attain perfection, happiness, or the supreme goal.**

**The main principle is that if a human does not take shelter of proper conduct, even though he has attained all types of sense knowledge, he is** ***narādhama*****, a low human being. If a person does not accept dependence on the Lord, even though being endowed with sense knowledge and good conduct, everything is inauspicious for him. The person who does not cultivate** ***bhakti*** **to the Lord with the assistance of pure knowledge even though he accepts dependence on the Lord, is not qualified for the supreme goal. Therefore, the conclusion of all scriptures is that** ***bhakti*** **is most beneficial for the** ***jīva*****.**

### Text 24

*tasmāc chāstraṁ pramāṇaṁ te kāryākārya-vyavasthitau\|*  
*jñātvā śāstra-vidhānoktaṁ karma kartum ihārhasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. Therefore, scripture is the only authority in determining what to do and what not to do. Understanding that bhakti is the conclusion of all scriptures, become qualified to perform karmas.**

**Комментарий:**

Aversion to serving the Lord because of independence is the root offense. It causes jīva’s bondage to māyā, who is a maidservant of the Lord. Being bound by māyā, after giving up a situation of sattva, which reveals the Lord, the jīva, descending to tamas, develops the nature of a demon. At that time, there arise many offenses such as criticism of devotees, belief in many gods, atheism, disrespect for guru, disregard for scriptures, thinking glorification of bhakti is mere praise, establishing jñāna and karma as bhakti , committing sin on the strength of bhakti, equating bhakti with karma and jñāna, not having faith in bhakti and selling bhakti to unqualified people. After rejecting this demonic nature, it is necessary to perform sādhana-bhakti of nine types with faith in scripture. This is taught in this chapter.

## Chapter Seventeen - Śraddhā-traya vibhāga-yoga

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca*  
*ye śāstra-vidhim utsṛjya yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ \|*  
*teṣāṁ niṣṭhā tu kā kṛṣṇa sattvam āho rajas tamaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**1. Having heard this, Arjuna said: O Kṛṣṇa! I have one doubt. You said that persons with faith attain knowledge (BG 4.39). You again said that he who acts on his own whim, giving up rules of scripture does not attain perfection, happiness or the supreme goal. (BG 16.23) I ask, if one cultivates faith {which is opposite of what is in scriptures}/\[while rejecting scriptures\], what happens? Does a faithful person attain the result of purification of consciousness of being situated in** ***jñāna-yoga*** **or not? Please explain clearly. Is the faith of those who giving up rules of scripture, taking shelter of faith born of customary conduct, perform worship of** ***devatās*** **and others, sattvic, rajasic or tamasic?**

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*trividhā bhavati śraddhā dehināṁ sā svabhāva-jā \|*  
*sāttvikī rājasī caiva tāmasī ceti tāṁ śṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. The Lord said: The faith of embodied beings is of three types, arising from their natures (previous impressions). It is sattvic, rajasic or tamasic.**

### Text 3

*sattvānurūpā sarvasya śraddhā bhavati bhārata \|*  
*śraddhā-mayo ’yaṁ puruṣo yo yac-chraddhaḥ sa eva saḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. O descendent of Bharata! All humans have faith. This faith corresponds to the nature of his consciousness. Whatever faith he has, that becomes his** ***svarūpa*****.**

**The main principle is this. The** ***jīva*** **by his nature is my** ***aṁśa*****. Thus, he is** ***nirguṇa*****. The** ***jīva*** **who is forgetful concerning me has become endowed with** ***guṇas*****. As long as he is in a bound state, because of ancient impressions, his nature with** ***guṇas*** **has manifested. From that nature is formed his** ***antaḥkaraṇa*****. That** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **is his consciousness (*****sattva*****). Purity of that consciousness is the position of fearlessness. The faith of purified consciousness is the seed of** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **and faith of impure consciousness is endowed with** ***guṇas*****. As long as the faith is not** ***nirguṇa*** **or aimed at** ***nirguṇa,*** **it is called** ***kāma.*** **I will explain the subject of faith in the** ***guṇas*** **filled with** ***kāma*****.**

### Text 4

*yajante sāttvikā devān yakṣa-rakṣāṁsi rājasāḥ \|*  
*pretān bhūta-gaṇāṁś cānye yajante tāmasā janāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. Those with sattvic faith worship the** ***devatās*****. Those with rajasic faith worship the Yakṣas and Rākṣasas. Those with tamasic faith worship the Pretas and Bhūtas.**

### Texts 5-6

*aśāstra-vihitaṁ ghoraṁ tapyante ye tapo janāḥ \|*  
*dambhāhaṁkāra-saṁyuktāḥ kāma-rāga-balānvitāḥ \|\|*  
*karśayantaḥ śarīra-sthaṁ bhūta-grāmam acetasaḥ \|*  
*māṁ caivāntaḥ-śarīra-sthaṁ tān viddhy āsura-niścayān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5-6. Those people endowed with ostentation and pride, filled with attachment, lust and strength, undergo extreme austerities not prescribed in the scriptures. Torturing the elements situated in the body by the extreme austerities such as fasting, giving suffering to the** ***jīva*** **who is my** ***aṁśa,*** **they are fixed in demonic nature.**

### Text 7

*āhāras tv api sarvasya trividho bhavati priyaḥ \|*  
*yajñas tapas tathā dānaṁ teṣāṁ bhedam imaṁ śṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. You should know that food that people enjoy, their sacrifice, austerity and charity have three divisions of** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*****.**

### Text 8

*āyuḥ-sattva-balārogya-sukha-prīti-vivardhanāḥ \|*  
*rasyāḥ snigdhāḥ sthirā hṛdyā āhārāḥ sattvika-priyāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. Foods which prolong life span, increase will, strength, immunity to disease, happiness and appetite, and are tasty, mild, substantial, and beneficial to the body are dear to those in** ***sattva-guṇa*****.**

### Text 9

*kaṭv-amla-lavaṇāty-uṣṇa-tīkṣṇa-rūkṣa-vidāhinaḥ \|*  
*āhārā rājasasyeṣṭā duḥkha-śokāmaya-pradāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. Foods that are excessively bitter like** ***nīma*****, excessively sour like amla, excessively salty, excessively hot, excessively pungent like pepper and chilies, excessively dry and burning, such as {roasted chickpea,} ground mustard seeds, and which produce pain while eating, sorrow afterwards and finally disease, are dear to the rajasic person.**

### Text 10

*yāta-yāmaṁ gata-rasaṁ pūti paryuṣitaṁ ca yat \|*  
*ucchiṣṭam api cāmedhyaṁ bhojanaṁ tāmasa-priyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. Stale food (cooked over one** ***prahara*** **previously), food without taste, bad-smelling or rotten food, leftovers from others (other than that of** ***gurus*****), and impure foods such as wine and meat, are dear to the tamasic person.**

### Text 11

*aphalākāṅkṣibhir yajño vidhi-dṛṣṭo ya ijyate \|*  
*yaṣṭavyam eveti manaḥ samādhāya sa sāttvikaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. That sacrifice which is performed without desire for material results, with the mentality that it should be performed because of scriptural injunction, is sattvic*****.***

### Text 12

*abhisandhāya tu phalaṁ dambhārtham api caiva yat \|*  
*ijyate bharata-śreṣṭha taṁ yajñaṁ viddhi rājasam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Know that the sacrifice performed with intention of enjoying the result or for ostentation is rajasic, O best of Bharata’s lineage.**

### Text 13

*vidhi-hīnam asṛṣṭānnaṁ mantra-hīnam adakṣiṇam \|*  
*śraddhā-virahitaṁ yajñaṁ tāmasaṁ paricakṣate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13. They say that the sacrifice which is contrary to scripture, with no food given in charity, without proper** ***mantras*****, in which the priests are not given donation, and which is performed without faith, is tamasic.**

**Tamasic faith cannot be accepted as faith since it is very distorted from the very nature of faith.**

### Text 14

*deva-dvija-guru-prājña-pūjanaṁ śaucam ārjavam \|*  
*brahmacaryam ahiṁsā ca śārīraṁ tapa ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. Here are the different types of austerity. Austerity of the body consists of worship of** ***devas, brāhmaṇas, guru*** **and the wise, as well as purity, sincerity, celibacy and non-violence*****.***

### Text 15

*anudvega-karaṁ vākyaṁ satyaṁ priya-hitaṁ ca yat \|*  
*svādhyāyābhyasanaṁ caiva vāṅ-mayaṁ tapa ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. Speech which does not disturb others, which is true, agreeable and beneficial, and as well, the recitation and study of the Vedas, is austerity of speech.**

### Text 16

*manaḥ-prasādaḥ saumyatvaṁ maunam ātma-vinigrahaḥ \|*  
*bhāva-saṁśuddhir ity etat tapo mānasam ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. Austerity of the mind consists of tranquility, gentleness, silence, control of the mind, and purity of the heart.**

### Text 17

*śraddhayā parayā taptaṁ tapas tat tri-vidhaṁ naraiḥ \|*  
*aphalākāṅkṣibhir yuktaiḥ sattvikaṁ paricakṣate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. When these three types of austerity are practiced with full faith aiming at** ***bhakti*** **to the Lord and without longing for results, they are said to be sattvic austerity.**

### Text 18

*satkāra-māna-pūjārthaṁ tapo dambhena caiva yat \|*  
*kriyate tad iha proktaṁ rājasaṁ calam adhruvam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. Austerity undertaken for praise from others and for respect from others, thinking, “They will call me a good person” or undertaken for show alone to attain worship, is said to be rajasic. It is temporary and uncertain in result.**

### Text 19

*mūḍha-grāheṇātmano yat pīḍayā kriyate tapaḥ \|*  
*parasyotsādanārthaṁ vā tat tāmasam udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. Austerity performed out of foolishness, inflicting pain on oneself or for the purpose of destroying others, is tamasic austerity.**

### Text 20

*dātavyam iti yad dānaṁ dīyate ’nupakāriṇe \|*  
*deśe kāle ca pātre ca tad dānaṁ sattvikaṁ smṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. Here are the different types of charity. That charity performed without expecting repayment, with the conviction that it should be given, and given to a proper person, at the proper place and time, is known as sattvic charity.**

### Text 21

*yat tu pratyupakārārthaṁ phalam uddiśya vā punaḥ \|*  
*dīyate ca parikliṣṭaṁ tad dānaṁ rājasaṁ smṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. That gift given with expectation of reciprocation, with desire for results such as Svarga, or given with later repentance for giving, is said to be rajasic.**

### Text 22

*adeśa-kāle yad dānam apātrebhyaś ca dīyate \|*  
*asat-kṛtam avajñātaṁ tat tāmasam udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. Charity to an undeserving person (a dancer, prostitute or person not in need), at the wrong place (where not necessary), and at the wrong time (when it is not useful to anyone), is said to be tamasic charity. If given to a deserving person, but without respect and with disrespect it is also tamasic.**

### Texts 23-24

*oṁ tat sad iti nirdeśo brahmaṇas tri-vidhaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇās tena vedāś ca yajñāś ca vihitāḥ purā \|\|*  
*tasmād oṁ ity udāhṛtya yajña-dāna-tapaḥ-kriyāḥ \|*  
*pravartante vidhānoktāḥ satataṁ brahma-vādinām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23-24. I will speak the purport. Please listen. There are three types of austerity, sacrifice, charity and food: sattvic, rajasic and tamasic. The faith present in undertaking these while in the** ***guṇas,*** **even if excellent, medium or inferior, is all within the** ***guṇas*** **and of no value.**

**If all** ***karmas*** **are undertaken with faith, aiming at** ***bhakti,*** **i.e., with** ***nirguṇa*** **faith, then they are suitable for attaining fearlessness, in the form of purification of consciousness. Everywhere in scriptures it is taught that one should perform** ***karmas*** **with this supreme faith.**

**In the scriptures, the three arrangements indicating Brahman, in the form of** ***oṁ tat sat,*** **are seen. With these indications of Brahman,** ***brāhmaṇas*****, the Vedas and** ***yajña*** **were ordained.**

**Having faith while giving up the rules of scripture, is within the** ***guṇas,*** **indicates something other than Brahman and is full of material desires.**

**Therefore, supreme faith is prescribed in all scriptures. Your doubt concerning scriptures and faith has arisen from lack of discrimination.**

**Thus, the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **perform** ***yajña,*** **charity, austerity and** ***kriyās*** **according to scriptures while \[always\] using the** ***oṁ*** **which indicates Brahman.**

### Text 25

*tad ity anabhisandhāya phalaṁ yajña-tapaḥ-kriyāḥ \|*  
*dāna-kriyāś ca vividhāḥ kriyante mokṣa-kāṅkṣibhiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. In order to attain liberation from material bondage, aiming for the spiritual or** ***tat*** **object beyond the** ***atat*** **or impermanent object, one should undertake** ***yajña,*** **austerity, charity and various actions while giving up \[desire for\] direct material results.**

### Text 26

*sad-bhāve sādhu-bhāve ca sad ity etat prayujyate \|*  
*praśaste karmaṇi tathā sac-chabdaḥ pārtha yujyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26*****.*** **The word** ***sat*** **means Brahman and also the seekers of Brahman. The word** ***sat*** **indicates that all excellent** ***karmas*** **which aim for Brahman.**

### Text 27

*yajñe tapasi dāne ca sthitiḥ sad iti cocyate \|*  
*karma caiva tad-arthīyaṁ sad ity evābhidhīyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. Yajña, usterity and charity are the purport of the word sat since all these karmas are meant for the purpose of sat, i.e., when Brahman is to be indicated, one uses the word sat.**

**Комментарий:**

When it does not aim for Brahman, the yajña, austerity and charity is asat. All material karmas are contrary to the svarūpa of the jīva but when all these karmas are fixed in Brahman, and are dedicated to making pure bhakti appear, then all these karmas are suitable for purification of the consciousness, i.e., for perfecting one’s svarūpa as servant of Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 28

*aśraddhayā hutaṁ dattaṁ tapas taptaṁ kṛtaṁ ca yat \|*  
*asad ity ucyate pārtha na ca tat pretya no iha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. O Arjuna! Whatever is offered in the fire, whatever is given in charity, whatever is undertaken as austerity, but which is done without faith, is all asat.**  
**All such karmas do not give benefit in this world or the next, at any time.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus, all the scriptures teach *nirguṇa* faith. If one gives up scriptures, one must give up *nirguṇa* faith. Thus *nirguṇa* faith is the only seed for the creeper of *bhakti*.

Scriptural faith is necessary for the bound *jīva*. In the *jīva’s* bound state there are three types of faith arising one’s nature—in *sattva*, where devatās are worshipped, in *rajas*, where *yakṣas* are worshipped and in *tamas* where *bhūtas* are worshipped. Though *sāttvika* faith is the best of these, in order to attain a position beyond the guṇas, one should take shelter of *nirguṇa* faith based on scriptures. It is very difficult to attain *nirguṇa* faith, by becoming detached through *karma-yoga* mentioned in the first six chapters. The *nirguṇa* faith that arises by devotee association and whose subject is topic of the Lord, mentioned in the middle six chapters, is easy to achieve. That faith is called *niṣṭhā*. When *niṣṭhā* becomes ripe it is called *ruci*, and then *āsakti*. Becoming *bhāva* it finally appears as *prema*. This is the final goal of the *jīva*. Therefore, giving up attempts at indifference to matter, when one chants the Lord’s name indicated by *oṁ*, while having *nirguṇa* faith one conquers all tendencies of *saṁsāra*.

## Chapter Eighteen - Mokṣa-yoga

### Text 1

*arjuna uvāca*  
*sannyāsasya mahā-bāho tattvam icchāmi veditum \|*  
*tyāgasya ca hṛṣīkeśa pṛthak keśī-niṣūdana \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

1\. In the first six chapters it was clearly stated that *bhakti* is the auspicious final result of all actions. In the second six chapters the *svarūpa* of *nirguṇa-bhakti* was described. In the third six chapters *jñāna, vairāgya,* distinguishing what to do from what not to do, and *bhakti* as the final result through deliberation on *guṇa* and *nirguṇa* are described.

The deep purport of the *Gītā* was shown by previous great devotees. All the said teachings were complete by the end of the seventeenth chapter. Having heard them, Arjuna, desiring to hear all the *tattvas* in brief as a summary, inquired.

**Перевод:**

**O Hṛṣīkeśa! O Keśī-nisūdana! I desire to hear the conclusion about sannyāsa and tyāga as distinct items.**

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*kāmyānāṁ karmaṇāṁ nyāsaṁ sannyāsaṁ kavayo viduḥ \|*  
*sarva-karma-phala-tyāgaṁ prāhus tyāgaṁ vicakṣaṇāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**2. The Lord said: The learned know that** ***sannyāsa*** **means to give up all** ***kāmya-karmas*** **completely while performing** ***nitya*** **and** ***naimittika karmas*** **without desire. The learned say that** ***tyāga*** **means giving up the** ***results*** **of all** ***nitya, naimittika*** **and** ***kāmya karmas*****. The wise have made this distinction between** ***sannyāsa*** **and** ***tyāga*****.**

### Text 3

*tyājyaṁ doṣa-vad ity eke karma prāhur manīṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*yajña-dāna-tapaḥ-karma na tyājyam iti cāpare \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**3. Concerning** ***tyāga,*** **some learned persons say that all** ***karmas*** **should be given up because they are all faulty. Other learned persons say that activities like sacrifice, charity and austerity should not be given up.**

### Text 4

*niścayaṁ śṛṇu me tatra tyāge bharata-sattama \|*  
*tyāgo hi puruṣa-vyāghra tri-vidhaḥ samprakīrtitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**4. Hear from me the definite conclusion regarding** ***tyāga*****, O best descendent of Bharata. O best among men,** ***tyāga*** **also is said to be of three types.**

### Text 5

*yajña-dāna-tapaḥ-karma na tyājyaṁ kāryam eva tat \|*  
*yajño dānaṁ tapaś caiva pāvanāni manīṣiṇām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**5.** ***Yajña,*** **charity and austerity should not be given up completely, but should be performed by all \[intelligent\] men. The bound** ***jīva*** **should perform them as the main method of purification of consciousness \[and for maintaining life in this body\].**

### Text 6

*etāny api tu karmāṇi saṅgaṁ tyaktvā phalāni ca \|*  
*kartavyānīti me pārtha niścitaṁ matam uttamam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**6. All** ***karmas*** **must be done as duty, but without attachment and without longing for results. This is the highest conclusion.**

### Text 7

*niyatasya tu sannyāsaḥ karmaṇo nopapadyate \|*  
*mohāt tasya parityāgas tāmasaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**7. It is not possible to give up** ***nitya-karmas*****. Rejection of** ***nitya-karmas*** **arising from ignorance of scripture is** ***tyāga*** **in** ***tamas*****.**

### Text 8

*duḥkham ity eva yat karma kāya-kleśa-bhayāt tyajet \|*  
*sa kṛtvā rājasaṁ tyāgaṁ naiva tyāga-phalaṁ labhet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**8. One who gives up** ***nitya-karmas*** **out of fear of bodily pain, performs** ***tyāga*** **in the mode of** ***rajas*****, and does not attain the result of that** ***tyāga.***

### Text 9

*kāryam ity eva yat karma niyataṁ kriyate ’rjuna \|*  
*saṅgaṁ tyaktvā phalaṁ caiva sa tyāgaḥ sāttviko mataḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**9. O Arjuna! That** ***tyāga*** **in which** ***nitya-karmas*** **are performed out of duty, but giving up attachment and desire for results, is considered** ***tyāga*** **in the mode of** ***sattva*****.**

### Text 10

*na dveṣṭy akuśalaṁ karma kuśale nānuṣajjate \|*  
*tyāgī sattva-samāviṣṭo medhāvī chinna-saṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**10. The wise renunciate situated in** ***sattva-guṇa*****, devoid of doubt, does not hate uncomfortable** ***karmas*** **nor becomes attached to comfortable** ***karmas*****.**

### Text 11

*na hi deha-bhṛtā śakyaṁ tyaktuṁ karmāṇy aśeṣataḥ \|*  
*yas tu karma-phala-tyāgī sa tyāgīty abhidhīyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**11. It is not possible for the** ***jīva*** **with a body to give up all** ***karmas*** **completely. Thus he who gives up the results of all** ***karmas*** **is the real** ***tyāgī*****.**

### Text 12

*aniṣṭam iṣṭaṁ miśraṁ ca tri-vidhaṁ karmaṇaḥ phalam \|*  
*bhavaty atyāgināṁ pretya na tu sannyāsīnāṁ kvacit \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**12. Those who do not renounce the results of** ***karmas*** **get three types of results: in the form of hellish suffering (undesirable), heavenly enjoyment (desirable) or human birth (mixed) in the next life. The** ***sannyāsī*** **does not experience these three types of results.**

### Text 13

*pañcaitāni mahā-bāho kāraṇāni nibodha me \|*  
*sāṅkhye kṛtānte proktāni siddhaye sarva-karmaṇām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**13*****.*** **O mighty-armed one! I will speak about the five factors necessary for completion of** ***karmas*** **mentioned in the** ***Vedānta.*** **Please listen.**

### Text 14

*adhiṣṭhānaṁ tathā kartā karaṇaṁ ca pṛthag-vidham \|*  
*vividhāś ca pṛthak ceṣṭā daivaṁ caivātra pañcamam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**14. These five factors are the body (*****adhiṣṭhāna*****), the false ego (*****kartā*****)—the knot between consciousness and matter, the senses of various types (*****karaṇa*****), the life airs with various functions (*****ceṣṭā*****), and assistance of the regulator of all affairs in the universe (*****daiva*****). Without these five factors, action cannot be performed.**

### Text 15

*śarīra-vāṅ-manobhir yat karma prārabhate naraḥ \|*  
*nyāyyaṁ vā viparītaṁ vā pañcaite tasya hetavaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**15. All activities performed by man, by body, words and mind, whether lawful or unlawful, are accomplished by five causes alone.**

### Text 16

*tatraivaṁ sati kartāram ātmānaṁ kevalaṁ tu yaḥ \|*  
*paśyaty akṛta-buddhitvān na sa paśyati durmatiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**16. Though these five are the causes of action, the person who sees only himself as the doer is an unintelligent fool. He does not see things as they are.**

### Text 17

*yasya nāhaṅkṛto bhāvo buddhir yasya na lipyate \|*  
*hatvāpi sa imāl lokān na hanti na nibadhyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**17. O Arjuna! Your bewilderment in fighting arose only from your sense of** ***ahaṅkāra*****. If you know that the five causes are causes of all action you cannot be bewildered. Thus, one who is not bewildered by** ***ahaṅkāra*****, though killing all people, does not kill anyone and is not bound by results of the karma of killing.**

### Text 18

*jñānaṁ jñeyaṁ parijñātā tri-vidhā karma-codanā \|*  
*karaṇaṁ karma karteti tri-vidhaḥ karma-saṅgrahaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**18. The principles for action (*****karma-codanā*****) are the process for gaining knowledge (*****jñānam*****), the object of knowledge (*****jñeyam*****) and the knower. Or, stating it another way, the factors in performance of action are the instrument, the object and the doer (subject). These three are the conglomerate of action (*****karma-saṁgraha*****).**

**In all actions that a human performs, there are two states:** ***codanā*** **and** ***saṁgraha*****. The rule that supports action before the action is performed is called** ***codanā*****.** ***Codanā*** **means “setting in motion.” That impelling force is the subtle portion of action. The state of knowledge before the gross existence of action is called the impelling state. The state previous to the action is divided into the knowledge of the instrument of action, the object to be known by the action and the state of the doer who is a knower. In the state of the action, in gross form, the three divisions are the instrument of action, the object of action and the doer.**

### Text 19

*jñānaṁ karma ca kartā ca tridhaiva guṇa-bhedataḥ \|*  
*procyate guṇa-saṅkhyāne yathāvac chṛṇu tāny api \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**19. I will speak about the three types of knowing, action and the knower or doer, according to** ***sattva, rajas*** **and** ***tamas*****. Please listen.**

### Text 20

*sarva-bhūteṣu yenaikaṁ bhāvam avyayam īkṣate \|*  
*avibhaktaṁ vibhakteṣu taj jñānaṁ viddhi sāttvikam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**20. One** ***jīva*** **is present in all life forms such as human in order to enjoy various results. Though existing amidst temporary objects, he is indestructible. Though the many** ***jīvas*** **are each separate, they have one form as conscious entities. This knowledge is called** ***sāttvika*** **knowledge.**

### Text 21

*pṛthaktvena tu yaj jñānaṁ nānā-bhāvān pṛthag-vidhān \|*  
*vetti sarveṣu bhūteṣu taj jñānaṁ viddhi rājasam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**21. All the** ***jīvas*** **in various wombs such as human or animals are separate types of** ***jīvas*****. They all have separate types of** ***svarūpa*****. Such knowledge is** ***rājasika*****.**

### Text 22

*yat tu kṛtsna-vad ekasmin kārye saktam ahaitukam \|*  
*atattvārtha-vad alpaṁ ca tat tāmasam udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**22. He who is attached to bodily actions like bathing and eating, seeing them as important acts, his knowledge is meager and tamasic because such knowledge, though false, appears to be natural (*****ahaitukam*****). One cannot attain real value in the form of truth from it.**

**The conclusion is that knowledge of** ***tat*** **beyond the body is sattvic knowledge. Knowledge of scriptures like** ***nyāya*** **which produce various philosophies is rajasic knowledge and the knowledge of worldly actions like bathing and eating is tamasic knowledge.**

### Text 23

*niyataṁ saṅga-rahitam arāga-dveṣataḥ kṛtam \|*  
*aphala-prepsunā karma yat tat sāttvikam ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**23. That action which is performed regularly (*****nitya-karma*****), without attachment, without desire or hatred, without desire for results, is called** ***sāttvika*** **action.**

### Text 24

*yat tu kāmepsunā karma sāhaṅkāreṇa vā punaḥ \|*  
*kriyate bahulāyāsaṁ tad rājasam udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**24. That action which is performed with desire for results, or with strong identity as the doer, with plenty of exertion, is called rajasic action.**

### Text 25

*anubandhaṁ kṣayaṁ hiṁsām anapekṣya ca pauruṣam \|*  
*mohād ārabhyate karma yat tat tāmasam ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**25. That worldly action leading to future suffering, which is destructive to** ***dharma*** **and knowledge, harmful to others, and performed without prior consideration, out of bewilderment, is called tamasic action.**

### Text 26

*mukta-saṅgo ’nahaṁ-vādī dhṛty-utsāha-samanvitaḥ \|*  
*siddhy-asiddhyor nirvikāraḥ kartā sāttvika ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**26. The agent or doer free from attachment and false ego, endowed with fortitude and enthusiasm, unperturbed in success or failure, is in** ***sattva*****.**

### Text 27

*rāgī karma-phala-prepsur lubdho hiṁsātmako ’śuciḥ \|*  
*harṣa-śokānvitaḥ kartā rājasaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**27. The agent who is attached to his work, who desires the results of his actions, who is attached to the enjoyment of the sense objects, who likes giving pain to others, who is unclean and subject to joy and sorrow, is known to be in the mode of** ***rajas.***

### Text 28

*ayuktaḥ prākṛtaḥ stabdhaḥ śaṭho naiṣkṛtiko ’lasaḥ \|*  
*viṣādī dīrgha-sūtrī ca kartā tāmasa ucyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**28. One who is fond of doing what should not be done, who is absorbed in material actions, who dull, who is deceitful, who offends others, who is lazy, always dejected, and delays the work, is known as a tamasic worker.**

### Text 29

*buddher bhedaṁ dhṛteś caiva guṇatas tri-vidhaṁ śṛṇu \|*  
*procyamānam aśeṣeṇa pṛthaktvena dhanañjaya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**29. O conqueror of wealth, listen to the three types of intelligence and determination, according to** ***sattva, rajas,*** **and** ***tamas,*** **which will be discussed completely.**

### Text 30

*pravṛttiṁ ca nivṛttiṁ ca kāryākārye bhayābhaye \|*  
*bandhaṁ mokṣaṁ ca yā vetti buddhiḥ sā pārtha sāttvikī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**30. The intelligence of** ***sattva-guṇa*** **understands the difference between** ***dharma*** **and** ***adharma*****, what should be done and what should not be done, what is to be feared and not feared, and the difference between bondage and liberation.**

### Text 31

*yayā dharmam adharmaṁ ca kāryaṁ cākāryam eva ca \|*  
*ayathāvat prajānāti buddhiḥ sā pārtha rājasī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**31. That intelligence is of** ***rajo-guṇa*****, which understands incompletely the difference between** ***dharma*** **and** ***adharma*****, and what is to be done as duty and what is not to be done as duty.**

### Text 32

*adharmaṁ dharmam iti yā manyate tamasāvṛtā \|*  
*sarvārthān viparītāṁś ca buddhiḥ sā pārtha tāmasī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**32. That intelligence covered by illusion is in** ***tamo-guṇa*** **by which one considers** ***adharma*** **to be** ***dharma*** **and sees all things contrary to the truth.**

### Text 33

*dhṛtyā yayā dhārayate manaḥ-prāṇendriya-kriyāḥ \|*  
*yogenāvyabhicāriṇyā dhṛtiḥ sā pārtha sāttvikī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**33. O Pārtha! Determination by which one restrains the activities of the mind, life airs and senses, using unswerving determination, is in** ***sattva*****.**

### Text 34

*yayā tu dharma-kāmārthān dhṛtyā dhārayate ’rjuna \|*  
*prasaṅgena phalākāṅkṣī dhṛtiḥ sā pārtha rājasī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**34*****.*** **That determination desiring results, by which one restrains the senses and mind to attain** ***dharma, artha*** **and** ***kāma*****, is in** ***rajas.***

### Text 35

*yayā svapnaṁ bhayaṁ śokaṁ viṣādaṁ madam eva ca \|*  
*na vimuñcati durmedhā dhṛtiḥ sā pārtha tāmasī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**35. That determination by which one does not give up sleep, fear, lamentation, dejection and passion is in** ***tamas*****.**

### Texts 36-37

*sukhaṁ tv idānīṁ tri-vidhaṁ śṛṇu me bharatarṣabha \|*  
*abhyāsād ramate yatra duḥkhāntaṁ ca nigacchati \|\|*  
*yat tad agre viṣam iva pariṇāme ’mṛtopamam \|*  
*tat sukhaṁ sattvikaṁ proktam ātma-buddhi-prasāda-jam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**36-37. O best of Bharata’s lineage, hear from me about the three types of happiness. The happiness by which the bound** ***jīva*** **cultivating again and again, attains pleasure through practice, and sometimes attaining detachment, brings an end to suffering in the form of** ***saṁsāra,*** **that happiness which in the beginning brings difficulty but finally produces peace by understanding** ***ātmā,*** **like nectar, is** ***sāttvika*** **happiness.**

### Text 38

*viṣayendriya-saṁyogād yat tad agre ’mṛtopamam \|*  
*pariṇāme viṣam iva tat sukhaṁ rājasaṁ smṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**38. That happiness which, arising from contact with objects of the senses, experienced like nectar in the beginning and like poison in the end is known to be in the mode of** ***rajas*****.**

### Text 39

*yad agre cānubandhe ca sukhaṁ mohanam ātmanaḥ \|*  
*nidrālasya-pramādotthaṁ tat tāmasam udāhṛtam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**39. That happiness is said to be of** ***tamo-guṇa*** **which arises from sleep, laziness and intoxication and produces an illusion for the self from beginning to end.**

### Text 40

*na tad asti pṛthivyāṁ vā divi deveṣu vā punaḥ \|*  
*sattvaṁ prakṛti-jair muktaṁ yad ebhiḥ syāt tribhir guṇaiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**40. There is no** ***jīva*** **among humans on earth or** ***devatās*** **in heaven who is free from these three** ***guṇas*** **arising from** ***prakṛti.***

**All** ***jñānīs*** **and** ***karmīs*** **are controlled by the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****. The devotees accept the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*** **only for maintaining their lives. Actually, their existence is separate from the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****. Thus, by direct vision, they can see everyone covered by the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****.**

### Text 41

*brāhmaṇa-kṣatriya-viśāṁ śūdrāṇāṁ ca parantapa \|*  
*karmāṇi pravibhaktāni svabhāva-prabhavair guṇaiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**41. O afflicter of enemies! The three** ***guṇas*** **are manifest by the birth of the** ***jīva*** **bound by** ***prakṛti*****. By these** ***guṇas*** **produced by their previous impressions, the** ***karmas*** **of** ***brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas*** **and** ***śūdras*** **are divided.**

### Text 42

*śamo damas tapaḥ śaucaṁ kṣāntir ārjavam eva ca \|*  
*jñānaṁ vijñānam āstikyaṁ brahma-karma svabhāva-jam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**42. The natural activities of the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **are control of the mind, control of the external senses, austerity, cleanliness, tolerance, honesty, knowledge of scripture, realization, and faith in the scriptures.**

### Text 43

*śauryaṁ tejo dhṛtir dākṣyaṁ yuddhe cāpy apalāyanam \|*  
*dānam īśvara-bhāvaś ca kṣātraṁ karma svabhāva-jam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**43. The natural activities of the** ***kṣatriya*** **are courage, confidence, fortitude, expertise in fighting, not fleeing from battle, charity and controlling others.**

### Text 44

*kṛṣi-go-rakṣya-vāṇijyaṁ vaiśya-karma svabhāva-jam \|*  
*paricaryātmakaṁ karma śūdrasyāpi svabhāva-jam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**44. The natural duties of the** ***vaiśya*** **are agriculture, raising cows, and commerce. The natural duty of the** ***śūdra*** **is service to the other** ***varṇas*****—*****brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas*** **and** ***vaiśyas*****.**

**The** ***varṇas*** **of humans are defined by these four types of natures, not just by birth.**

### Text 45

*sve sve karmaṇy abhirataḥ saṁsiddhiṁ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*sva-karma-nirataḥ siddhiṁ yathā vindati tac chṛṇu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**45. Man achieves complete success being engaged in his particular duties. Hear how one attains success by engagement in one’s duties.**

### Text 46

*yataḥ pravṛttir bhūtānāṁ yena sarvam idaṁ tatam \|*  
*sva-karmaṇā tam abhyarcya siddhiṁ vindati mānavaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**46. Men achieve success by worshipping through their** ***karmas*** **the Lord who is spread everywhere in the universe as** ***vyaṣṭi*** **and** ***samaṣṭi*****, and by whom all beings are endowed with previous impressions according their nature.**

### Text 47

*śreyān sva-dharmo viguṇaḥ para-dharmāt sv-anuṣṭhitāt \|*  
*svabhāva-niyataṁ karma kurvan nāpnoti kilbiṣam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**47. It is better to perform one’s own duties (*****sva-dharma*****) with fault than to perform another’s duties perfectly since** ***karma*** **according to one’s nature is called** ***sva-dharma*****. Even if sometimes the** ***karmas*** **are performed incompletely, by that** ***sva-dharma*** **there is everlasting benefit. No sin is possible by performing** ***karmas*** **according to one’s nature.**

### Text 48

*saha-jaṁ karma kaunteya sa-doṣam api na tyajet \|*  
*sarvārambhā hi doṣeṇa dhūmenāgnir ivāvṛtāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**48. O son of Kuntī! One should not give up work, which is according to your nature, even if it has some fault. All undertakings are covered by fault. Just as fire is covered by smoke, so** ***karmas*** **are covered by fault.**

**Rejecting the faulty part, take shelter of the good part of the** ***karma*** **according to one’s nature for purification of consciousness.**

### Text 49

*asakta-buddhiḥ sarvatra jitātmā vigata-spṛhaḥ \|*  
*naiṣkarmya-siddhiṁ paramāṁ sannyāsenādhigacchati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**49. The person desiring to advance, whose intelligence is unattached to material objects, whose mind is under control, and who is devoid of desire for material happiness up to Brahma-loka, while giving up** ***karmas*** **in their** ***svarūpa,*** **attains the highest perfection, in the form of complete renunciation of action.**

### Text 50

*siddhiṁ prāpto yathā brahma tathāpnoti nibodha me \|*  
*samāsenaiva kaunteya niṣṭhā jñānasya yā parā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**50. I will explain in brief, O son of Kuntī, how a person, having attained cessation of all work, attains Brahman, the final attainment of** ***jñāna.***

### Texts 51-53

*buddhyā viśuddhayā yukto dhṛtyātmānaṁ niyamya ca \|*  
*śabdādīn viṣayāṁs tyaktvā rāga-dveṣau vyudasya ca \|\|*
