*vivikta-sevī laghv-āśī yata-vāk-kāya-mānasaḥ \|*  
*dhyāna-yoga-paro nityaṁ vairāgyaṁ samupāśritaḥ \|\|*  
*ahaṅkāraṁ balaṁ darpaṁ kāmaṁ krodhaṁ parigrahaṁ \|*  
*vimucya nirmamaḥ śanto brahma-bhūyāya kalpate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**51-53. That person who is endowed with pure intelligence, with mind controlled by determination; who has given up all the sense objects such as sound; who has eliminated attachment and repulsion; who resorts to solitude, eats little, controls speech, body and mind; who is completely absorbed in contemplating the Lord; who has taken shelter of detachment; who is free from false ego; who is devoid of strength related to material attachment, devoid of pride, lust, anger, and possessions; who is free from possessive instinct; and who is peaceful, can attain realization of Brahman, the** ***svarūpa*** **of eight qualities.**

### Text 54

*brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā na śocati na kāṅkṣati \|*  
*samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu mad-bhaktiṁ labhate parām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**54. When the** ***jīva*** **is free of material** ***upādhis*****, he attains a state of Brahman {in his** ***svarūpa*** **of eight qualities}/\[in his** ***svarūpa*** **of uncovered consciousness\]. That person, having attained the** ***svarūpa*** **of Brahman, being satisfied, being equal to all beings, does not lament or hanker. Eventually, being fixed in the state of Brahman, he attains** ***nirguṇa bhakti,*** **full dedication to me.**

### Text 55

*bhaktyā mām abhijānāti yāvān yaś cāsmi tattvataḥ \|*  
*tato māṁ tattvato jñātvā viśate tad-anantaram \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**55. When** ***nirguṇa bhakti*** **arises, the** ***jīva*** **can know me in detail with my** ***svarūpa*** **and nature. When he develops knowledge related to me, the** ***jīva*** **attains me.**

**This is secret knowledge related to me. It is called attaining Brahman in the form of accepting the** ***āśrama*** **of** ***sannyāsa*** **by performing** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **as a member of the** ***varṇas*****. Its final result is** ***nirguṇa bhakti*** **or** ***prema*****.**

**He enters (*****viśate*****) me. This expression does not mean the dry, foolish concept of destroying the self. It should be understood that “entering me” mean attaining my** ***svarūpa,*** **the highest conscious entity, when one becomes free from matter in one’s** ***svarūpa*****. This attainment of my** ***svarūpa*** **is called pure** ***prema*** **for the Lord.**

### Text 56

*sarva-karmāṇy api sadā kurvāṇo mad-vyapāśrayaḥ \|*  
*mat-prasādād avāpnoti śāśvataṁ padam avyayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**56. {I have called the Vedic method of attaining** ***bhakti*** **by** ***jñāna*****—which is attained by** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*****—the secret method of attaining me. This is one among the three methods which I will clearly explain. Now I will explain the second method, worshipping the Lord (*****īśvara*****). Please listen.**

**Having taken shelter of me, in an inferior way (with some material desires), when one offers all** ***karmas*** **to me understanding me as** ***īśvara*****, by my mercy, in the end that person attains the indestructible, eternal position of** ***nirguṇa bhakti*****.}**

**56. \[My dedicated devotee, offering all** ***karmas*** **at all times, by my mercy, in the end attains the indestructible, eternal, spiritual abode.\]**

### Text 57

*cetasā sarva-karmāṇi mayi sannyasya mat-paraḥ \|*  
*buddhi-yogam upāśritya mac-cittaḥ satataṁ bhava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**57. {Previously I said that I had three manifestations: Brahman, Paramātmā and Bhagavān. Taking shelter of** ***buddhi-yoga,*** **having fixed the heart in my form as Paramātmā, giving all** ***karmas*** **to me, be devoted to me.}**

**57. \[Giving up thoughts of being the doer, offering the results of all** ***karmas*** **to me, taking shelter of** ***buddhi-yoga*** **with pure** ***bhakti,*** **become fully devoted to me by practicing** ***karmas*** **aimed at** ***bhakti*****.\]**

### Text 58

*mac-cittaḥ sarva-durgāṇi mat-prasādāt tariṣyasi \|*  
*atha cet tvam ahaṅkārān na śroṣyasi vinaṅkṣyasi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**58. Fixing your mind on me in this way, you will cross over all obstacles while living your life. If, on the other hand, you do not do this, and consider yourself the doer by** ***ahaṅkāra*****, thinking you are the body, {falling from the eternal** ***svarūpa*****,}/\[not taking shelter of my instructions,\] you will meet destruction in the form of entering** ***saṁsāra*****.**

### Text 59

*yad ahaṅkāram āśritya na yotsya iti manyase \|*  
*mithyaiṣa vyavasāyas te prakṛtis tvāṁ niyokṣyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**59*****.*** **If, taking shelter of false ego, you think, “I will not fight,” it will be a false promise because your** ***kṣatriya*** **nature will impel you to fight.**

### Text 60

*svabhāva-jena kaunteya nibaddhaḥ svena karmaṇā \|*  
*kartuṁ necchasi yan mohāt kariṣyasy avaśo ’pi tat \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**60. You do not want to fight due to bewilderment but by your actions arising from your nature you will be engaged in that action, beyond your control.**

### Text 61

*īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ hṛd-deśe ’rjuna tiṣṭhati \|*  
*bhrāmayan sarva-bhūtāni yantrārūḍhāni māyayā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**61. O Arjuna, I in the form of Paramātmā am situated in the heart of all** ***jīvas*****. Paramātmā is the controller and lord of all** ***jīvas*****. The Lord gives results according to the actions of the** ***jīvas*****. As an object mounted on a machine are made to move about, all the** ***jīvas*** **are made to wander in the universe due to the Lord’s nature of controlling everything. Your tendency according to previous** ***karmas*** **acts naturally by the inspiration of the Lord.**

### Text 62

*tam eva śaraṇaṁ gaccha sarva-bhāvena bhārata \|*  
*tat-prasādāt parāṁ śāntiṁ sthānaṁ prāpsyasi śāśvatam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**62. O descendent of Bharata! Take refuge in the Lord. By his mercy, you will attain supreme peace and the eternal spiritual abode.**

### Text 63

*iti te jñānam ākhyātaṁ guhyād guhyataraṁ mayā \|*  
*vimṛśyaitad aśeṣeṇa yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**63. Previously I explained to you knowledge of Brahman. That was secret knowledge. Now I spoke to you knowledge of Paramātmā. That is more secret knowledge. Considering thoroughly, do what you desire to do.**

**The conclusion is that if you desire Brahman under the shelter of** ***jñāna*** **through** ***niṣkāma-karma-yoga*** **and then desire to attain** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **to me*****,*** **fight as** ***niṣkāma-karma*****.**

**And if you surrender to Paramātmā, then fight while offering** ***karmas*** **to the Lord, with the tendency that arose from your** ***kṣatriya*** **nature inspired by the Lord. Then,** ***īśvara,*** **the form of my** ***avatāra*** **will gradually bestow** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **to you. To conclude, for you, fighting is better.**

### Text 64

*sarva-guhyatamaṁ bhūyaḥ śṛṇu me paramaṁ vacaḥ \|*  
*iṣṭo ’si me dṛḍham iti tato vakṣyāmi te hitam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**64*****.*** **I spoke secret knowledge of Brahman and more secret knowledge of Paramātmā. Now, I will teach the most secret knowledge of Bhagavān. Please listen. This is the best of what I have taught in the** ***Gītā*****. You are very dear to me. Thus, I speak for your benefit.**

### Text 65

*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru \|*  
*mām evaiṣyasi satyaṁ te pratijāne priyo ’si me \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**65. Becoming a devotee, offer you mind to me. Do not mediate as a** ***karma-yogī, jñāna-yogī*** **or** ***dhyana-yogī*** **does. In all actions worship my form as Bhagavān. I promise that you will attain eternal service to my** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form. I teach this** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **to you since you are dearest to me.**

### Text 66

*sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja \|*  
*ahaṁ tvāṁ sarva-pāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**66. In teaching about attainment of Brahma-jñāna and** ***īśvara*** **(Paramātmā)-*****jñāna*** **I spoke of many** ***dharmas, varṇāśrama, sannyāsa, vairāgya, sama*** **and** ***dama, dhyāna-yoga,*** **and being controlled by the ruling power of the Lord. Giving up all those** ***dharmas,*** **just accept surrender to me as Bhagavān. Then I will deliver you from all types of sin, and all sins incurred by giving up the previously mentioned** ***dharmas*****. Do not lament that you have not performed your** ***karmas*****. When the** ***jīva*** **performs** ***nirguṇa-bhakti*** **to me, his pure nature attains a sound condition naturally.**

**Performance of** ***dharma,*** **proper conduct, atonements, practice of** ***jñāna, yoga*** **and** ***dhyāna*** **are not necessary at all. In a bound condition, one should perform all** ***karmas*** **related to body, mind and soul. But while giving up being fixed in Brahman or Paramātmā through all these** ***karmas,*** **just surrender to Bhagavān being attracted to the Lord’s beauty and sweetness.**

**Комментарий:**

The purport is this. One should perform all the actions that the embodied *jīva* performs while maintaining his life to a higher attachment of three types mentioned, or from the lowest attachment of happiness of the senses. From the lowest attachment arises *akarma* and *vikarma,* which produce obstacles. The three types of superior attraction are attraction to Brahman, *īśvara* (Paramātmā) and Bhagavān.

All *karmas* such as *varṇāśrama* and *vairāgya,* taking support of respective attachments, produce individual states. When they become dependent on attachment to Brahman, *karmas* manifest as *jñāna*. When they become dependent on Paramātmā, offering *karmas* to the Lord manifests as *dhyāna-yoga*. When they become dependent on Bhagavān, they mature into faith and pure *bhakti.* Thus, this *bhakti* is the most secret *tattva* and *prema* alone is the final goal of the *jīva*. This is the main purport of *Gītā*. Though the lives of the *karmī, jñānī, yogī* and devotee are of one type, because of different attachments, they become very different.

### Text 67

*idaṁ te nātapaskāya nābhaktāya kadācana \|*  
*na cāśuśrūṣave vācyaṁ na ca māṁ yo ’bhyasūyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**67. This is not to be spoken to a person not having control of his senses, to one who does not have true devotion, to one who does not render humble service, or to one who envies the** ***sac-cid-ānanda*** **form of Bhagavān. By this, the qualification for the** ***Gītā*** **is defined.**

*ya idaṁ paramaṁ guhyaṁ mad-bhakteṣv abhidhāsyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**68. One who teaches this supreme secret to my devotees attains my** ***nirguṇa-bhakt*****i and finally attains me without doubt.**

*na ca tasmān manuṣyeṣu kaścin me priya-kṛttamaḥ \|*  
*bhavitā na ca me tasmād anyaḥ priyataro bhuvi \|\|*

**69. Among men there is no one who performs activities so dear to me. There is not and will never be one dearer to me than he.**

*adhyeṣyate ca ya imaṁ dharmyaṁ saṁvādam āvayoḥ \|*  
*jñāna-yajñena tenāham iṣṭaḥ syām iti me matiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**70. He who studies my conversation of the highest** ***dharma*** **will have worshipped me through the sacrifice of knowledge (*****jñāna-yajña*****).**

### Text 71

*śraddhāvān anasūyaś ca śṛṇuyād api yo naraḥ \|*  
*so ’pi muktaḥ śubhāl lokān prāpnuyāt puṇya-karmaṇām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

*kaccid etac chrutaṁ pārtha tvayaikāgreṇa cetasā \|*  
*kaccid ajñāna-sammohaḥ praṇaṣṭas te dhanañjaya \|\|*

**72. O conqueror of wealth! Have you heard this with close attention? Has your bewilderment born of ignorance been destroyed?**

*arjuna uvāca*  
*naṣṭo mohaḥ smṛtir labdhā tvat-prasādān mayācyuta \|*  
*sthito ’smi gata-sandehaḥ kariṣye vacanaṁ tava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**73. Arjuna said: My delusion has been destroyed, and I again remember that the** ***jīva*** **is the eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa by your mercy, O Acyuta. I am free of doubts. Situated in surrender to you, which is the chief** ***dharma*** **of the** ***jīva,*** **I will follow your instructions.**

*sañjaya uvāca*

**Перевод:**

*rājan saṁsmṛtya saṁsmṛtya saṁvādam imam adbhutam \|*

**Перевод:**

### Text 78

*yatra yogeśvaraḥ kṛṣṇo yatra pārtho dhanur-dharaḥ \|*  
*tatra śrīr vijayo bhūtir dhruvā nītir matir mama \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**78. Where there is Kṛṣṇa, the master of yoga, and where there is Arjuna, the holder of the bow, there will be prosperity, victory, wealth, and righteousness. That is my statement of conviction.**

**Комментарий:**

In the eighteenth chapter the purport of the Gītā is summarized.

*Karma-yoga* culminating in *dhyāna-yoga* which produces realization of *ātmā* is one division and pure *bhakti-yoga* arising from faith in the lord is another division. This is the essence of the *Gītā*. Within that, attaining the path of *jñāna* gradually by performing *karmas* with *niṣkāma* intent while taking support of a dharmic life, according to *varṇa* accomplished by one’s nature, is the secret teaching. Performing *jñāna* in the form of realizing ātmā gradually, after combining *dhyāna-yoga* in one’s life is the more secret teaching. Performing *bhakti-yoga* by surrender to Kṛṣṇa is the most secret teaching. This is the purport of the eighteenth chapter.

The purport of the whole *Gītā* is this. The non-dual substance is the only *tattva*. This is known as Bhagavān. All other *tattvas* emanate as *śaktis* from this *Bhagavat-tattva.* By the *cit-śakti* the Lord’s *svarūpa* and the splendor of the spiritual world manifest. By the *jīva-śakti* unlimited *jīvas*, some liberated and some conditioned, manifest. By *māyā-śakti,* material *tattva* of twenty-four elements from *pradhāna* to stalk manifest. By *kāla-śakti,* creation, maintenance, destruction, causation of all material conditions manifest. By the *kriyā-śakti* all types of *karma* manifest. *Īśvara, prakṛti, jīva, kāla* and karma—these five *tattvas* emanate from bhagavat-tattva. The states of Brahman and Paramātmā are included with Bhagavat-tattva. Though these five tattvas are different from the Lord they are also one *tattva*, dependent on Bhagavat-tattva. And though they are all one *tattva*, because of the quality of *viśeṣa*, they are eternally different from him. This is the difference and nondifference *tattva* mentioned in the *Gītā*. It is beyond human logic. Because of this, the previous devotees called this *tattva* taught in *Gītā acintya-bhedābheda-tattva.* The knowledge related to this is called *tattva-jñāna.*

The *jīva* is by nature pure consciousness. It is a special *tattva* like a particle in the ray of the spiritual sun of Kṛṣṇa. The *jīva* by nature has conscious and unconscious aspects: the *jīva* is suitable for both worlds. His first state is at the junction of the spiritual and material worlds. As a conscious entity, the *jīva* is by nature independent. When absorbed in the spiritual world, being favorable to Kṛṣṇa, the *jīva* is able to experience pure bliss with the assistance of the *hlādini-śakti* of the spiritual realm. When the *jīva* is absorbed in the material world situated next to the Lord, the *jīva*, being averse to Kṛṣṇa, by the pull of *māyā-śakti*, falls into material happiness and suffering. Those who are endowed with spiritual *rati* are eternally liberated. Those who are endowed with material *rati* are eternally bound. The number of both types of *jīvas* is unlimited.

The bound *jīva*, having almost lost his nature, going up and down in the material ocean, at some time develops indifference to matter and taking shelter of guru, maturing *dhyāna* by *karma-yoga*, attains attraction for the Lord, which is his very nature. Or sometimes, developing faith in the lord’s topics, taking shelter of a suitable guru, the jīva attains sādhana*, bhāva* and *prema*. There is no means to attain real *ātmā* other than these two methods. Of these two methods, usually one takes shelter of *karma-yoga*, which gives realization of *ātmā* through its culmination in *dhyāna*, since it depends on one’s own actions. Though *bhakti-yoga* arising from faith is better than *karma-yoga* and easier, it does not occur unless good fortune arises in the form of the Lord’s or devotee’s mercy. Thus, the majority of the world is fond of *karma-yoga* which contains within it *jñāna*. Among those people, those that have good fortune develop faith in *bhakti-yoga* and manifest surrender mentioned in the *Gītā’s* last verse. This is the method for all people.

Attaining material enjoyment or *bhukti* in the fourteen planets on the path of *kāmya-karma* is most unfavorable for *jīva*, which has a conscious *svarūpa*. Even at the beginning of the *Gītā*, this *kāmya-karma* and *bhakti* which arises from it are considered most insignificant. Liberation, called *sāyujya* and *nirvāṇa*, in the form of pure *monism*, after becoming free from old age and death, is not the final goal of the *jīva*. This has been mentioned in many places. Penetrating the place of liberation in the form of attainment of an abode of power of four types such as *sālokya*, and *advaita-siddhi* (merging into oneness) and attaining *bhāva* and pure *prema* after entering the Lord’s pastimes with one’s ultimate nature, is the final goal of the jīva. This has been stated in many places at the time of drawing conclusions.

Therefore, Bhagavān in the form of two-armed Śyāmasundara, the *jīva’s* final object of worship, after gathering all the Vedas and Vedānta in the *Gītā* scripture concludes that one should attain prema as the highest goal after performing *bhakti-yoga,* on attaining *sambandha-jñāna*. Take shelter of *bhakti-yoga* with hearing, etc., always, while living a dharmic life according to one’s qualification. Maintain your life taking support of *sva-dharma,* in the form of conduct favorable to *bhakti-yoga.* Gradually, endowed with faith, giving up *svaniṣṭhā*, becoming *pariniṣṭhita* in *bhakti-yoga* by surrender even then, maintain your life by *sva-dharma.* Then, in a short time, I will give you pure *prema*, served by the *nirapekṣa* devotee. In this way having entered the workings of *śuddha-sattva,* having attained my mercy in the form of absence of lamentation, absence of fear and absence of death, you will become absorbed in my eternal *prema*.

## References

He was the care-taker of Lord Caitanya's mother and wife after the Lord took *sannyāsa*. His family descendant was Vipina-vihārī Gosvāmī, the dīkṣā-guru of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura.

*Vidvad-rañjana* has Kṛpa, while *Rasika-rañjana* has Jayadratha in its place.

*Rasika-rañjana* has *uttamā bhakti* and *Vidvad-rañjana* has *śuddha-bhakti* real nature of *ātmā*.

It is not manifested in the spiritual world.

With regards to why Meru is not considered immovable while Himālayas are, it is said that once Meru wobbled when Vāsuki coiled around it and Vāyu tried to loosen its coil.

Munis are different Devarṣis (in text 26) in the sense that Devarṣis would reside in Svaraga, etc.

*Rasika-rañjana* has *vīkṣante* here, but that seems to be a mistaken reading borrowed from the next verse.

A division of time, about 3 hours.
